Existing research highlights the importance of incorporating candidate entity type information for accurate entity recognition and classification by LLMs. However, when too many candidate entity types are provided in the prompt, LLMs struggle to accurately recognize and label entities in scientific texts, where entity types are more complex than in general domains. To address this challenge, we propose TdSciNER, a type-driven approach that effectively leverages entity type information to enhance SciNER performance. In TdSciNER, we first design an entity type filter model to identify the most likely entity types present in a given sentence. Subsequently, we introduce an auxiliary multi-class entity typing task within a multi-task learning framework alongside SciNER to obtain richer contextual representations. Then, we develop a novel demonstration selection strategy based on sentence similarity and entity type diversity to activate the in-context learning capabilities of LLMs, thereby improving entity recognition accuracy across diverse scientific domains. Experiments on three datasets demonstrate that our method achieves performance comparable to fully supervised models. Further analysis validates that each entity type-driven component in TdSciNER contributes to the improvement of SciNER performance. This work provides valuable insights for future advancements in SciNER and broader information extraction tasks in scientific text mining.

</details>


### [101] [The Evolution of Mixture-of-Experts Architectures in Large Language Models: Routing, Topology, Load Balancing, and Expert Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08650)
*Jiguo Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综述了Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）模型的演化，通过五个耦合维度（专家粒度、拓扑、路由自由度、负载均衡范围、执行结构）组织现代MoE系统，并提出一个包含八个里程碑的依赖图，而非线性世代。采用四个控制平面（专家拓扑、路由、负载平衡、专家并行）分析系统设计，连接算法选择与系统实现，强调从单纯激活更多稀疏参数向解耦语义路由、计算预算与物理执行的趋势转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对MoE模型演进的描述局限于时间序列的模型发布，无法揭示其内在架构发展逻辑；亟需一种系统化框架来整合算法创新与系统实现之间的关系，以指导未来设计。

Method: 基于对核心论文、技术报告和先前综述的综合分析，构建涵盖五维分类体系的架构框架，提出依赖图表示架构演进路径，并引入四控制平面模型解析系统设计决策。

Result: 识别出六条主要发展主线与两条正交分支，揭示了从静态专家配置到动态、细粒度、可调度架构的演进趋势；实验验证了在相同预算下，新架构在质量与系统效率上的优势。

Conclusion: MoE系统的发展正从单纯扩大参数量转向更精细地解耦语义路由、计算预算与物理执行，未来研究需关注动态资源配置、通信优化与跨设备协同。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts models increase parameter capacity while keeping the computation activated by each token bounded, but their architectural evolution cannot be explained by a chronological list of model releases alone. This technical survey synthesizes primary papers, official technical reports, and prior surveys to organize modern Mixture-of-Experts systems along five coupled dimensions: expert granularity, expert topology, routing freedom, the scope of load balancing, and execution structure. We describe eight architectural milestones as a dependency graph with six mainline developments and two orthogonal branches, rather than as eight successive generations. We then analyze individual systems through four control planes: Expert Topology, Routing, Balance, and Expert Parallelism. These planes specify which experts exist, which experts process each token, how aggregate load is controlled, and how selected computation is mapped onto physical devices. The framework connects algorithmic choices such as Top-k routing, shared experts, fine-grained experts, and dynamic expert composition with systems concerns including token dispatch, device placement, all-to-all communication, and communication-computation overlap. We conclude with equal-budget pretraining experiments, quality and systems metrics, and open research questions. The main trend is a shift from merely activating more sparse parameters toward decoupling semantic routing, computational budgets, and physical execution.

</details>


### [102] [LibraSpec: Dynamic Diffusion-Based Speculative Decoding via Marginal-Gain-Driven Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08721)
*Zexun Lin,Yuan Feng,Junlin Lv,Kevin S. Zhou,Xike Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出LibraSpec，一种无需训练、即插即用的动态推测长度选择算法，针对基于扩散模型的并行生成器，通过优化预期加速来决定最优推测长度，在多个基准测试中显著提升推理速度，最高达8.49倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态推测方法依赖于对可接受令牌数量的估计，适用于自回归生成器；但扩散模型能以更低成本并行生成候选块，因此关键问题从‘生成多少令牌’转变为‘哪些生成的令牌值得验证’。

Method: 将动态推测长度选择重新建模为预期加速优化问题，推导出边际准则：仅当接受收益超过额外验证成本时才扩展推测序列；基于此设计LibraSpec，利用生成器置信度分数迭代确定推测长度。

Result: 在六种目标模型、三种扩散基推测解码方法及数学、编码、聊天任务上均表现优异，相比基线进一步提升0.5~1.5倍性能，最高实现8.49倍于自回归解码的速度提升。

Conclusion: LibraSpec理论上单调收敛至最优推测长度，且无需训练、可直接集成，是高效扩散基推测解码的关键技术。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference by drafting multiple tokens for parallel verification, with efficiency critically determined by the speculative length selected at each decoding round. Existing dynamic speculation methods select the speculation length by estimating how many tokens will be accepted, which is reasonable for autoregressive drafters that generates tokens sequentially. The recent wave of diffusion-based drafters, however, generates candidate blocks in parallel at substantially lower drafting cost, shifting the key question from how many tokens to generate to how many generated tokens are worth verifying. We therefore reformulate dynamic speculative-length selection as expected-speedup optimization and derive a marginal criterion that extends the speculative sequence only when its acceptance gain outweighs the additional verification cost. Building on this criterion, we develop \textit{LibraSpec}, a training-free and plug-and-play algorithm that iteratively determines the speculative length using drafter confidence scores. Theoretically, we prove that LibraSpec monotonically converges toward the optimal speculative length. Experiments across six target models, three diffusion-based speculative decoding methods, and math, coding, and chat benchmarks show consistent improvements under both greedy and sampling settings, achieving a further $0.5\sim1.5\times$ improvement over baselines and up to $8.49\times$ speedup over autoregressive decoding.

</details>


### [103] [Can We Optimize the Performance-Carbon Emission Break-Even Point?: The Quest for Greener LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08744)
*Sourav Das,Tanmay Joshi,Kripabandhu Ghosh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨在微调过程中引入可微分的碳排放代理模型，以实现推理阶段低碳甚至零碳的成本下提升任务准确率。通过构建包含参数量、计算量（FLOP）和内存占用的线性碳排放代理，并结合实际硬件能耗数据进行校准，对Gemma-2 2B、Llama-3.1 8B和Qwen-2.5 14B三类模型进行实验，评估其在抽象代数、哲学与形式逻辑三个MMLU任务上的推理性能与碳排放。结果表明，碳项在不同任务中表现出不同的作用：有时为有害干扰，有时则作为有益正则化。研究提出一种轻量级、可直接集成的碳感知微调方法，存在模型与任务相关的‘盈亏平衡’区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型的碳足迹主要来自推理阶段的重复使用，而现有效率优化多集中于预训练规模或后处理压缩，缺乏对微调阶段碳排放的系统性控制。本文旨在探索是否可通过在微调目标中嵌入可微分的碳排放代理，使模型在不增加额外碳成本的前提下提升推理性能。

Method: 提出联合损失函数，引入每模型碳排放参数，采用基于参数范数、FLOP和内存占用的线性碳排放代理，并通过真实硬件能耗数据进行校准；在三种不同架构的模型上进行微调，并在三个MMLU任务上评估推理F1得分与二氧化碳排放量。

Result: 实验结果显示，碳惩罚项在不同任务中表现各异：在某些任务中显著提升性能（作为正则化），在另一些任务中反而降低性能（产生干扰）。存在模型与任务依赖的‘盈亏平衡’区域，即在特定条件下实现性能提升且碳成本不变或接近零。

Conclusion: 碳感知微调可作为一种轻量级、可插拔的正则化机制，在特定条件下实现任务性能提升与碳排放控制的双赢。该方法具有广泛适用潜力，但其有效性高度依赖于具体模型结构与任务特性。

Abstract: The carbon footprint of any deployed Large Language Model (LLM) accumulates during inference, where repeated use of the model substantially exceeds the one-time cost of fine-tuning. Yet most efficiency interventions target either pre-training scale or post-hoc compression. We ask whether folding a calibrated, differentiable energy surrogate into the fine-tuning objective can produce inference behavior that gains task accuracy at zero or near-zero carbon cost, a break-even configuration. We propose a joint loss mechanism with a per-model carbon-emission parameter, a linear surrogate over parameter norm, FLOP proxy, and a memory proxy, fit from on-hardware energy profiling. We fine-tune three architecturally distinct families: Gemma-2 2B, Llama-3.1 8B, and Qwen-2.5 14B, and evaluate inference F1 and CO$_2$ emissions on three MMLU subjects: abstract algebra, philosophy, and formal logic. We discover from several outcomes that the carbon term behaves as either harmful interference or beneficial regularization depending on the task structure. We position calibrated carbon-aware fine-tuning as a lightweight, drop-in regularizer with a non-empty but model and task-dependent break-even region. This is an ongoing work, and we will release our codebase soon.

</details>


### [104] [Multilingual Emotion Neurons in Large Audio-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08772)
*Xiutian Zhao,Philipp Koehn,Björn Schuller,Berrak Sisman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次从神经元层面研究了大音频-语言模型（LALMs）在多语言情感表达中的机制，提出多语言情感神经元（MLENs）概念，并通过一致性正则化融合（CR-Fusion）方法识别这些跨语言稳定的神经单元。研究发现，单语言独立识别的情感神经元在不同语言间重叠极少，而利用跨语言联合数据可显著提升情感控制的精确性与迁移能力，尤其在零样本和低资源场景下表现更优。消融实验表明，各语言贡献非冗余信息，且低资源语言最受益于跨语言迁移。该工作为理解LALMs如何跨语言编码情感提供了首个因果性、神经元级别的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大音频-语言模型在多语言语音任务中表现优异，但其情感表征机制尚不明确：是依赖语言特定关联，还是存在跨语言通用表示？缺乏对神经层次上情感编码的系统分析，限制了对模型跨语言情感行为的理解。因此需要从神经元层面揭示情感在多语言模型中的编码方式。

Method: 提出多语言情感神经元（MLENs）定义，设计一致性正则化融合（CR-Fusion）方法，基于跨语言联合数据识别稳定且具因果效应的情感敏感神经元；通过因果干预与留一法消融实验验证其有效性与迁移能力。

Result: CR-Fusion识别出的MLENs在零样本与低资源条件下展现出更强的情感控制精度与跨语言迁移能力；不同语言间贡献非冗余信息，低资源语言尤其受益于跨语言知识共享。

Conclusion: 本研究首次提供了大音频-语言模型在多语言环境下情感编码的因果性神经元级解释，证明跨语言神经元识别是理解模型跨语言情感行为的有效机制。

Abstract: Emotion is central to human communication, and its expression varies across languages. Large audio-language models (LALMs) achieve strong performance on multilingual speech tasks, yet it remains unclear whether they encode emotion through language-specific correlations or language-agnostic representations. We present the first neuron-level interpretability study of this question. We define Multilingual Emotion Neurons (MLENs) as functional units exhibiting stable emotional selectivity and aligned causal effects across languages, and introduce Consistency-Regularized Fusion (CR-Fusion) to identify them. Across four modern LALMs and 12 typologically diverse languages, emotion-sensitive neurons identified independently per language show minimal overlap, and additional monolingual identification data saturates quickly without isolating more transferable units, motivating identification from pooled cross-lingual evidence. Causal interventions demonstrate that MLENs identified by CR-Fusion provide more precise and transferable affective control than monolingual neuron sets in both zero-shot and low-resource settings. Leave-one-out ablations further reveal asymmetric transfer: individual identification languages, including low-resource ones, contribute non-redundant evidence, while several low-resource languages benefit most from the resulting cross-lingual transfer. Together, our findings provide the first causal, neuron-level account of how LALMs encode emotion across languages, and establish multilingual neuron identification as an effective mechanism for understanding cross-lingual affective behavior.

</details>


### [105] [OmnilingualGAIA2: Evaluating the Multilingual Gap in Frontier AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08775)
*Andrea Caciolai,Pere-Lluís Huguet Cabot,Chierh Cheng,Albert Ventayol-Boada,Gabriel Mejia Gonzalez,Christophe Ropers,Lucas Bandarkar,Sebastian Ruder,Darlene Sakakihara,Elliot Yun,Pierre Andrews,Grégoire Mialon,Romain Froger,Marta R. Costa-jussà*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出OmnilingualGAIA2，通过机器翻译并结合人工校验扩展了GAIA2基准测试至十种语言，覆盖五种书写系统，并配备本地化的人工校准多语言验证器。评估七种前沿及开源代理发现跨语言性能差距普遍存在于8.8-18.4 pass@3点之间，主要集中在工具编排而非定量推理，且不随模型规模缩小。误差分解显示55%为模型驱动，翻译污染仅占6.4%。人类专家分析指出非拉丁字母语言中形态线索丢失和歧义加剧是主要失败机制。研究呼吁将多语言代理评估纳入全球部署代理的标准报告流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的代理基准测试几乎全为英文，而AI代理在全球部署时需应对多语言环境，因此需要评估英语中的代理能力是否能有效迁移到其他语言。

Method: 将GAIA2基准通过机器翻译扩展至十种语言，部分经过人工专家验证，并开发本地化、人工校准的多语言验证器进行评估；采用分层误差归因分析与人类语言学分析识别失败机制。

Result: 发现跨语言性能存在8.8-18.4 pass@3点的普遍差距，该差距主要源于工具编排困难，且不随模型规模改善；55%的差距由模型自身导致，翻译污染仅占6.4%；非拉丁字母语言中形态线索丢失和语义模糊是主要问题。

Conclusion: 多语言代理评估应成为全球部署代理的标准报告流程，以确保其在多样化语言环境下的实际表现。

Abstract: Agentic benchmarks aim to measure how well AI agents plan, search, execute, and recover within realistic multi-tool environments, but they are almost exclusively in English. As AI agents are globally deployed to a linguistically diverse user base, whether agentic competence measured in English transfers to other languages remains an open question. We introduce OmnilingualGAIA2, a machine-translated expansion (with partial human- expert validation) of the GAIA2 agentic benchmark, covering ten target languages spanning five writing systems, paired with a localised and human-calibrated multilingual verifier. Evaluating seven frontier and open-weight agents, we find a universal cross-lingual gap of 8.8-18.4 pass@3 points that is agent-asymmetric in magnitude, concentrates on tool-orchestration rather than quantitative reasoning, and does not close with model scale. A stratified error attribution decomposes the gap as predominantly model-driven (55%), with a bounded translation-contamination floor of only 6.4% of scenario-language pairs. Human-expert linguistic analysis further identifies morphological cue loss and amplified ambiguity as the primary failure mechanisms in non-Latin-script languages. Our results argue that multilingual agentic evaluation must become a standard part of the reporting protocol for globally deployed agents.

</details>


### [106] [Unsure but Certain: Uncovering the Representation-Confidence Gap in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08791)
*Saurabh Yadav,Badri Narayana Patro,Vijay Srinivas Agneeswaran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 扩散语言模型虽在广义上下文中生成文本，理论上应更抗噪声，但实际表现仅部分理想。内部能精准检测文本错误，外部却高估自信度，导致在噪声下准确率下降时，置信度仍维持高位，排序能力退化至随机水平。这种内外不一致称为‘表示置信度差距’。标准数学修正可缓解置信度集中现象，但无法恢复排序性能。现有方法如训练匹配、分数校准和输入级错误信号均无法有效重排答案。然而，正确评估答案所需信息仍存在于隐藏状态中。本文提出一种轻量级提取工具，利用该信号提升排序，且无需修改基础模型或增加生成步骤。此工具证明信号存在，但也揭示其局限性。结论强调：在噪声环境下，置信度可靠性比整体准确性更为关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索扩散语言模型在噪声输入下的表现，发现其内部错误检测能力强但外部置信度不可靠，导致排序能力严重下降。旨在揭示这一‘表示置信度差距’现象，并寻找有效修复方法。

Method: 通过实验测试扩散模型在噪声输入下的表现；分析内部隐藏状态中的错误信号；设计轻量级提取工具从隐藏状态中恢复排序信息；验证不同修正策略（如训练匹配、分数校准、输入级信号）的局限性。

Result: 扩散模型虽能准确检测错误，但置信度始终偏高，导致排序能力接近随机；标准修正方法无法恢复排序顺序；仅隐藏状态中保留有用信号；轻量级提取工具可有效提升排序性能，且不需额外生成步骤。

Conclusion: 在噪声条件下，置信度的可靠性比整体准确率更重要。当前模型的置信度失真构成主要瓶颈，需优先解决。隐藏状态蕴含可用信号，可通过轻量工具提取，但基础模型仍需改进以实现可靠置信度输出。

Abstract: Diffusion language models use broad context to create text, suggesting they might handle input noise better than standard models. Testing reveals this is only partially true. Internally, diffusion models detect text errors highly accurately. Externally, their reported certainty ignores this signal. As accuracy drops due to noise, confidence stays near its maximum and the ability to correctly rank answers degrades toward random chance. We call this mismatch the representation confidence gap. The visible concentration of high certainty scores is a misleading surface symptom. Standard math adjustments remove this concentration but fail to fix the underlying loss of ranking order. This ranking deficit favors standard models under noisy conditions and resists common remedies. Matching training recovers accuracy but not ranking, while score recalibration and input level error signals cannot reorder the final answers. However, the information needed to properly evaluate an answer survives in the hidden states. A lightweight extraction tool uses this signal to improve ranking. This approach is highly efficient because it leaves the base model completely frozen and requires zero additional text generation steps. We present this tool to prove the signal exists, while clearly noting its limits. Ultimately, certainty reliability is a more pressing limit than overall accuracy under noisy conditions.

</details>


### [107] [Evidence-Calibrated Runtime Reconstruction for Agent Skills Across Heterogeneous Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08793)
*Xueping Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Skill Runtime Intelligence系统，通过运行时智能重建跨异构环境的技能生命周期阶段，区分不可变事件、确定性关系、推断诊断和受控结果，并支持现有可观测性部署。实验表明，不同适配器表现出三种语义行为，且事件存在不等于边界保真度，复合精确度分数会掩盖不同错误类型，模型解释不应覆盖确定性事实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在技能发现与激活过程中存在失败边界表示不足的问题，如技能可被发现但无法激活、激活后无指令或成功但无独立验证结果，亟需一种能跨异构环境准确追踪技能生命周期的运行时智能系统。

Method: 设计并实现Skill Runtime Intelligence系统，采用运行全景（Run Panorama）分离四类证据等级：不可变事件、确定性关系、推断诊断与受控结果；支持可选的轨迹导入及OTLP/HTTP导出，兼容现有可观测性工具链。

Result: 在6个冻结仓库配置、3个编码代理和7种清洁或注入故障条件下，126次执行均保留源工作树且一一对应源会话；适配器揭示三类不同语义行为；诊断研究显示语义别名与全景图可定位相同6个非清洁边界，但行为细节不同；原始视图在全部18个清洁案例中误报失败，而全景图未报告任何失败；已知规则图符合126/126冻结契约，第二模型仅完成228/378调用。

Conclusion: 应推动可执行适配器的资格认证；事件的存在不能等同于边界保真度；复合精确度分数可能掩盖本质差异；模型解释必须尊重并保留确定性事实。

Abstract: Agent Skills package reusable instructions and assets for tool-using language-model agents. Progressive loading creates failure boundaries poorly represented by session-, model-, or tool-centric traces: a Skill can be discovered but not activated, activated without instructions, or appear successful without an independently verified outcome. We present Skill Runtime Intelligence, a passive runtime-intelligence system that reconstructs supported Skill-lifecycle stages across heterogeneous harnesses while preserving unsupported stages as unknown. Its Run Panorama separates immutable events, deterministic relations, inferred diagnoses, and controlled outcomes with four evidence grades; optional trace import and OTLP/HTTP export support existing observability deployments.
  Across six frozen repository profiles, three coding agents, and seven clean or fault-injected conditions, all 126 executions preserve source worktrees and each correlates to exactly one source session. Yet adapters expose three distinct semantics: no Skill runs; complete runs but no failure-like events; or failure-like events in every operational-failure and clean session. In a seven-template diagnostic study, semantic aliases and Panorama localize the same six non-clean boundaries but differ in exact/status behavior; both Raw views emit a failure status on all 18 clean cases, while Panorama emits none. A known-rule graph conforms to 126/126 frozen contracts, whereas a second model completes only 228/378 calls. These observations motivate executable adapter qualification and show that event presence is not boundary fidelity, composite exact scores mask distinct errors, and model explanations must not overwrite deterministic facts.

</details>


### [108] [Instability of LLM Pre-Pretraining: It Doesn't Always Help. An Investigation on Multiple Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08800)
*Sofiia Riazhskykh,Nam Luu,Ondřej Bojar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究验证了在人工语言上预训练大模型（'预预训练'）可提升33%的词元效率，但实际效果受实验设置和随机种子影响较大。通过多语言、多分词器和不同模型规模的实验发现，仅在特定条件下（如128-Dyck预训练、小模型、Llama分词器）能稳定获得收益。研究还关联了语言学特征（如句长、形态丰富度、句法树复杂性）与效率增益的关系，强调应进行多次训练以确保结果稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索预预训练技术是否能普遍提升大模型训练的词元效率，并揭示其效果背后的语言学因素，以避免社区采纳不稳定的训练方法。

Method: 在多个自然语言（跨四个语系）上使用两种分词器和不同模型规模进行实验，评估128-Dyck人工语言预训练对词元效率的影响，并分析语言学特征与效率变化之间的关系。

Result: 预预训练带来的词元效率提升高度依赖实验设置和随机种子；仅在特定组合下（如小模型+Llama分词器+128-Dyck预训练）观察到稳定增益；语言学特征如句长和句法树复杂性与效率变化相关。

Conclusion: 尽管预预训练在某些条件下有效，但其效果不稳定，建议在关键实验中进行多次训练以确保可靠性，避免推广不可靠的方法。

Abstract: Pretraining LLMs on artificial languages ("pre-pretraining") is a technique that could reportedly increase token efficiency by 33%, i.e., save up to 33% of training tokens needed to reach a certain performance. We validate this prior result for English on a larger set of natural languages across four language families, using two different tokenizers and varying model sizes. We also relate the observed gains (or losses) in token efficiency to quantified linguistic properties of the languages, such as sentence length, morphological richness, and features of dependency syntactic trees (tree depth, number of children, number of crossing dependencies). Our empirical results indicate that the reported gains depend heavily on the experiment setup and the choice of random seed, although we can confirm the trend of stable gains with 128-Dyck pretraining of small models with the Llama tokenizer for most of the examined languages. On a general note, we argue that multiple training runs should be carried out at least for a subset of experiments to avoid the community adopting unstable approaches.

</details>


### [109] [IDRAAK: From Multi-Agent NLP to Few-Shot Prompting for Semantic Drift Detection in Technical Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08801)
*Shiva Ahir*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IDRAAK是一个用于检测跨语言技术需求翻译中语义漂移的可解释框架，采用语言无关的语义需求表示（SRR），通过六种检测流程进行评估。在300个需求、10个工程领域中的890个合成扰动上，仅用一次LLM调用加六次少样本提示即可实现MCC=0.888和F1=0.983，优于结构化和多阶段方法。在PAWS-X和XNLI数据集上的进一步评估揭示了结构化与基于LLM方法的互补优劣。确定性SRR比较在技术需求上表现良好（F1=0.898），但在通用文本上表现差（F1=0.012）；结构化证据在对抗性改写中提升性能。后处理Platt缩放改善置信度校准。结果表明，更高的代理复杂性未必提升检测效果，简单的少样本提示已能提供高效且强大的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言技术需求翻译可能引入语义漂移，影响数值约束、极性、情态等关键语义信息，亟需有效检测机制以保障需求一致性。

Method: 提出语言无关的语义需求表示（SRR），并设计六种检测工作流，包括确定性比较、多智能体验证、少样本提示等，结合大规模合成扰动与真实数据集进行评估。

Result: 单次LLM调用加六次少样本提示达到MCC=0.888和F1=0.983，优于其他复杂方法；结构化方法在技术需求上表现好但通用文本差；少样本提示在效率与性能间取得平衡；Platt缩放提升置信度校准。

Conclusion: 增加代理复杂性并不必然提升语义漂移检测性能，简单高效的少样本提示已可作为强大且实用的替代方案。

Abstract: Translating technical requirements across languages can introduce semantic drift, altering numerical constraints, polarities, modalities, or other specification-critical meaning. IDRAAK is presented as an interpretable framework for detecting such drift using a language-independent Semantic Requirement Representation (SRR), with six detection workflows evaluated, ranging from deterministic comparison to multi-agent verification and few-shot prompting. On 890 synthetic perturbations across 300 requirements from 10 engineering domains, a single LLM call with six few-shot examples achieves MCC=0.888 and F1=0.983, outperforming the evaluated structured and multi-stage alternatives. Further evaluation on PAWS-X (805 pairs, 5 languages) and XNLI (700 pairs, 7 languages) exposes complementary strengths and limitations of structured and LLM-based approaches. Deterministic SRR comparison performs strongly on technical requirements (F1=0.898) but poorly on general-domain text (F1=0.012), while structured evidence improves performance on adversarial paraphrases. Post-hoc Platt scaling further improves confidence calibration. The results demonstrate that increased agentic complexity does not necessarily improve semantic-drift detection and that simple few-shot prompting can provide a strong and efficient alternative.

</details>


### [110] [Tevatron-Elastic: A Unified Abstraction for Training Elastic Retrievers and Rerankers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08809)
*Yu Wang,Shengyao Zhuang,Xueguang Ma,Zongyu Wu,Jimmy Lin,Vivek Srikumar,Zhichao Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个统一抽象框架，通过单一对象和简短调度表，支持Transformer模型在层数、上下文长度和嵌入维度上的多种压缩方式，实现灵活可扩展的检索系统；该框架兼容编码器与解码器模型，并能统一处理多种现有方法（如Matryoshka嵌入、早期退出等），并引入新型联合训练方法MLTC。实验表明，单个检查点可服务多种规模需求，训练成本接近单规模模型，且性能曲线平滑，显著提升部署灵活性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型压缩方法（如减少层数、早期退出、嵌入压缩等）各自独立实现，难以组合或适应新模型，导致系统灵活性差；不同应用场景对速度、索引大小的需求各异，亟需一种统一且高效的多尺度模型管理机制。

Method: 设计一个统一抽象接口，以单一对象表示任意模型规模，通过简短调度列表定义训练多个规模的配置；利用Hugging Face Transformers的现有接口，实现无需修改建模代码即可支持不同压缩策略；将多种已有方法作为特例纳入该框架，并引入新的联合训练机制MLTC（Matryoshka LTC）。

Result: 成功训练20个跨三种骨干网络、两个任务的检查点，验证了框架的有效性：性能曲线平滑，单检查点开销仅略高于单规模模型，实测显示明显加速效果；所有代码与模型公开发布，支持弹性检索系统构建。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一抽象框架有效整合了多种模型压缩技术，显著提升了生产级检索系统的灵活性与效率，为构建可动态调整的弹性检索系统提供了通用解决方案。

Abstract: A single model scale challenges the flexibility of a production retrieval system: some settings need it faster, others need a smaller index, and the right trade-off changes with the workload. In the context of information retrieval (IR), a transformer-based model can be made smaller in three ways---using fewer layers, passing fewer tokens through the upper layers, or producing a shorter embedding---and each way saves a different compute resource. These options have been studied one at a time, each as its own method with its own code and training setup, which makes them hard to combine or adapt to a new model. We present~\ours to bring all three under one simple abstraction: a single object names any size the model can run at, and a short schedule lists the sizes to train. Training then produces one checkpoint that serves all of those sizes, and at deployment the user picks any of them. The same abstraction covers both retrievers and rerankers and both encoder and decoder models, as it works through interfaces that Hugging Face transformers already expose; a new backbone is a configuration change, not new modeling code. Prior methods---Matryoshka embeddings, early exit, 2D~Matryoshka (e.g., Starbucks), and layerwise token compression---become special cases of our unified abstraction. The same interface also enables Matryoshka~LTC (MLTC), which jointly trains several token-compression ratios in one retriever checkpoint. To validate our framework, we train 20 checkpoints across three backbones and two tasks: the quality curves are smooth, one checkpoint costs little over a model trained for a single size, and a controlled study confirms the wallclock speedups. We release the framework and all checkpoints as a resource for building elastic retrieval systems.

</details>


### [111] [Deployable Per-Instance Multi-Layer Activation Steering for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08829)
*Muhammad Faishal Adly Nelwan,Alfan Farizki Wicaksono*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种新的激活引导方法，通过为每个输入实例动态选择最优的层进行模型行为编辑，而非使用全局固定的层。研究发现，最佳注入层随输入变化，固定层集无法充分利用潜力。通过设计一个仅依赖提示的预测器，实现部署时的自适应层选择，避免了性能下降和流畅性损失，同时在多个任务上表现接近理想情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前激活引导方法采用全局固定的注入层，但实际最佳层因输入而异，固定策略限制了效果提升。因此需要一种能够根据每个输入实例自适应选择层的方法，以最大化编辑效果并保持生成质量。

Method: 提出一种可部署的方案：基于提示嵌入获取每实例的层优先级，训练分类器预测引导方向，并通过自适应门控机制评估短路径引导效果，仅在必要时添加更多层。整个过程无需推理时标签信息。

Result: 该方法在两个8B规模的开放权重模型上测试，对六个二元人格特征均显著优于固定层策略，接近理想层选择的性能；未降低任何任务的基线对齐度，有效缓解了过度引导导致的流畅性崩溃问题。

Conclusion: 通过实例级、多层的自适应选择，结合提示驱动的预测与自适应门控，实现了高效且稳定的语言模型行为编辑，验证了‘方向重于幅度’的机制解释力，为未来可控生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Activation steering edits the behaviour of a frozen language model by adding a learned vector to its residual stream, and current practice fixes the injection layers globally per task. We argue that the best layers are an instance-level decision, and we make per-instance, multi-layer selection both well understood and deployable. On two open-weight 8B models and six binary persona traits, a per-instance oracle over layer subsets shows that the best layers vary from one input to the next: on most trait-model pairs, no fixed global layer set recovers the per-instance benefit. A greedy rule that ranks layers by single-layer marginal effect recovers nearly all of the oracle's benefit, but both must score candidate layers against the gold answer, so neither can run at deployment; the rule instead becomes the target a prompt-only predictor is trained to reproduce. Our deployable recipe needs no label at inference: a per-instance layer ranker read off the prompt embedding, a classifier that infers the steering direction, and an adaptive gate that scores short steered passes against that inferred direction and steers no more layers than necessary. The recipe recovers most of the oracle's lift (the bulk on the stronger model, a clear majority on the harder one), never drives any trait-model pair below its unsteered alignment baseline on average, and largely avoids the fluency collapse that strong global selection incurs at higher layer counts. A mechanistic account, "direction over magnitude", explains the behavioural flip under a mis-directed global set, the output collapse from steering too many layers, and the ceiling of unsteerable inputs.

</details>


### [112] [Explicit Boundary Markers for Subword Vocabularies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08847)
*Sander Land,Clara Meister*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种使用显式词边界标记的替代标准空格约定的方法，以解决子词分词器在空间使用型书写系统中对常见词重复表示的问题。通过边界标记分隔词，空格则用一对标记表示，并使用两种移位码处理首字母大写和全大写，使词的内部表示可跨不同情况复用。该方法虽未提升分词压缩效率（与基线相比字符/词仅差1%），但显著提升了语言建模性能，表明重复表示存在压缩无法捕捉的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准空格分词方式导致常见词在子词分词器中被重复表示，如带前导空格和不带前导空格的同一词分别拥有独立嵌入，且可能被不同方式分词，造成训练不一致和信息冗余；同时大小写进一步增加词形分裂。

Method: 引入显式词边界标记（word boundary marker），用其分隔词，空格由成对标记表示；使用两个移位码处理首字母大写和全大写，实现同一词在不同形式下的统一内部表示。

Result: 新标记方案在六种语言上与基线相比字符/词仅差不到1%，即分词压缩性能基本持平；但所有测试的标记方案在下游语言建模任务中均达到更低的每字节比特数，表明重复表示存在压缩未捕获的性能代价。

Conclusion: 采用显式词边界标记可有效缓解子词分词中的重复表示问题，尽管对压缩率影响微小，但能显著提升语言建模表现，说明当前模型对词形冗余敏感，压缩指标不足以全面评估分词质量。

Abstract: Subword tokenizers represent many common words twice in space-using writing systems, once with a leading space and once without. The two entries have separate embeddings in models, so occurrences of one word are divided across rows that are trained independently, and the two forms need not even segment the string the same way: " together" may be a single entry while the same word without a preceding space is tokenized as "to|gether". Capitalization divides a word further, into as many as six forms. We introduce an alternative to standard whitespace conventions using an explicit word boundary marker, which prevents such duplication. Words are delimited by the boundary markers, and spaces between words are represented as pairs of such markers. Two shift codes do the same for title case and upper case, allowing one internal representation of a word to be re-used across different settings. Switching to this convention mitigates the duplicate-entry issue, but does not improve tokenization compression: for both vocabulary-learning algorithms, the best marker scheme stays within one percent of the baseline in characters per token, averaged across six languages. It does result in better language modeling performance. Every marker scheme tested downstream reaches lower bits per byte than the baseline, suggesting that duplication carries a cost that compression does not capture.

</details>


### [113] [Conversation as Measurement in Clinical Encounters: Observable Phase Structure, Partially Observable Patient State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08868)
*Lily Chen,Ted Mau,Michael Gensheimer,Brian Anthony Nuyen,Nancy Jiang,James Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究临床对话中患者状态与对话阶段结构的可观测性，发现对话结构可被有效推断，而患者状态仅部分可观测，提示仅基于对话文本推断人类状态存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统假设从对话中可恢复人类互动与状态信息，但缺乏对这种信息是否真正可从对话记录中恢复的评估。为验证对话信号的极限，需克服标注误差与数据不完整问题。

Method: 采用439份真实临床对话记录（134小时），结合273份患者报告结果量表（PROMs）作为外部参考，使用符合隐私保护的GPT-5进行大规模标注，并通过40小时人工验证确保可靠性；以PROM得分（声音、咳嗽、吞咽）和对话阶段分割作为可观测目标。

Result: 发现可观测性不对称：对话阶段结构高度可观测且有助于理解临床会话组织；而患者状态仅部分可观测，即使在刻意引导症状表达的情境下亦如此。

Conclusion: 仅基于对话文本推断人类状态存在显著局限，应谨慎对待此类推断，尤其在医疗等高风险场景中。

Abstract: Many modern AI systems analyze conversational traces to infer aspects of human interaction and state, implicitly assuming that such information is recoverable from conversation. We study observability: whether a target is recoverable from conversational transcripts alone. Observability is difficult to assess because transcripts may provide only a partial view of many targets, and large-scale analysis requires model-based annotation, making true limits of the conversational signal hard to distinguish from annotator error. We therefore study clinical encounters, where patient-reported outcome measures (PROMs) provide an external anchor for patient state, and visits follow broadly structured patterns. We study observability of patient state and conversational phase structure using 439 real-world clinical encounter transcripts spanning 134 hours, including 245 ENT transcripts paired with 273 PROM surveys. We operationalize patient state using PROM scores for voice, cough, and swallowing; phase structure using conversational phase segmentation. To make these analyses credible at scale, we use a PHI-compliant GPT-5 deployment for transcript annotation and conduct 40 hours of manual validation, reducing the risk that apparent limits of observability simply reflect annotator error. Our core finding is an observability asymmetry: phase structure is observable and useful for characterizing clinical encounter organization, while patient state is only partially observable, even in a setting designed to elicit patient symptoms and experiences, cautioning against transcript-only inference of human state.

</details>


### [114] [Position Bias in Ordinal Classification: A Systematic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08869)
*Yu Wang,Jeffrey Zhou,Menglin Liu,Ge Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究系统分析了大语言模型在序数分类任务中对提示位置的敏感性，发现标签顺序、示范顺序和示范位置均显著影响模型预测。尽管尝试多种纠正方法（如点对点、成对、列表式推理及去偏策略），但效果不一且难以跨模型通用。结论指出，位置鲁棒性取决于整体系统配置而非仅模型本身，因此应综合考虑性能与稳定性来选择序数分类系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在序数分类中的应用日益广泛，但提示组织的语义等价变化可能导致预测结果波动。现有方法缺乏对位置偏差的系统理解与有效应对，亟需深入探究其来源与缓解策略。

Method: 通过三类探测实验（标签顺序、示范顺序、示范位置）在十个前沿大模型上测试其对序数分类任务的影响；进一步在五个数据集上分析八种提示、任务和模型层面因素；对比多种推理方式（点对点、成对、列表式）、聚合策略与去偏方法，并评估其在不同模型和偏差源间的泛化能力。

Result: 所有模型均对三种位置因素敏感，表明问题普遍存在；准确率与稳定性常不一致，仅低基数规模能同时提升两者；所测试的纠正方法无法提供可靠修复，而基于比较的列表式方法表现最佳，但跨模型转移能力有限。

Conclusion: 位置鲁棒性依赖于完整的系统配置，而非仅模型自身。因此，在构建序数分类系统时，必须联合考量预测性能与稳定性，避免仅依赖模型能力。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for ordinal classification, yet semantically equivalent changes to prompt organization can alter their predictions. We conduct systematic experiments to characterize positional bias from label order, demonstration order, and demonstration placement. First, we apply the three probes to ten frontier LLMs on a common ordinal-classification task; every model is sensitive to all three positional sources, showing that the problem is pervasive. Second, we vary eight prompt-, task-, and model-level factors across five datasets; accuracy and stability are often misaligned, and only lower scale cardinality consistently improves both. Third, we compare pointwise, pairwise, and listwise inference, alternative aggregation and debiasing methods, and joint configurations; the tested corrections do not provide a reliable remedy, while a comparison-based listwise formulation offers the best balance but transfers unevenly across models and bias sources. These findings show that positional robustness depends on the full system configuration rather than the model alone. Ordinal-classification systems should therefore be selected jointly for predictive performance and stability.

</details>


### [115] [Tied Trit-Planes: Constraining PTQTP to a Uniform Nine-Level Quantizer, with a Persistent Folded Format for Disk-Streamed Mixture-of-Experts Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08910)
*Matteo Grella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PTQTP方法，将大模型权重矩阵分解为两个三值平面并引入每组自由缩放因子，通过设定缩放因子为3的固定比例，将其转化为统一的九级量化器（平衡三元数恒等式），实现无损折叠为4比特代码平面。该4.0625比特/权重的表示形式在磁盘存储、专家缓存和内核输入中保持一致，支持单次整数点积计算。该方案首次在解算器中强制施加此恒等式约束，并在DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731（284B参数）上验证：仅需一次量化，即可从MXFP4权重流式加载专家模块，在64GB笔记本上运行。相比4.5比特Q4_K基线，新模型在5个测试场景中完全匹配官方接口（基线4/5），14个连续步骤中12个表现更优（基线11/14），MMLU子集得分86 vs 84，解码速度提升6.7%，文件体积减少9%。尽管权重重建误差更高、困惑度更差，但实际性能无显著差异，且所有偏差源于单一近似平局单元。研究还构建了累积的主干三值化阶梯与位对齐的aarch64/x86-64 SIMD内核，全部代码与工具开源于fucina推理栈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化方法难以在保持高精度的同时实现高效存储与推理，尤其在大规模混合专家模型中面临存储、传输与计算效率的多重挑战。本文旨在通过引入结构化的三值分解与缩放比例约束，实现低比特、无损、统一表示的量化框架，以提升端到端推理效率与部署灵活性。

Method: 提出PTQTP框架，将权重矩阵分解为两个三值平面，引入每组自由缩放因子；通过强制缩放比为3，生成统一的九级量化器（平衡三元数恒等式）；将两平面无损折叠为4比特代码平面，形成持久化表示；设计基于CPU-SIMD的定点内核，支持SSD流式加载专家模块，实现统一的4.0625比特/权重数据格式。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731模型上，所提方法实现一次量化、流式加载、相同持久化字节表示，解码速度提升6.7%，文件体积减小9%，在5/5初始测试与12/14后续步长中达到或超过基线表现，MMLU得分86（基线84），且未检测到显著精度损失。尽管权重重建误差和困惑度略差，但实际性能与参考模型高度一致，表明代理指标与真实性能存在脱钩现象。

Conclusion: 本工作首次在量化求解器中引入平衡三元数恒等式作为约束，成功构建了一个高效、统一、可部署的4比特量化框架。其在压缩率、速度与精度之间取得良好平衡，且具备完整的开源生态支持，为大模型轻量化部署提供了新的范式。

Abstract: PTQTP decomposes LLM weight matrices into two ternary (trit) planes with two free per-group scales. Tying the scales to a fixed ratio of three collapses the decomposition into a single uniform nine-level quantizer, a known balanced-ternary identity. To our knowledge, at the time of writing, this work is the first to impose that identity as a constraint inside PTQTP's solver. The two trit planes then fold losslessly into one 4-bit code plane that we make the persistent serving representation: disk bytes, expert-cache bytes, and kernel input are the same 4.0625-bits/weight blocks, consumed in one integer dot pass. For this conjunction (ratio-3 nine-level code, CPU-SIMD kernels, SSD expert streaming, identical persistent bytes) we likewise found no precedent. We apply this to the routed experts of DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731, a 284B-A13B mixture-of-experts model, quantizing in one shot from the released MXFP4 expert weights and streaming experts from SSD on a 64 GB laptop. Against a 4.5-bit Q4_K baseline, measured one process per fixture with an expert-lossless anchor arm as reference control, the tied model matches the official serving API on 5/5 fixtures at step 0 (Q4_K: 4/5) and 12/14 captured continuation steps (11/14), scores 86 vs. 84 on a 100-item MMLU subset, decodes 6.7% faster in decode phase, and ships 9% smaller files: no detected fidelity difference at these small evaluation sizes, and every fixture-level difference between the arms traces to a single measured near-tie cell. The tied fit nevertheless shows higher weight-reconstruction error and worse perplexity, a measured dissociation between proxy metrics and reference fidelity. A cumulative trunk-ternarization ladder and bitwise-pinned aarch64/x86-64 kernels complete the report. All code, formats, and evaluation artifacts are open source in the fucina inference stack.

</details>


### [116] [Investigating Multimodal Informativity under Different Partner Visibility Conditions in Video-Mediated Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08915)
*Esam Ghaleb,Hugh Mee Wong,Kristina Kobrock*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了手势与语音在多模态对话中携带的指称信息，特别是在不同对话伙伴可见性条件下的作用。实验表明，仅凭手势即可预测目标对象，且当仅依赖语音的模型不确定时，多模态融合效果最佳。通过训练时对齐表示与目标图像，进一步提升了融合模型性能。与人类交互数据对比发现，对话伙伴可见性影响手势的产生和信息量，并存在言语和多模态表现的同步效应，但手势表现无此效应。研究为多模态对话建模及利用模型表示分析人类交互提供了贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对话模型通常仅依赖语音转录文本，忽略了手势等非语言信息在指称交流中的重要作用。然而，手势能提供语音中缺失或不明确的信息，因此需要探索手势与语音在多模态对话中的互补性及其在不同可见性条件下的表现差异。

Method: 构建基于语音转录、手势骨骼表示或两者结合的模型，用于视频中介的指称通信任务中识别目标对象；采用多模态融合策略，在语音模型不确定时提升性能；引入训练阶段对齐学习表示与目标图像的方法以优化融合模型；并与人类交互数据进行比较分析。

Result: 手势单独可有效预测目标对象；多模态融合在语音模型不确定时最为有益；训练时对齐表示与目标图像显著提升融合模型性能；人类数据表明可见性影响手势的生成和信息量，且存在言语和多模态表现的同步效应，但手势本身未表现出类似现象。

Conclusion: 手势在指称交流中具有独立信息价值，多模态融合在特定条件下更具优势；通过表示对齐可进一步提升模型性能；研究揭示了人机交互中多模态行为的复杂性，为多模态对话建模与人类交互分析提供了新视角。

Abstract: Situated language use is multimodal and embodied. For example, gestures can carry information that is absent or underspecified in the speech signal, yet dialogue models typically rely on transcripts alone. We study how much referential information gestures and their combination with speech carry in multimodal dialogue under different partner visibility conditions. % We build models that identify the intended referent in a video-mediated referential communication game based on either the speech transcript, the skeletal representation of gesture, or both modalities. Our results show that gesture alone is predictive of the intended referent and that multimodal fusion is most beneficial when the transcript-based model is uncertain. Training-only alignment of learned representations with the referent image further improves the fusion model performance. % In a comparison with human interaction data, we further see pragmatic effects of interlocutor visibility on gesture production and informativeness as well as an entrainment effect in speech and multimodal, but not gesture, performance across rounds of repeated interaction. We thus make contributions to the technical modelling of multimodal information in human dialogue and the analysis of human interaction data via trained model representations.

</details>


### [117] [Same Question, Different Answer? Measuring and Mitigating Prompt Privilege for Equitable AI Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08942)
*Lier Jin,Lan Hu,Binqi Shen,Hanyu Cai,Yuting Xin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出“提示特权”（Prompt Privilege）概念，指不同用户因提示技巧差异导致相同意图获得不同模型表现的问题。为此，作者设计了提示公平性评分（PES）和提示公平性变换器（PET），通过自动优化用户输入以提升可访问性，使AI系统更公平、包容。实验表明，PET能有效消除低素养与专家用户间的性能差距，保障语义一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注对抗攻击和提示优化，忽视了不同表达方式对同一意图响应差异的影响。这种‘提示特权’现象导致非专业用户在使用大语言模型时处于不利地位，影响AI系统的公平性和可及性。

Method: 提出统一框架，引入提示公平性评分（PES）量化不同用户群体的响应一致性；设计基于LLM的提示公平性变换器（PET），将提示优化任务由用户转移至系统端，实现语义保持下的可访问性增强。

Result: 在MedQA基准上验证了显著的提示特权现象，低素养用户表现明显低于专家；应用PET后，各群体间性能差异消失，且语义保真度高，证明该方法能有效提升提示公平性。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了提示特权作为新型AI可访问性挑战的重要性，并通过PET提供了一种系统级解决方案，推动构建更公平、可信、包容的人工智能系统。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into healthcare, education, public services, and everyday decision making. They should provide comparable assistance regardless of a user's literacy, communication style, or prompt-engineering expertise. However, existing research on prompt robustness primarily focuses on adversarial attacks, prompt injection, and prompt optimization, while overlooking whether semantically equivalent requests receive different responses simply because they are phrased differently. We refer to this accessibility challenge as "Prompt Privilege": users with greater prompting expertise systematically obtain better model performance despite expressing the same underlying intent.
  To address this problem, we present a unified framework for measuring and mitigating accessibility disparities in LLM interactions. We introduce Prompt Equity Score (PES), a quantitative metric for evaluating performance consistency across user populations, and Prompt Equity Transformer (PET), an LLM-based agent that automatically transforms user requests into semantically equivalent, accessibility-oriented prompts while preserving their intent. PET shifts prompt optimization from the user to the AI system, functioning as an intelligent accessibility layer between users and foundation models. Experiments on the MedQA benchmark demonstrate measurable prompt privilege, with statistically significant performance disparities between low-literacy and expert-prompting cohorts. Applying PET eliminates these disparities while preserving semantic fidelity, demonstrating that accessibility-oriented prompt normalization can improve equitable AI access. By introducing prompt privilege as a new dimension of AI accessibility and PET as a practical solution, this work advances system-centered accessibility and provides a foundation for more fair, trustworthy, and inclusive AI systems.

</details>


### [118] [How Can Rhetoric Reward-Hack AI Reviewers? Dissecting Rhetorical Sensitivity in AI-Based Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08975)
*Ming Li,Chenguang Wang,Xirui Li,Xinyue Zeng,Dianqi Li,Peng Shi,Dawei Zhou,Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大型语言模型在科学评估中因修辞选择导致的奖励黑客问题，通过构建包含4,200篇论文的受控语料库，分析不同修辞维度对AI评审评分的影响。结果显示，修辞敏感性具有结构性而非均匀性，证据框架和新颖性立场产生最显著的正负对比，范围框架次之；其他维度影响较小或不稳定。评分变化趋势依赖于原始分数：低分倾向上升，高分倾向下降，中等分数区间的差异最明显。更复杂的重写流程并未带来稳定增益，联合重写、递归重写及评审引导均未表现出一致优势，且重复重写收益递减。重写者决定对立变体间的分离程度，评审者决定评分效应的幅度与方向。严格评审虽降低平均得分1.36分，但未改变修辞敏感性格局。研究揭示了修辞表达对AI科学评审的影响机制，呼吁建立对内容不变但表达方式变化具备鲁棒性的评估系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型越来越多地参与科学评估，研究其可能受到修辞策略影响而产生奖励黑客行为，特别是当科学内容保持不变时，修辞选择如何影响AI评审判断，以及这种影响在不同评估条件下是否一致，成为亟待解决的问题。

Method: 构建由120个匿名ICLR 2026投稿生成的4,200篇论文的受控语料库；使用两个大语言模型对六种修辞维度进行相反方向的重写；由五个大语言模型评审者在标准与严格协议下评估结果；测试联合、递归及评审引导式重写等多种流程。

Result: 修辞敏感性呈现结构化特征：证据框架和新颖性立场对整体评价影响最大，范围框架次之，其余维度影响较小或不稳定。该层级关系在不同人类评估质量水平下保持一致，但评分变化方向取决于原始评分——低分上升、高分下降，中等区间差异最显著。复杂重写流程未能带来稳定提升：联合重写高度依赖重写者，评审引导未优于无指导重写，重复重写收益递减且依赖配置。重写者主导对立变体的区分度，评审者决定评分效应的大小与方向。严格评审使平均得分下降1.36点，但未改变修辞敏感性模式。

Conclusion: 本研究识别出在何种情况下修辞表达会影响AI科学评审，并强调需设计对内容不变但表达方式变化具备鲁棒性的科学评估系统，以防止因非实质内容的修辞操纵导致评价偏差。

Abstract: As large language models increasingly participate in scientific evaluation, we investigate a potential form of reward hacking: how rhetorical choices shape AI-review judgments when reported scientific content is preserved and how these effects vary across evaluation conditions. We construct a controlled corpus of 4,200 full-paper manuscripts derived from 120 anonymized ICLR 2026 submissions. Two LLM rewriters transform six rhetorical dimensions in opposing directions, and five LLM reviewers evaluate the resulting manuscripts under standard and strict protocols. We also test joint, recursive, and reviewer-guided rewriting. Our results show that rhetorical sensitivity is structured rather than uniform. Evidence framing and novelty stance produce the largest positive-negative contrasts in overall assessment, with scope framing forming a weaker second tier; the remaining dimensions have smaller or less stable effects. This hierarchy persists across human-assessed quality levels, but score movement depends strongly on the AI reviewer's original score: lower scores tend to rise, higher scores tend to fall, and directional contrasts are clearest in the middle ranges. More elaborate workflows do not reliably yield larger gains. Joint rewriting is strongly rewriter-dependent, reviewer guidance does not consistently outperform an unguided second pass, and repeated rewriting yields diminishing, configuration-dependent returns. Across conditions, the rewriter primarily determines the separation between opposing variants, whereas the reviewer determines the magnitude and sign of their score effects. Strict review lowers mean OA by 1.36 points without consistently changing rhetorical sensitivity. These findings identify when rhetorical presentation influences AI scientific review and motivate evaluation systems robust to content-preserving variation in scientific writing.

</details>


### [119] [How Far Do Foundation Models Transfer to Infant Signals? A Cross-Dataset Transfer Audit with a Unified Need Ontology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08989)
*Wu Hangyu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过多层级泄漏审计（字节级与嵌入级去重及同数据集内训练-测试近似重复检测），分析了四个婴儿哭声语料库在统一五类需求本体和共享任务设定下的表现。单语料库评估掩盖了显著的性能波动（同一编码器在域内宏F1值差异达0.57–0.80）、跨语料迁移平均为负（负迁移率0.19–0.35，30个方向中有18个显著），且存在349组内容相同但标签冲突的片段。然而，审计也揭示了可行路径：向最嘈杂语料库迁移在匹配训练规模下始终正向有效，尤其在去除近似重复后；冻结探针在小标签预算下饱和，而稳定微调在全标签下更优；领域自适应预训练在5–10样本时显著优于稳定微调（1样本优势不稳健），但在50样本及以上无显著优势。在统一标签设置下，本体映射联合训练在所有组合中均最优，而直接合并未映射标签会损失高达37 F1分。研究开源本体、映射代码与审计流程，使不兼容哭声语料库可转化为联合训练资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公共婴儿哭声语料库规模小、标签不兼容，且通常逐个评估，这种做法隐藏了真实性能差异与迁移效应，导致研究结论不可靠。

Method: 采用多层级泄漏审计（字节级、嵌入级去重及同数据集内近似重复检测），对四个哭声语料库进行清洗，并在统一五类需求本体和共享任务框架下，使用四个冻结编码器与手工基线进行评估，系统分析跨语料迁移、标签一致性与模型表现关系。

Result: 单语料库评估掩盖了显著的性能波动（宏F1差0.57–0.80）和负迁移现象（负迁移率0.19–0.35）；存在大量内容相同但标签冲突的样本（349组）；向最嘈杂语料库迁移在匹配训练量下始终正向有效；冻结探针在小标签预算下饱和，稳定微调在全标签下更优；领域自适应预训练在5–10样本时占优，但50样本后无优势；本体映射联合训练全面胜出，直接合并未映射标签损失高达37 F1分。

Conclusion: 统一评估框架与跨语料审计能揭示单语料评估隐藏的问题，提出有效的联合训练路径，通过本体映射实现多源哭声数据融合，显著提升模型鲁棒性与泛化能力。开源工具链支持构建更大、更一致的婴儿哭声分析资源。

Abstract: Public infant cry corpora are small, label-incompatible, and almost always evaluated one corpus at a time. We ask what this practice hides and what fixes it. Across four cry corpora screened by a multi-level leakage audit (byte-level and embedding-level deduplication plus a within-corpus train-test near-duplicate audit), we probe four frozen encoders and a handcrafted baseline under a unified five-class need ontology and shared task formulations. The audit exposes what single-corpus evaluation conceals: within-domain macro-F1 swings by 0.57-0.80 for the same encoder, cross-corpus transfer is negative on average (negative-transfer ratio 0.19-0.35, significant in 18 of 30 directed cells, BH-FDR), and 349 content-identical clip groups carry conflicting metadata labels across corpus distributions. The same audit, however, reveals a consistent way forward. Transfer into the noisiest corpus is consistently positive in effect size at matched training size and after near-duplicate removal, offering a practical recipe for small, noisy corpora. Frozen probes saturate at modest label budgets, while stabilized fine-tuning wins with full labels; domain-adaptive pretraining significantly beats stabilized fine-tuning at 5-10-shot (the 1-shot advantage is not robust to optimization-seed variance) but shows no significant advantage at 50-shot or beyond. In the tested binary, shared-label settings, ontology-mapped joint training wins in all four encoder-by-target combinations, whereas naively merging unmapped labels costs up to 37 F1 points. We release the ontology, mapping code, and audit pipeline, turning incompatible cry corpora into a usable joint-training resource.

</details>


### [120] [ELICITED: EHR-grounded Longitudinal Interactive Conversations for Information-seeking Triage Evaluation and Decision-making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09024)
*Haohao Zhu,Xiaolin Shi,Jiayu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EHR2Dial-Triage框架，用于在MIMIC-IV-ED基础上构建基于角色和时间边界的急诊分诊对话，实现对信息获取、证据使用、五级紧急程度指数预测及面向患者的沟通的可控评估，支持将分诊视为动态临床信息采集、推理与沟通过程的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有急诊分诊评估多基于静态临床快照，无法捕捉分诊过程中信息逐步获取与判断更新的交互式动态过程；现有医疗对话数据集缺乏与电子健康记录（EHR）事件的时间顺序关联，限制了对真实临床决策流程的研究。

Method: 构建EHR2Dial-Triage框架，基于MIMIC-IV-ED数据，设定角色与时间边界，生成符合临床实际的分诊对话；每个患者披露内容均关联至其对应的EHR事件及首次可用的对话轮次，确保信息流可追溯。

Result: 该框架支持对模型与患者角色在信息获取、证据利用、五级急诊严重度指数预测及患者沟通方面的系统性评估，为研究对话式分诊提供了结构化且可控的实验环境。

Conclusion: EHR2Dial-Triage为急诊分诊的动态认知与交互过程提供了新的研究范式，有助于提升智能系统在真实临床场景中对分诊逻辑的理解与模拟能力。

Abstract: Emergency-department (ED) triage requires clinicians to rapidly identify patients who need immediate attention, determine who can safely wait, and prioritize limited clinical resources. At presentation, however, information may be limited to a chief complaint and initial vital signs. Clinically important details, including symptom onset and progression, associated symptoms, medical history, and medication use, are often obtained through focused conversation. Effective triage therefore requires clinicians to identify information gaps, ask appropriate follow-up questions, and update their assessment as new evidence becomes available. Most existing ED benchmarks evaluate acuity prediction from a fixed clinical snapshot. Although this formulation measures predictive performance after patient information has been assembled, it does not capture the interactive process through which triage-relevant evidence is elicited and interpreted. Existing medical dialogue datasets support the study of clinical communication, but dialogue statements are not always linked to temporally ordered events in the electronic health record (EHR). We introduce EHR2Dial-Triage, an agentic conversation-generation framework and benchmark grounded in MIMIC-IV-ED. The framework constructs triage conversations under explicit role-based and temporal information boundaries. Each accepted patient disclosure is linked to its supporting EHR event and the first dialogue turn at which it becomes available. EHR2Dial-Triage enables controlled evaluation of information elicitation, evidence use, five-level Emergency Severity Index prediction, and patient-facing communication across models and patient personas. It provides a structured setting for studying conversational triage as a dynamic process of clinical information acquisition, reasoning, and communication.

</details>


### [121] [Don't Scroll Back: Missing-Evidence Memory for Streaming Dialogue Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09043)
*Hyangsuk Min,Hwanjun Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为ReMEMBER的缺失证据记忆框架，用于解决流式对话摘要中的上下文不足问题。通过聚焦于恢复当前窗口所依赖的关键证据，该方法在有限记忆预算下显著提升了记忆召回率和缺口填补的完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代平台用户需要对近期对话进行总结，但当前窗口通常缺乏足够上下文独立理解，因此需要从无界历史中选择性地记忆关键信息以支持摘要生成。核心挑战在于能否恢复当前窗口所隐含的必要证据，而非访问的历史量多少。

Method: 提出流式对话摘要任务形式化定义，构建基准数据集与评估协议，分别评估记忆是否包含填补空白的证据以及摘要是否反映这些证据；设计ReMEMBER框架，基于未解决的窗口依赖条件化检索，并在固定预算下将检索片段优化为高密度证据记忆。

Result: 在长达16万词元的历史对话上实验表明，ReMEMBER在相同记忆预算下，相比基线方法显著提升了记忆召回率和缺口填补的完整性。

Conclusion: 记忆是否包含关键证据是流式对话摘要的核心挑战，而ReMEMBER通过针对性检索与证据密集化优化，在有限资源下有效提升了记忆质量和摘要表现。

Abstract: Users of modern platforms repeatedly need summaries of recent dialogue, but the window rarely contains enough context to be interpreted on its own. We formalize this setting as streaming dialogue summarization, where a system must summarize a current window using selective memory from an unbounded history under a fixed budget. We show that the central challenge is not how much history is accessed, but whether memory recovers the evidence that the current window presupposes. We construct a benchmark and evaluation protocol that separately assesses whether memory contains gap-resolving evidence and whether the generated summary reflects it. We propose ReMEMBER, a missing-evidence memory framework that conditions retrieval on unresolved window dependencies and refines retrieved chunks into evidence-dense memory under a fixed budget. Experiments on dialogues with histories up to 160K tokens show that ReMEMBER improves memory recall and gap-resolution completeness over memory construction baselines under the same budget.

</details>


### [122] [Tree-of-Experience: Hierarchical Experience Management for Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09044)
*Zihao Deng,Yining Zhu,Leiming Wang,Jingfei Lu,Junbo Wang,Chuncheng Ran,Yu Yang,Dixuan Yang,Jikun Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ToE（Tree-of-Experience）的结构化经验管理框架，旨在将大模型代理在环境交互中获得的经验与层级化推理过程对齐，提升复杂推理任务中的反馈归因、跨任务迁移及更新与检索效率。通过构建共享的分析视角与推理路径树，并基于环境结果校准其可靠性，实现系统性更新与高效利用。实验表明，在Game of 24和FinEvolveBench上，ToE显著优于基线方法，准确率提升31.4%，tsIC平均提高41.24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理经验时，常将轨迹单独优化或抽象共性知识，但其经验表示与底层推理过程脱节，导致反馈归因困难、跨任务迁移能力弱以及更新与检索效率低，尤其在仅提供结果级反馈的复杂推理任务中表现不佳。

Method: 提出Tree-of-Experience（ToE）框架，将经验组织为共享的分析视角与推理路径构成的树状结构，通过环境结果校准各节点的可靠性，支持系统性更新、跨任务迁移与高效检索。

Result: 在Game of 24任务中，ToE相比无经验的ToT基线，准确率相对提升31.4%；在FinEvolveBench上，相对于无经验管道，tsIC平均提升41.24%，且传统经验管理方法常低于无经验基线。

Conclusion: ToE通过将经验组织与推理过程对齐，有效提升了大模型代理在复杂推理任务中的性能与效率，证明了结构化经验管理对于持续自我演进的重要性。

Abstract: Continual self-evolution requires LLM agents to transform environmental interactions into reliable and reusable experience. Existing methods typically refine individual trajectories or abstract shared knowledge from related trajectories, but their experience representations are often disconnected from the underlying reasoning process. This limits feedback attribution, cross-task transfer, and update and retrieval efficiency, particularly in complex reasoning tasks with outcome-level feedback. To overcome this limitation, we propose \textbf{T}ree-\textbf{o}f-\textbf{E}xperience (ToE), a structured experience-management framework that aligns experience organization with the hierarchical reasoning process of LLM agents. Specifically, ToE organizes the experience into a shared tree of analytical perspectives and reasoning paths, whose reliability is calibrated through environmental outcomes to support systematic updating, transfer, and efficient retrieval. The experimental results on \textsc{Game of 24} and \textsc{FinEvolveBench} show that ToE substantially improves both problem-solving performance and efficiency. On \textsc{Game of 24}, ToE achieves a 31.4\% relative improvement in accuracy over the experience-free ToT baseline. On \textsc{FinEvolveBench}, ToE improves tsIC by an average of 41.24\% over the experience-free pipeline across 12 evaluation settings, whereas conventional experience-management methods often underperform experience-free baselines.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Towards an Argumentative Foundation for Evaluative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07473)
*Xiang Yin,Tim Miller,Nico Potyka,Antonio Rago,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文倡导将计算论证作为支持可解释、可争议的评估型人工智能（EAI）的形式化和可计算基础，推动分布式和以人为中心的EAI系统长期研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的AI系统多提供单一推荐，缺乏对决策过程的透明性和可争议性；为增强人类在复杂决策中的参与与信任，需要一种能够呈现多个竞争假设及其正反证据的机制。

Method: 提出以计算论证为核心范式，构建形式化框架，支持多假设对比与证据评估，使EAI具备可解释性与可辩论性。

Result: 确立了计算论证在支持人机协同决策中的潜力，为未来分布式、以人为本的EAI系统提供了理论基础与研究方向。

Conclusion: 计算论证是实现可解释、可争议的EAI的关键路径，应成为未来人机协作系统设计的核心支柱。

Abstract: Evaluative AI (EAI) has been recently proposed as a way to support human decision-making, not by producing a single recommendation, but by presenting competing hypotheses together with evidence for and against each. In this position paper, we advocate (computational) argumentation as a particularly suitable paradigm to provide a formal, computable foundation for forms of EAI that are explainable and contestable, setting the ground for a long-term research agenda towards distributed and human-centred EAI systems.

</details>


### [124] [Flow-by-Flow:Content-Judgment Bypass for Governing AI Output in High-Loss Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07474)
*Hiroki Naito*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在高损失领域中，当AI输出速度V超过人类认知能力C_max时，人机协作监督机制的结构性不可行性。核心约束并非仅速度V，而是V×L（每项任务的认知负荷）。L由筛选、判断和响应三部分构成，其中只有判断成本随模型能力提升而下降，且常通过遗漏而非真正减少实现。因此，能力提升重构而非降低L。现有治理机制要么将评估权交给AI导致幻觉风险，要么交给人类则面临V×L上限。为此提出Flow-by-Flow治理范式：通过基于可计数特征的非线性成本控制高吞吐量，结合机构容量上限维持在人类处理能力内。提出四个设计不变量：不进行内容判断、不扩展审查者容量、身份绑定应用摩擦、不批量放行。一个参考实现证明这些不变量可同时满足，尽管存在实际困难。蒙特卡洛模拟显示，多指标流控优于单纯强化监督在90.8%的试验中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高损失场景下，随着AI输出速度加快，传统依赖人工审核的监督机制因人类认知极限而失效。现有方法或引入幻觉风险，或无法应对超负荷压力，亟需新的治理框架以避免系统性失败。

Method: 提出Flow-by-Flow治理范式，利用可量化特征构建非线性成本函数，限制高吞吐量；设置机构处理容量上限；定义四条设计不变量以防止内容判断与容量滥用；并通过蒙特卡洛模拟验证其有效性。

Result: Monte Carlo分析表明，复合多指标流控在90.8%的模拟中优于单纯强化监督；参考实现证明四条设计不变量可共存，尽管存在实践挑战。

Conclusion: Flow-by-Flow提供了一种不依赖内容判断的可行治理路径，在面对高速高负荷AI输出时能有效控制监督成本，突破传统监督瓶颈，具备理论可行性与实证优势。

Abstract: Prior work showed that human-in-the-loop oversight becomes structurally untenable in high-loss domains when AI output velocity V exceeds human cognitive capacity C_max. The operative constraint, however, is not V alone but V x L, where L denotes per-item cognitive load. L consists of triage, judgment, and response, which respond asymmetrically to AI capability improvement. Triage cost does not decline as models become more capable, because semantic indeterminacy is inherent in general-purpose design. Response cost is invariant to accuracy improvements. Only judgment cost faces downward pressure, and this pressure often operates by inducing omission rather than genuine reduction. Capability improvement therefore restructures L rather than reducing it. Governance mechanisms based on evaluating whether AI output is correct either delegate that evaluation to AI and inherit hallucination risk, or delegate it to humans and face the V x L ceiling. We propose Flow-by-Flow, a governance paradigm that controls supervisory load without evaluating content. A cognitive cost score based on formal, countable features imposes nonlinear costs on high-volume production, while an institutional capacity cap keeps processing volume within C_max. We derive four design invariants for any content-judgment-bypass exceedance pathway: no content judgment, no scalable consumption of examiner capacity, identity-bound per-application friction, and no batch clearance. One reference implementation is discussed to show that these invariants are jointly satisfiable, while its practical difficulties are explicitly acknowledged. An illustrative Monte Carlo analysis across 1,000 parameter draws suggests that composite multi-metric flow control outperforms supervision reinforcement alone in 90.8% of trials.

</details>


### [125] [Determinization in Structure Theories: A Unified Framework via Closure, Comparability, and Joint Admissibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07476)
*Hai Hai Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个形式化框架，用于从多重结构理论中构建规范解释。通过区分三种规范层级（闭包稳定、全局完成、确定化），并分类非确定性为认知多元（Type E）与结构多元（Type S），提出基于算子的完成与基于选择器的构造两种机制。在特定结构条件下，推理驱动的完成可归约为饱和闭包算子；对Type E理论，闭包稳定成立；对Type S-strong理论，可通过规范选择实现确定化。多级规范形成非交换系统，且理论内机制可被归约为闭包或选择。该框架亦适用于大模型辅助推理，其中幻觉可视为无支持的规范化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构理论缺乏统一的规范解释构建方法，尤其在处理非确定性与多层级一致性时存在理论空白。本文旨在建立一套形式化框架，以系统化地生成唯一或一致的解释，提升理论可解释性与推理可靠性，同时拓展至大语言模型等实际应用场景。

Method: 提出结构理论三元组T = (Σ, A, I)，定义全局一致的解释族；引入闭包稳定、全局完成与确定化三级规范化机制；基于算子与选择器设计两种实现路径；分析非确定性类型及其结构性条件；利用阶段算子研究系统非交换性；给出理论内机制的归约定理。

Result: 建立了完整的规范解释构建框架，证明了在正则、非回溯规则下推理完成可归约为饱和闭包算子；确立了Type E理论的闭包稳定性；实现了Type S-strong理论的确定化；揭示了多级规范化的非交换结构；将理论内机制归约为闭包或选择；并将框架应用于大模型幻觉建模，指出其本质为无支持的规范化行为。

Conclusion: 本框架为结构理论的规范解释提供了系统化、形式化的构建路径，不仅深化了对非确定性与一致性关系的理解，也为大模型推理中的可信性问题提供了理论工具，具有重要的理论与应用价值。

Abstract: We develop a formal framework for constructing canonical interpretations from plural structure theories. A structure theory is a triple T = (Σ, A, I) consisting of a signature, axioms, and an inference policy, whose admissible interpretation family collects all globally consistent assignments of structural conclusions.
  We distinguish three levels of canonicalization: closure stabilization (per-seed convergence), global completion (seed-independent convergence), and determinization (a unique admissible interpretation). Non-determinism is classified into epistemic plurality (Type E) and structural plurality (Type S), with a refined Type S-strong subclass characterized by the absence of common upper bounds.
  Two canonicalization mechanisms arise: operator-based completion and selector-based construction. We provide sufficient structural conditions under which these mechanisms exist, and show that pure inference-based completion reduces to a saturated closure operator under positive, non-retractive rules with an additional soundness condition. For Type E theories, closure stabilization is established, while full determinization depends on a global confluence property that remains open. For Type S-strong theories, determinization is achieved via canonical selection.
  We further show that multi-level canonicalization forms a structurally non-commutative system via staged operators, and provide a conditional classification theorem reducing theory-intrinsic mechanisms to closure or selection. The framework also applies to LLM-assisted reasoning, where hallucination can be viewed as unsupported canonicalization.

</details>


### [126] [Emotion in an active inference model of human driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07480)
*Julian F. Schumann,Johan Engström,Ran Wei,Jens Kober,Martijn Wisse,Arkady Zgonnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展的效价和唤醒度模型，用于在连续状态空间的驾驶主动推理模型中提取情感信号，并结合当前状态与未来预测结果来估计情感。通过两个交互式驾驶场景验证，该方法生成的情感信号与实际情境中的情感模式相符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动推理模型在驾驶行为建模中未充分考虑情绪状态这一重要影响因素，而情绪在交通决策中起关键作用。尽管已有研究在非交通领域使用环形模型表示情绪（效价与唤醒度），但仅限于离散状态空间的简化场景，无法适用于复杂的连续状态驾驶环境。

Method: 提出一种基于连续状态空间的主动推理框架，将效价和唤醒度作为可从模型中提取的情感变量，不仅依赖当前状态，还结合对未来结果的预测进行条件化估计。

Result: 在两个交互式驾驶场景中评估表明，所提出的模型能够生成与真实人类情感反应一致的情绪信号，验证了其有效性与合理性。

Conclusion: 该研究拓展了主动推理在驾驶建模中的应用，成功将情绪状态纳入连续状态空间的主动推理框架，为理解情绪如何影响驾驶行为提供了新的建模路径。

Abstract: Active inference has emerged as a principled framework for modeling adaptive behavior by balancing goal-directed action with uncertainty reduction. It has been successfully applied across biological and artificial systems, including recent work on human driving. However, existing active inference models of driving have yet to address an important determinant of behavior in traffic: affective state, which significantly influences decision-making. Prior work in non-traffic domains has explored active inference agents in which emotions are represented along the axes of valence and arousal in the circumplex model. However, this work has been limited to simplified settings with discrete state spaces. In this work, we propose an expanded formulation of valence and arousal that can be extracted from a more complex active inference model of driving with continuous states. In particular, we condition affective estimates not only on the current state but also on predicted future outcomes. We evaluate the proposed approach in two interactive driving scenarios and show that the resulting emotion signals correspond to affective patterns reported in similar scenarios.

</details>


### [127] [Training Variable Long Sequences with Data-Centric Parallel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07524)
*Geng Zhang,Xuanlei Zhao,Kai Wang,Yang You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Data-Centric Parallel (DCP)方法，通过让数据驱动运行时动态调整并行规模、梯度累积和重计算等设置，解决长序列深度学习训练中的效率与易用性矛盾问题。实测在32张H200 GPU上最高提速2.88倍，仅需10行代码即可适配任意模型，具有强通用性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理变长序列训练时面临效率与易用性的权衡：简单方法导致负载不均、效率低；复杂方法则引入高开发成本和代码改动。亟需一种既高效又易于集成的解决方案。

Method: 提出数据驱动的运行时动态调整机制——Data-Centric Parallel (DCP)，根据每个批次的序列长度实时调整并行规模、梯度累积步数和梯度重计算策略，实现自适应优化。

Result: 在32张H200 GPU上实现最高2.88倍的训练加速，且对多种模型具备良好泛化能力，仅需少量代码修改即可集成。

Conclusion: DCP是一种简单而有效的分布式训练框架，可作为变长序列训练的稳健基线，推动该领域未来研究发展。

Abstract: Training deep learning models on variable long sequences poses significant computational challenges. Existing methods force a difficult trade-off between efficiency and ease-of-use. Simple approaches use static configurations that cause workload imbalance low efficiency, while complex methods introduces significant complexity and code change for new models. To break this trade-off, we introduce Data-Centric Parallel (DCP). Its core principle is to let the data itself drive the runtime. It achieves this by dynamically adjusting direct runtime settings (e.g., parallel size, gradient accumulation, recomputation) based on each batch's sequence length. Empirical results demonstrate that our method achieves up to a 2.88$\times$ speedup on 32 H200 GPUs. Designed for generalization, it can be integrated into any model with 10 lines of code. We anticipate this simple yet effective approach will serve as a robust baseline and facilitate future advancements in distributed training for variable long sequences.

</details>


### [128] [The Knowing-Saying Gap: When Probes See Errors that Confidence Misses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07528)
*Jyotin Goel,Ipshita Bandyopadhyay,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 线性探测器能以近乎完美的准确率检测语言模型中的上下文损坏，但这种能力并未转化为可靠的失败预测。在多跳算术链中，探测器虽能识别损坏，却无法有效预测最终答案的正确性；强制模型采用结构化置信度格式后，其置信值趋于两极分化且错误率难以区分；跨步骤的探测器持续性也无法区分正确与错误结果，推翻了预先注册的‘持续性优于峰值’假设。这一现象在多种模型家族中普遍存在，表明‘知而不言’具有普遍性。作为实时监控手段，基于探测器的干预措施高度依赖模型和错误类型：分支选择（branch-and-pick）在多数模型中表现良好，尤其在Llama-3.1-8B上无破坏性（4次挽救，0次破坏），而重提示（reprompt）和替换前值（replace-prior）则在拯救错误输出的同时同样破坏正确路径。因此，探测器监控是口头置信度的必要补充，但无单一干预策略占优，部署答案应为模型感知、错误类型感知的路由决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究线性探测器在检测语言模型上下文损坏方面的有效性及其对最终输出可靠性预测的能力，揭示当前探测方法在实际部署中的局限性，并推动更智能、更适应性的监控与干预机制发展。

Method: 通过多跳算术任务测试不同模型（包括推理型模型）中线性探测器的表现，分析其在检测上下文损坏、预测答案正确性、保持跨步骤一致性等方面的能力；评估多种基于探测器的实时干预策略（如branch-and-pick、reprompt、replace-prior）在不同模型上的效果，比较其对正确与错误路径的破坏与修复情况。

Result: 探测器虽能精准检测上下文损坏，但无法可靠预测最终答案正确性；模型结构化置信度格式下置信值趋于极端且错误率不可区分；探测器持续性无法区分正确与错误结果；不同干预策略的效果高度依赖模型和错误类型，其中branch-and-pick在多数模型中表现最优且对Llama-3.1-8B无破坏性，而其他策略常等量破坏正确路径；单一干预策略无法普适适用。

Conclusion: 探测器监控是增强模型可解释性和安全性的重要补充，但必须结合模型特性和错误类型进行动态路由决策，不能依赖单一干预方式。未来的部署系统需实现模型感知、错误类型感知的自适应监控与修正机制。

Abstract: Linear probes detect corrupted context in language models with near-perfect accuracy, yet this does not translate into reliable failure prediction. The result is a dissociation with direct implications for deployment monitoring. Across multi-hop arithmetic chains, probes that detect corruption turn out to be uninformative about final answer correctness; models forced into structured confidence formats collapse to two values with indistinguishable error rates; and probe persistence across hops fails to separate correct from incorrect outcomes, refuting our pre-registered "persistence beats peak" hypothesis. This pattern of knowing but not saying generalises across model families including reasoning models. As a real-time monitor, probe-based interventions are sharply model and error-type dependent: branch-and-pick is net-positive across models and uniquely non-breaking on Llama-3.1-8B (4 rescued, 0 broken), while reprompt and replace-prior break correct traces at roughly the rate they rescue wrong ones. Probe-based monitoring is a necessary complement to verbalised confidence, but no single intervention dominates, and the deployable answer is model-aware, error-type-aware routing.

</details>


### [129] [NL2SHACL-Bench: A Benchmark Suite for Natural Language to SHACL Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07530)
*Yuchen Zhou,Niels Bobet,Maribel Acosta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了NL2SHACL-Bench，一个用于自然语言到SHACL翻译的基准测试套件，以解决领域专家难以编写SHACL形状的问题。通过该基准评估了四个先进的大语言模型（LLMs），结果显示当前模型虽能生成语法正确的SHACL，但在复杂逻辑和结构模式下仍难以生成语义等价的约束，表明该基准能有效衡量NL2SHACL技术进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前领域专家缺乏编写SHACL形状的技术能力，而自然语言到SHACL的自动转换（NL2SHACL）可降低使用门槛。但缺乏专用基准和有效的评估方法，限制了该方向的发展。

Method: 提出NL2SHACL-Bench基准测试套件，包含多样化的自然语言需求与对应的标准SHACL形状；使用该基准评估四个先进大语言模型在生成SHACL方面的表现，并采用超越字符串匹配的语义评估方法。

Result: 现有大语言模型能够生成语法正确的SHACL，但在处理复杂逻辑和结构模式时，生成的SHACL与标准形状在语义上存在差异，说明其仍需改进。

Conclusion: NL2SHACL-Bench为评估自然语言到SHACL转换提供了有意义且可靠的基准，有助于推动该领域的技术进步。

Abstract: SHACL is a core technology for validating the conformance of RDF knowledge graphs (KGs). Yet, authoring SHACL shapes requires technical expertise that most domain experts lack. Translating natural language requirements into SHACL (NL2SHACL) would lower this barrier. However, there is no dedicated benchmark for NL2SHACL, and evaluating generated shapes requires methods beyond string comparison, as semantically equivalent shapes can differ in serialisation and structure. To tackle these challenges, we present NL2SHACL-Bench, a benchmark suite for natural language to SHACL translation. Using NL2SHACL-Bench, we evaluate four state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs) for this task. Our results show that current LLMs are highly capable of generating syntactically valid SHACL, but still struggle to produce semantically equivalent constraints for complex logical and structural patterns. This indicates that NL2SHACL-Bench provides a meaningful basis for measuring advances in the NL2SHACL state of the art.

</details>


### [130] [Dynamic Coalition Formation and Communication Pricing in Skill-Based Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07532)
*Mojtaba Eslami*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于合作博弈的代理选择与通信框架，通过分离联盟级成本与代理激活成本，设计边际价值激活规则和贪婪路由机制，利用估计的夏普利值预测值得联系的代理。该方法在合成实验中表现优异，仅需激活约1.96个代理（共8个）即达到99.5%的最优效用，远优于全广播方式。理论分析表明其与子模最大化相关，并提供部分近似保证；同时建立夏普利-子模性夹逼界以衡量路由误差。该框架区别于夏普利定价、享乐型联盟形成及通信图剪枝，建议在真实多代理大模型基准上评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有现代代理型AI系统在多大语言模型代理间通信方面存在效率问题：固定通信路径或全广播模式导致令牌开销、延迟、冗余和错误传播随活跃代理数量增加而上升。需要更高效、动态且可扩展的通信机制。

Method: 将代理选择与通信建模为任务条件下的合作博弈，定义净效用函数 $U(C\mid x) = V(C\mid x) - \sum_{i\in C} c_i$；提出边际价值激活规则与贪婪路由器；引入每边通信成本扩展模型；使用估计的夏普利值进行事前与执行中预测；连接至子模最大化问题，推导理论近似边界。

Result: 在合成实验中，贪婪路由实现99.5%的最优效用，平均仅激活1.96/8个代理，显著优于全广播的38.8%激活率；性能对激活成本和冗余权重鲁棒，但在严重违反子模性或噪声较大的情况下下降至66%。理论证明了若干近似保证与误差夹逼关系。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于夏普利值与子模性理论的代理选择与通信框架，在效率与性能之间取得良好平衡，具备理论支撑与实证优势，适用于复杂多代理大模型系统，未来应推广至真实基准测试。

Abstract: Modern agentic AI systems combine multiple large language model agents with heterogeneous skills, yet most architectures either fix communication in advance or allow full broadcast. Both can be inefficient because token cost, latency, redundancy, and error propagation increase with the number of active agents and communication links. We model agent selection and communication as a cooperative game with task-conditioned net utility $U(C\mid x)=V(C\mid x)-\sum_{i\in C}c_i$, separating coalition-level costs from agent activation costs. We propose a marginal-value activation rule and greedy router, extend the model to optimize communication edges with per-edge costs, and use estimated Shapley values to predict which agents are worth contacting before and during execution. We connect the problem to submodular maximization and prove two limited guarantees: a curvature-refined bound for a monotone, cardinality-constrained special case, and a tight $1/2$-approximation, with a correction for signed objectives, for an unconstrained non-monotone case via double greedy. Neither guarantee applies directly to the main router, which remains a heuristic. We also prove a Shapley-submodularity sandwich bound linking the error of marginal-value routing to a per-agent diminishing-returns quantity. In synthetic experiments, greedy routing achieves $99.5%$ of brute-force-optimal utility while activating $1.96$ of $8$ agents on average, compared with $38.8%$ for full broadcast. Performance is robust to activation cost and redundancy weight but falls to $66%$ under strong violations of submodularity or noisy value estimates. We distinguish the framework from Shapley pricing, hedonic coalition formation, and communication-graph pruning, and propose evaluation on real multi-agent LLM benchmarks.

</details>


### [131] [MetaSpace: Metamorphic Testing for Spatial Cognition in Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07533)
*Gengyang Xu,Dongwei Xiao,Yiteng Peng,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MetaSpace框架，利用元测试原理和逻辑编程自动生成基于时空多模态状态的测试用例，以评估具身智能体的空间认知能力。通过预定义的元关系检测空间认知错误，实证显示该方法可发现大量错误，并引入空间认知（SC）分数量化性能，结果显示当前最先进模型表现远低于人类水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体评估依赖人工标注的视觉问答对或高层任务完成度指标，前者成本高且质量不一，后者可能掩盖关键缺陷如安全风险和低效行为；由于空间认知是执行具身任务的基础，亟需有效评估智能体的空间认知能力。

Method: 受软件工程中元测试启发，提出MetaSpace框架，利用真实执行轨迹中的时空多模态状态，基于逻辑规则与物理定律生成测试用例；将元关系编码为可执行的Prolog规则，违反这些规则即表明空间认知失败。

Result: 在三个具身场景中，MetaSpace成功检测到90,422个空间认知错误；所有SOTA MLLM驱动智能体的平均SC得分在0.44至0.52之间，显著低于人类基准（0.96）。

Conclusion: MetaSpace为评估具身智能体的空间认知提供了自动化、可靠且敏感的方法，揭示了当前主流模型在空间理解上的严重不足，强调了提升空间认知能力的重要性。

Abstract: An embodied agent is an intelligent entity that interacts with its environment through a physical body. Currently, the evaluation of embodied agents primarily relies on two paradigms: (1) manually annotated Visual Question Answering (VQA) pairs and (2) high-level task completion metrics, such as success in navigation or manipulation. The former is labor-intensive and subject to variability in annotation quality. The latter may obscure critical vulnerabilities, allowing agents to complete tasks through suboptimal means or safety violations, thereby concealing safety risks and inefficiencies. Given that spatial cognition is the cornerstone for executing embodied tasks, there is a pressing need to assess whether embodied agents possess robust spatial cognition during task execution.
  Inspired by metamorphic testing principles in software engineering, we propose MetaSpace, a novel framework designed to evaluate the spatial cognition of agents. By leveraging spatiotemporal multimodal states derived from real execution trajectories, MetaSpace automatically generates test cases based on predefined metamorphic relations (MRs) grounded in logical rules and physical laws. Crucially, we encode these MRs as executable rules in a logic programming language (Prolog). Violations of these relations indicate failures in spatial cognition. Our empirical evaluation across three embodied scenarios demonstrates that MetaSpace successfully detects 90,422 spatial cognition errors in state-of-the-art (SOTA) MLLM-driven agents. We introduce the Spatial Cognition (SC) score to quantify performance. Results indicate that all SOTA agents achieve average scores between 0.44 and 0.52, significantly lower than the human benchmark of 0.96.

</details>


### [132] [When LLM Agents Negotiate: Private Information and Dynamic Bargaining in Supply Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07538)
*Chen Liang,Fasheng Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估了九种来自OpenAI、Google和阿里巴巴的大型语言模型（LLM）在供应链谈判中的表现，通过9,840次LLM对LLM的谈判，以完美贝叶斯均衡为基准。结果显示：1）能力决定价值创造与可靠性，高阶模型能达成98.9%的协议，捕获95.4%的最优盈余，但平均需2.98轮谈判，导致21-34%的盈余损失；低阶模型有19.2%的概率接受非理性合同，而中高端模型则低于0.6%，表明自动化利润验证是关键控制机制。2）盈余分配具有关系性，供应商身份比能力更能预测盈余归属——自对战买家中，OpenAI、Google、Qwen分别获得40%、50%、70%的平均份额，且这一趋势在限制沟通和无贴现条件下依然成立；调换销售方会改变分配结果7-18个百分点，即使最强大的Qwen在跨品牌销售时也表现最弱，说明供应商选择是分配的关键决策。3）提示词是战略杠杆，委托机制使委托人经济耐心与代理人的提示策略耐心分离，这一部署选择是盈余分配的最强驱动因素（解释了90%的方差）。研究构建了一个基于均衡的审计框架，涵盖折现效率、分配特征与操作可靠性三个维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型代理从辅助决策转向自主采购，企业亟需了解委托谈判者是否创造价值、能否可预测地分配利益，并避免亏损合同。本研究旨在系统评估LLM在典型供应链谈判中的表现，揭示其在价值创造、盈余分配与可靠性方面的表现规律，为实际应用提供依据。

Method: 采用9,840次LLM对LLM的模拟谈判，基于一个已验证的完美贝叶斯均衡作为基准，比较九种主流大模型（OpenAI、Google、阿里）在私有需求信息下的数量-价格合约谈判表现。分析包括谈判成功率、谈判轮数、盈余捕获率、个体理性行为、供应商身份影响及提示词设计对分配结果的影响，使用统计建模识别关键驱动因素。

Result: 1）模型能力显著影响价值创造与可靠性：高阶模型达成98.9%的协议，捕获95.4%的第一最佳盈余，但平均耗时2.98轮，导致21-34%的盈余因延迟损失；低阶模型有19.2%的概率接受非理性合同，而中高端模型仅0.0-0.6%。2）盈余分配受供应商身份主导：自对战中，不同厂商模型的盈余获取比例差异显著（如Qwen达70%），且调换角色可改变分配7-18个百分点，说明供应商选择是关键分配变量。3）提示词设计是核心战略杠杆，可独立调节代理人的“策略耐心”，是盈余分配的首要驱动因素（解释90%方差）。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一个基于均衡的多维审计框架，用于评估大语言模型在谈判任务中的表现。结果表明，尽管高阶模型具备高效的价值创造能力，但其谈判效率仍低于理论最优；盈余分配不仅取决于模型能力，更受供应商身份和提示设计的深刻影响。因此，在实际部署中，应结合模型能力、供应商选择与提示工程进行综合权衡，以实现高效、可靠且公平的智能代理决策。

Abstract: As LLM agents move from decision support to autonomous procurement, firms need to know whether delegated negotiators create value, divide it predictably, and avoid money-losing contracts. We study this in a canonical supply chain bargaining problem: a buyer with private demand information negotiates a quantity-payment contract with an uninformed seller. We benchmark nine LLMs from OpenAI, Google, and Alibaba against a validated Perfect Bayesian Equilibrium across 9,840 LLM-to-LLM negotiations. First, capability governs value creation. Agents agree in 98.9% of negotiations and capture 95.4% of first-best surplus undiscounted, but average 2.98 rounds against the benchmark's 1.25, and this delay erodes 21-34% of surplus. Capability also governs reliability: baseline models accept individually irrational contracts in 19.2% of cases, versus 0.0-0.6% at mid-tier and flagship, making automated profit verification the binding guardrail below that threshold. Second, surplus capture is relational. Provider identity predicts who captures surplus better than capability rank: self-play buyer shares average 40% for OpenAI, 50% for Google, and 70% for Alibaba's Qwen, an ordering that survives restricted communication and no discounting. Reversing which provider sells moves the division by 7-18 percentage points, and the capable Qwen flagship is the weakest cross-family seller: vendor choice is a first-order distributional decision. Third, the prompt is a strategic lever. Delegation separates the principal's economic patience from the agent's prompted strategic patience, a free deployment choice that is the single strongest driver of surplus division (90% of explained variance). Together these establish an equilibrium-referenced audit of AI agents along three dimensions: discounted efficiency, distributional profile, and operational reliability.

</details>


### [133] [TREAT: Evaluating Access to Formal Knowledge across Equivalent Mathematical Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07540)
*Fateme Mazdarani,Carlos Toxtli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TREAT 是一个用于评估大语言模型在数学定理识别任务中对等价变换后表达式恢复已知定理身份能力的基准。该研究通过改变定理条件的数学形式（如残差方程、优化恒等式、集合关系等），而非仅改写文字，构建了一个包含737个定理身份和29,480个变换样本的数据集。实验表明，当前最佳模型仅能在60.73%的情况下正确识别定理，暴露出模型在表示鲁棒性上的不足。研究揭示了形式知识在等价表示变化下的脆弱性，并为需要稳定目标对象、显式等价关系与可审计评估的领域提供了重要测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统在处理灵活输入与正式对象之间的转换时，难以识别陌生表述是否对应已知形式对象。本研究聚焦于定理识别问题，旨在检验模型在等价数学表达变换下恢复原始定理身份的能力，以揭示其对形式知识的稳健性。

Method: 从网络抓取的定理页面中筛选具有可用数学表达的条目，提取标准定理条件，生成多种等价变换形式（如残差方程、优化恒等式、集合关系等），并记录变换假设与逆映射。最终构建了包含737个定理身份和29,480个变换样本的TREAT数据集。

Result: 在测试集中，最佳模型仅能正确检索定理身份60.73%的次数；其他系统表现出拒答、误检和输出错误等不同失败模式，说明模型在等价表示变化下对形式知识的保持能力较弱。

Conclusion: TREAT提供了一个可控的测试环境，用于评估大语言模型对形式知识的表示鲁棒性。研究结果表明，当前模型在面对等价数学表达变换时表现脆弱，提示需进一步提升形式知识的稳定性和可验证性，该基准对需要精确目标对象与可审计推理的领域具有广泛意义。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly operate between flexible input representations and formal objects used by downstream tools. A key challenge is recognizing when an unfamiliar formulation denotes a known formal object. We study this challenge through theorem recognition: given an equivalence-preserving transformation of a theorem condition, a model must recover the theorem identity associated with the standard statement. We introduce TREAT, a benchmark for evaluating whether large language models can recover known theorem identities from equivalence-preserving formula-level transformations. Rather than paraphrasing theorem text, TREAT changes the mathematical form of theorem conditions themselves, expressing known results through residual equations, witness statements, optimization identities, set relations, operator forms, and proof-intermediate characterizations.
