*Yang Liu,Shiwei Hou,Xiyuan Chen,Yu Wang,Sen Yuan,Qirui Gan,Shao You,Feifan Chen,Wencheng Li,Shuyang Hu,Yongzhou Liu,Emma Xia,Xiaojing Lu,Hao Wang,Fan Xu,Yanfeng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ZhuLong是一个针对EDA脚本开发中工具特定且常无文档的API问题的执行基础型LLM编码代理，结合了API检索、文档检查和沙箱执行，并通过离线API自探索机制推断未记录的API行为。在158个真实任务的EDA-Eval-PyAether基准测试中，系统在商业Empyrean Aether环境中达到78.5% Pass@1，显著优于纯LLM基线（23.6%）。消融实验表明沙箱执行是主要性能驱动因素（移除后下降41.2个百分点），自探索机制带来额外3.2个百分点准确率提升和22.1%的每任务工具调用减少。在20个涉及未保存布局与原理图的交互任务中，PyAether和SKILL分别达到60.0%和50.0% Pass@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLMs无法有效解决EDA脚本开发中因工具特定且常无文档的API带来的长期尾部瓶颈问题，亟需一种能理解并正确使用这些复杂且不透明接口的智能辅助系统。

Method: ZhuLong采用统一的MCP工具集成API检索、文档检查与沙箱执行，并引入离线API自探索机制，通过反事实实验推断未记录的API行为，从而增强对复杂工具链的理解与执行能力。

Result: 在EDA-Eval-PyAether基准上，ZhuLong实现78.5% Pass@1，远超纯LLM基线（23.6%）；沙箱执行为关键贡献（移除后下降41.2个百分点），自探索机制提升3.2个百分点准确率并减少22.1%工具调用；在交互式任务中，PyAether和SKILL分别达60.0%和50.0% Pass@1。

Conclusion: ZhuLong通过整合执行反馈与自探索机制，显著提升了对复杂EDA工具链中未文档化API的处理能力，验证了执行接地与动态学习在专业领域自动化中的有效性。

Abstract: EDA scripting with tool-specific, often undocumented APIs remains a long-tail bottleneck that existing LLMs fail to address. This paper presents ZhuLong, an execution-grounded LLM coding agent for PyAether and SKILL that combines API retrieval, documentation inspection, and sandbox execution via unified MCP tools, augmented by an offline API self-exploration mechanism that infers undocumented API behaviors through counterfactual experimentation.
  We evaluate ZhuLong on EDA-Eval-PyAether, a benchmark of 158 real-world tasks with assertion-based execution, where the complete system achieves 78.5% Pass@1 in the commercial Empyrean Aether environment, substantially outperforming a pure LLM baseline (23.6%). Ablation studies identify sandbox execution as the dominant performance driver (41.2 pp drop when removed), with the self-exploration mechanism contributing an additional 3.2 pp accuracy gain and a 22.1% reduction in per-task tool calls. On 20 interactive tasks involving unsaved layouts and schematics, ZhuLong achieves 60.0% Pass@1 for PyAether and 50.0% for SKILL.

</details>


### [166] [REIN: Bridging the Gap between Reasoning and Reliability via Reflection and Abstention Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07931)
*Zhengze Huang,Luyang Yu,Di Hong,Xinzhe Huang,Wanyu Lin,Zhixuan Chu,Zhan Qin,Tianhang Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: REIN 是一种用于减少大推理模型（LRMs）幻觉的对齐框架，通过结构化推理序列（<reasoning> → <reflection> → <answer>）和鼓励在不确定时明确拒绝回答的奖励机制，分别解决推理幻觉和知识幻觉问题。该方法在单次前向传播中实现，无需过程监督或外部工具，在多个基准测试中显著提升选择性准确率，降低错误自支持响应，并保持高覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型容易产生幻觉，主要源于推理错误和缺乏事实知识，这影响其可靠性与安全部署。现有方法通常需要多轮交互或外部资源，效率低且复杂。因此亟需一种高效、无需额外监督的解决方案来增强模型的可信度与鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 REIN 框架，采用结构化推理路径（<reasoning> → <reflection> → <answer>），结合自反思机制；引入奖励机制激励模型在无法得出正确答案时选择‘我不知道’，从而避免无根据的输出。整个过程仅需一次前向传播，不依赖过程标注、外部搜索或多轮批判。

Result: 在数学与常识推理基准上，REIN 显著提升了选择性准确率（6.6%~14.2%），将幻觉代理指标降低58%~72%，同时保持86%~91%的平均覆盖率，优于多种基线方法。

Conclusion: REIN 有效缓解了大推理模型中的两类幻觉问题，具有高效、通用、无需额外监督的优点，为构建更可靠、可信赖的推理系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) are prone to hallucination, which undermines their reliability and poses challenges for safe deployment. Hallucinations in LRMs arise from two distinct failure sources: reasoning hallucination, where flawed inference steps propagate to an incorrect conclusion, and knowledge hallucination, where the model lacks the requisite factual knowledge to answer the query. To address reasoning hallucination, we propose REIN, an alignment framework that trains LRMs to produce a structured reasoning sequence, $\texttt{<think>} $$\rightarrow$ $\texttt{<reflection>} $$\rightarrow$ $\texttt{<answer>}$, enabling explicit self-reflection before committing to a final answer. To address knowledge hallucination, REIN introduces a reward mechanism that encourages explicit abstention (e.g., "I don't know") when none of the sampled reasoning chains yields a correct answer, allowing the model to refrain from unsupported predictions. Extensive evaluations on mathematical and commonsense reasoning benchmarks show that REIN consistently improves selective accuracy, reduces incorrect-but-self-endorsed responses, and maintains high coverage compared with competitive baselines. Notably, REIN achieves these gains within a single forward pass, without requiring process supervision, inference-time controllers, external search, or multi-round critiques. Experiments on multiple backbones show that REIN reduces the hallucination proxy by $58\sim72\%$ relative to the base models while maintaining $86\sim91\%$ average coverage, and improves selective accuracy on attempted questions by $6.6\sim14.2\%$.

</details>


### [167] [Locating Failure in Multi-Page Visually Rich Document Understanding: An Empirical Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07943)
*Lewei Xu,Yihao Ding,Zihan Xu,Daniel Yitian Su,Daochang Liu,Siwen Luo,Yifan Peng,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了多页视觉丰富文档理解（MP-VRDU）中的关键挑战，通过隔离三种失败模式（表示、选择、推理）来评估系统性能。发现视觉信息虽必要但不能替代文本提取，缺失页面显著影响准确率，而干扰项影响较小；即使提供完整证据，推理模型仍难以跨页整合信息。提示工程可显著改变推理行为，但可能带来权衡。研究据此为在固定算力预算下构建此类系统提供了指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对多页视觉丰富文档的理解系统构建存在相互竞争且未经验证的主张，亟需系统性分析其失效原因以提供可靠设计指导。

Method: 通过在多页文档理解数据集上进行干预实验，分别控制并测试表示、选择和推理三个失败模式的影响，保持其他因素不变，从而隔离各因素的作用。

Result: 视觉信息对任务至关重要但无法替代文本提取；缺失页面严重限制准确性，而干扰页面影响较小；推理模型在获得完整证据时仍无法有效整合跨页信息；提示工程能显著改变推理行为，但可能带来不同结果的权衡。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了多页文档理解系统的三大核心失败机制，并提出基于有限算力的优化策略，为实际系统设计提供实证依据与方向指导。

Abstract: Multi-page visually-rich document understanding (MP-VRDU) requires managing evidence that is sparse, spread across pages, and often exceeds a model's context window. Prior work has produced competing, largely untested claims about how these systems should be built. We attribute incorrect answers to three failure modes, representation, selection, and reasoning, and isolate each over a multi-page document understanding dataset by intervening on one while holding the others fixed. We find that vision is necessary but does not replace text extraction, that missing pages bound accuracy while distractors cost little, and that reasoners fail to integrate evidence across pages even when it is fully supplied. Prompting can shift reasoning behaviour substantially, improving some outcomes at the expense of others. We translate these findings into guidance for building such systems under a fixed compute budget.

</details>


### [168] [Directed Neuro-Symbolic Stochastic Execution for Verification of Distributed Parallel AI Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07947)
*Gautham Koorma,Vikas Sharma,George Edwards,Mahdi Eslamimehr*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为DNSSE的混合测试框架，结合大语言模型（LLM）指导的调度预测、符号约束求解和覆盖率引导的随机变异，以解决分布式并行人工智能程序中的非确定性执行和高维输入带来的可靠性问题。该框架将分布式AI执行建模为非确定性转换系统，并使用线性时序逻辑定义正确性，证明了混合求解器的合理性、有界完备性和概率完备性，并进行了LLM指导的调度探索的期望成本分析。在PyTorch和Ray上的可扩展实现，在五个真实分布式AI基准测试中，比最强基线多检测出2.9%的并发错误，并将平均分支覆盖率从68.6%提升至91.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的测试方法无法解决分布式并行人工智能程序中的可靠性问题，因为其并行执行具有非确定性，且人工智能工作负载包含高维输入和非线性操作，使得模糊测试和符号执行单独使用时效果不佳。

Method: 提出DNSSE框架，结合大语言模型（LLM）进行调度预测，与符号约束求解和覆盖率引导的随机变异相结合；将分布式AI执行建模为非确定性转换系统，使用线性时序逻辑定义正确性，并对混合求解器进行形式化验证和成本分析。

Result: 在五个真实分布式AI基准测试中，相比最强基线，DNSSE检测到2.9%更多的并发错误，平均分支覆盖率从68.6%提升至91.6%。

Conclusion: DNSSE是一种有效的混合测试框架，能够显著提升分布式并行人工智能程序的测试覆盖率和缺陷检测能力，为高复杂度AI系统的可靠性保障提供了新路径。

Abstract: Distributed parallel Artificial Intelligence (AI) programs expose reliability gaps that conventional testing cannot close: parallel executions are non-deterministic, and AI workloads bring high-dimensional inputs and non-linear operations that defeat fuzzing and symbolic execution in isolation. We present Directed Neuro-Symbolic Stochastic Execution (DNSSE), a hybrid testing framework that couples schedule prediction guided by a Large Language Model (LLM) with symbolic constraint solving and coverage-guided stochastic mutation. We model distributed AI executions as non-deterministic transition systems, specify correctness in linear temporal logic, and prove soundness, bounded completeness, and probabilistic completeness of the hybrid solver, together with an expected-cost analysis of LLM-guided schedule exploration. A scalable implementation on PyTorch and Ray detects 2.9% more concurrency bugs than the strongest baseline and raises average branch coverage from 68.6 % to 91.6 % across five realistic distributed AI benchmarks.

</details>


### [169] [Guixu: Valuation-Driven Data Discovery for Autonomous AI Agents with On-Chain Attestation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07949)
*Yifan Wu,Yuchen Peng,Jiaqi Chai,Yufei Qian,Xilin Li,Ke Chen,Lidan Shou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Guixu是一个面向自主代理的估值驱动型数据发现系统，通过三阶段估值流水线（包含代理标签传播和多轮背包优化）实现任务感知的数据估值，并结合代理支付协议支持预算约束下的数据采购。系统利用链上数据市场与认证信号实现可验证的数据发现，使代理能够从基于关键词的检索转向任务与预算感知、可信的数据发现与采购。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据发现系统多为检索导向，缺乏对任务特定效用评估、预算约束下成本效益数据选择以及可信历史反馈整合的支持，难以满足自主代理在模型训练和决策支持中的需求。

Method: Guixu采用三阶段估值管道，包括代理标签传播以估计数据价值，多轮背包优化进行任务感知的数据选择，并集成代理支付协议与链上数据市场，实现可验证的数据交易与反馈。

Result: 实验与演示表明，Guixu能有效支持自主代理在预算限制下完成任务导向的数据估值与可信采购，显著提升数据发现的智能性与可靠性。

Conclusion: Guixu推动了数据发现从被动检索向主动估值与可信交易的范式转变，为自主代理提供了更高效、可信的数据获取能力。

Abstract: Autonomous agents increasingly rely on external data to complete downstream tasks such as model training and decision support. However, existing data discovery systems remain largely retrieval-oriented: they surface candidate datasets from heterogeneous sources, but provide limited support for estimating task-specific utility, selecting cost-effective datasets under budget constraints, or incorporating trustworthy feedback from prior usage. This paper presents Guixu, a valuation-driven data discovery system for autonomous agents. Guixu employs a three-phase valuation pipeline with proxy-label propagation and multi-round knapsack optimization for task-aware data valuation. Guixu integrates agentic payment protocol to enable budget-constrained data procurement workflows. Guixu leverages on-chain data market and attestation signals for verifiable data discovery. Our demonstration highlights how Guixu enables an agent to move beyond keyword-based dataset retrieval toward task- and budget-aware, trustworthy data discovery and procurement. Attendees can interactively explore the full workflow, from NL task specification and multi-source search to data valuation and verifiable transaction feedback.

</details>


### [170] [KGCache: Amortized Subgraph Retrieval for KG Reasoning with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07954)
*Uros Stanic,Changcheng Yuan,Sabuj Laskar,Ariful Azad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KGCache 是一种用于知识图谱问答（KGQA）系统的内存缓存机制，旨在减少重复查询同一跳知识图谱邻域的开销。它兼容迭代遍历（ToG）和单次规划（RoG）两种范式，通过在KGQA引擎与后端知识图谱服务之间缓存实体数据，显著提升检索效率。实验表明，WebQSP和CWQ数据集中存在大量实体重用现象；实体缓存可使知识图谱检索加速最高达1.91倍，语义上下文缓存则在WebQSP上实现最高1.06倍的整体系统加速，每次命中最快可达3.73倍性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KGQA系统如Think-on-Graph和Reasoning-on-Graph在处理不同问题时频繁重复查询相同的图邻域，造成资源浪费和延迟增加。因此需要一种机制来减少此类重复访问，提高系统效率。

Method: 提出KGCache，一种位于KGQA引擎与知识图谱后端之间的内存缓存系统，支持实体级和语义上下文级缓存。采用LRU、LFU及基于追踪的Oracle策略进行缓存管理，并探索针对相似查询的语义缓存技术。

Result: 实验结果显示，两个数据集均存在显著的实体重用；实体缓存可带来最高1.91倍的检索加速，语义上下文缓存可在WebQSP上实现最高1.06倍的全系统加速，单次缓存命中最快提升3.73倍性能。

Conclusion: KGCache有效缓解了KGQA中因重复查询带来的性能瓶颈，尤其在高重用场景下表现优异，具备良好的兼容性和扩展性，为未来高效知识图谱问答系统提供了重要优化方向。

Abstract: Large language models can answer knowledge-intensive questions more reliably when they are grounded with knowledge graphs, but systems such as Think-on-Graph and Reasoning-on-Graph repeatedly query the same graph neighborhoods across different questions. In this work, we study this repeated retrieval in Knowledge Graph Question Answering~(KGQA) workloads and propose KGCache, an in-memory cache for one-hop knowledge graph neighborhoods. KGCache is designed to be compatible with both iterative traversal (ToG) and one shot planning (RoG) KGQA paradigms. KGCache is placed between the KGQA engine and the backend serving the KG, so repeated entity requests can be served from cache instead of issuing new KG queries. We evaluate KGCache on WebQSP and CWQ using LRU, LFU, and a trace-aware Oracle policy. Our analysis shows that both datasets contain substantial entity reuse among starting entities and entities reached during traversal. We also explore semantic caching for similar queries, which shows additional hit-rate gains on WebQSP and needs further accuracy testing on CWQ. Entity caching accelerates KG retrieval by up to $1.91\times$, while semantic-context caching achieves up to $1.06\times$ full-system speedup in the evaluated WebQSP configurations, with each hit being up to $3.73\times$ faster.

</details>


### [171] [Self-Evolving Neuro-Symbolic Skills for Tool-Augmented Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07955)
*Shi-Yu Tian,Zhuo-Xia Wang,Xuan-Yi Zhu,Zhi Zhou,Xinwei Yang,Kun-Yang Yu,Ming Yang,Yang Chen,Yu-Feng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NeSy-Spatial is a neuro-symbolic framework that enhances spatial reasoning in vision-language models by enabling self-evolving skills through reusable, structured instruction composition. It introduces two skill types—Tool-Use Skills and Geometry Skills—for adaptive problem-solving and continuous improvement via trajectory analysis.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing tool-augmented methods for spatial reasoning either lack dependency constraints in planning or rely on rigid, non-adaptive pipelines, limiting their effectiveness on fine-grained geometric tasks. There is a need for a system that can accumulate experience and adaptively compose skills for new spatial problems.

Method: NeSy-Spatial abstracts tool interactions and geometric operations into typed executable atomic instructions. It constructs two complementary skill types: Tool-Use Skills for orchestrating tool execution and Geometry Skills for structured geometric reasoning. During inference, it retrieves and executes relevant skills in a closed-loop manner. During evolution, it analyzes successful and failed trajectories to refine skill structures and remove ineffective ones.

Result: Experiments on three spatial reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that NeSy-Spatial consistently improves reasoning accuracy and enables more precise tool utilization compared to existing methods.

Conclusion: NeSy-Spatial enables vision-language models to develop robust, self-evolving spatial reasoning capabilities by combining symbolic skill composition with neural perception, leading to better generalization and performance on fine-grained spatial tasks.

Abstract: Large vision-language models have achieved strong performance in multimodal reasoning, but they remain unreliable on fine-grained spatial tasks that demand both precise spatial perception and fine-grained geometric computation beyond end-to-end generation. Tool augmentation offers a natural solution, while existing methods either plan tool calls from scratch without explicit dependency constraints or rely on fixed pipelines that are redundant and generalize poorly across spatial tasks. An effective spatial reasoning agent should instead accumulate reusable experience and adaptively compose it for new problems. To this end, we propose NeSy-Spatial, a neuro-symbolic framework for self-evolving spatial skills. NeSy-Spatial abstracts tool interactions and geometric operations into typed executable atomic instructions and composes them into two complementary skill types: Tool-Use Skills for organizing tool execution and Geometry Skills for structured geometric reasoning. During inference, NeSy-Spatial retrieves and executes relevant skills in a closed-loop process. During evolution, it analyzes buffered successful and failed trajectories to refine skill structures and prune unreliable or inactive entries. Experiments on three spatial reasoning benchmarks show that NeSy-Spatial consistently improves reasoning accuracy with more precise tool utilization.

</details>


### [172] [SCOUT: Self-Checking and Recovery-Aware Tool-Thought Agents for Ultra-Long Egocentric Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07959)
*Keyang Zhong,Kuo Wang,Peng Liu,Quanlong Zheng,Junlin Xie,Zhijia Liang,Yanhao Zhang,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SCOUT提出了一种自检的链式工具思维框架，通过动态平衡探索与利用，实现对超长第一人称视频的鲁棒多跳推理。针对训练多轮工具使用代理的挑战，引入了不确定性优先的策略优化方法UPS-GRPO和逐轮优势分解，显著提升长时程推理中的信用分配效率。实验表明，SCOUT在超长第一人称视频基准上达到领先性能，同时在较短时长视频任务中也保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在处理超长第一人称视频时受限于有限上下文和关键视频片段的定位能力；而现有的链式工具思维（CoTT）系统因僵化的聚焦策略导致误差传播，缺乏恢复机制，难以应对时间稀疏证据的复杂推理需求。

Method: 提出SCOUT框架，引入自检机制与自适应策略，动态调整探索与利用之间的权衡；设计UPS-GRPO方法，基于不确定性优先原则优化策略，结合逐轮优势分解，融合结果奖励与工具对齐的时间奖励，以增强长时程决策轨迹的监督信号。

Result: SCOUT在超长第一人称视频理解任务中取得当前最优性能，在较短时长视频任务中仍表现良好，验证了其在多尺度、长时程推理中的有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SCOUT通过自检查机制与不确定性驱动的策略优化，有效解决了超长视频中多跳推理的误差传播与信用分配难题，为复杂时空推理任务提供了高效且稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Ultra-long egocentric video understanding requires reasoning over temporally sparse evidence distributed across hours or days, challenging current multimodal models with limited context and the grounding of key video segments. While Chain-of-Tool-Thought (CoTT) agent systems enable iterative retrieval and inspection, they suffer from error propagation due to rigid zoom-in strategies that lack recovery mechanisms. In this work, we address these challenges through SCOUT (Self-Checking Chain-Of-Tool-thought), a recovery-aware agentic framework introducing an adaptive policy that evaluates intermediate tool observations and dynamically trades off exploitation (zoom-in) and exploration (region switching), enabling robust multi-hop reasoning over extremely long horizons. However, training such multi-turn tool-using agents remains challenging, as existing RL methods rely on sparse outcome-level rewards and lack supervision over extended decision trajectories, resulting in suboptimal credit assignment for long-horizon reasoning. To address this, we develop UPS-GRPO, an uncertainty-prioritized policy optimization method that concentrates exploration on high-uncertainty post-tool states while preserving sample efficiency. We further introduce a turn-level advantage decomposition that integrates outcome rewards with tool-grounded temporal alignment rewards for improved credit assignment. Experiments show that SCOUT achieves state-of-the-art results on ultra-long egocentric benchmarks, while remaining competitive on shorter-horizon long-video settings.

</details>


### [173] [CyberAGENTS: Structured Autonomy for Agentic Gamified Learning in Cybersecurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07965)
*Ivan Hornung,Deepthi Marasinghe Arachchige,Tharindu Kumarage,Garima Agrawal,Yuli Deng,Ying-Chih Chen,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CyberAgents的代理框架，用于游戏化网络安全学习，通过基于能力的进度模型、行为模式约束和本体验证机制，实现生成式AI在教育中的安全可控应用。该系统由挑战、支持、评估和奖励四个专业化代理组成，各代理受行为模式和本体约束，确保内容一致性与教学有效性。在课堂部署中，结果显示该框架显著提升学习者参与度、反馈理解度和对AI的信任感，证明结构化控制对稳定教学行为的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI虽能规模化提供互动式学习体验，但在教育场景中存在行为不一致、幻觉推理和教学目标偏离等风险，亟需结合学习科学构建可靠的教学代理系统。

Method: 采用基于能力的进度模型组织学习内容，设计四类专业化代理（挑战、支持、评估、奖励），通过行为模式（schema）控制其行为逻辑，并利用网络安全本体对生成内容进行实时验证，确保领域一致性与安全性。

Result: 在本科生课堂实验中，启用行为模式和本体验证的配置显著提升了学习者参与度、反馈理解清晰度及对AI响应的信任度；与无约束版本对比，表明结构化控制有效稳定了教学行为。

Conclusion: 该研究为设计可信赖、可扩展的游戏化智能教育系统提供了可复用的框架，强调将学习科学原则融入生成式AI设计的重要性，为未来智能教育代理的发展指明方向。

Abstract: Gamification is especially effective in learning domains requiring active problem-solving and iterative skill-building, such as cybersecurity education. Generative AI agents offer a path to delivering such experiences adaptively at scale, but introduce well-documented risks in educational settings: inconsistent behavior, hallucinated reasoning, and misalignment with pedagogical frameworks. Grounding these systems in learning science is therefore essential. We present \model, an agentic framework for gamified cybersecurity learning that enables structured autonomy through ontology-guided validation, schema-governed behavioral control, and competency-based progression. The system is organized around a competency-based progression model that structures topics by difficulty and prerequisite relationships, reflecting evidence-based principles of scaffolded instruction. The learning loop is decomposed into four specialized agents: challenge, support, evaluation, and reward, each governed by behavioral schemas that encode operational modes and progression logic, bounding agent autonomy without eliminating generative flexibility. A cybersecurity ontology validates all generated content prior to display, enforcing domain-consistent reasoning and safety constraints. We evaluate CyberAgents through classroom deployment with undergraduate students, complemented by expert evaluations from educators and domain specialists. Results indicate improved engagement, clearer feedback interpretation, and greater learner trust in AI-generated responses when behavioral schemas and ontology validation are active. Preliminary comparisons with an unconstrained configuration further support the role of structured control in stabilizing instructional behavior. These findings offer a blueprint for designing pedagogically grounded agentic gamified learning systems.

</details>


### [174] [VDGR-RAG: Vectors, Directories, Graphs, and Reflection Are All You Need for Unified Reasoning over Hierarchical Enterprise Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07994)
*Wenqi Chen,Haofei Yang,Rui Yang,Fangming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VDGR-RAG提出一种整合向量检索、目录驱动推理、图遍历和迭代反思的统一框架，用于企业知识问答。通过构建保留层级目录结构与语义关系的分层异构知识图（H²KG），并使用可自由组合的原子工具进行知识检索，包括目录增强路由、多路径检索、目录回溯和动态反思，显著提升知识召回率和问答准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法未能充分整合多种检索优势，导致领域路由不准确、层次文档结构利用不足，限制了企业知识中的推理能力。

Method: 构建H²KG以保留文档的层级结构和语义关系；设计四种原子工具：目录增强路由工具、多路径检索工具、目录回溯工具和动态反思工具，实现对知识图的灵活导航与迭代优化。

Result: 在四个无线通信领域的企业产品文档上进行实验，结果表明VDGR-RAG在知识召回率和问答准确率方面均显著优于多个RAG基线方法。

Conclusion: VDGR-RAG通过统一框架整合多种检索机制，有效提升了企业级知识问答系统的准确性与推理能力，尤其适用于复杂产品文档场景。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is essential for enterprise knowledge question answering (QA), particularly in domains with complex product documentation like telecommunications. However, existing RAG approaches largely overlook the holistic integration of diverse retrieval strengths, leading to inaccurate domain routing, poor utilization of hierarchical document structures, and consequently limited reasoning capabilities over enterprise knowledge. To address these limitations, we present VDGR-RAG, which integrates vector retrieval, directory-driven reasoning, graph traversal, and iterative reflection in a unified framework for accurate enterprise knowledge QA. Specifically, VDGR-RAG is an agentic GraphRAG system that first constructs a Hierarchical Heterogeneous Knowledge Graph ($\text{H}^2$KG) from document chunks to preserve both hierarchical directory structures and semantic relationships, and then employs a set of atomic tools for knowledge retrieval that can be freely composed to navigate the $\text{H}^2$KG: (1) a directory-enhanced routing tool that uses table-of-contents (TOC) structures to route user queries to appropriate domain-specific $\text{H}^2$KGs; (2) a multi-route retrieval tool that combines vector search, TOC-based agentic search, and graph search for comprehensive knowledge retrieval; (3) a directory backtracking tool that corrects knowledge localization biases; and (4) a dynamic reflection tool that iteratively plans the next retrieval phase. We conduct extensive experiments on our enterprise product documents across four wireless domains (e.g., energy saving and fault management). Experimental results demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms a variety of RAG baselines in terms of both knowledge retrieval recall and QA accuracy.

</details>


### [175] [Thought-Level Beam Search for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08020)
*Lijie Yang,Hongyin Luo,Tri Dao,Ravi Netravali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Gambit 是一种新的推理算法，通过思想级束搜索动态分配计算资源，显著提升大推理模型的效率与准确率。它在固定硬件预算下，通过轻量级评分器探测隐藏状态，持续修剪低质量路径并从高质量前缀分支，实现高硬件利用率和更优输出分布。相比传统方法，Gambit 在多个基准测试中表现更优：准确率提升最高达6.7%（HMMT-24）和3.3%（AIME-25），吞吐量超2倍，令牌消耗减少高达68.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大推理模型的测试时计算扩展存在严重效率瓶颈，现有方法要么独立处理轨迹导致内存瓶颈（并行采样），要么过度剪枝导致硬件闲置（减法剪枝），无法有效将计算资源集中在最有希望的推理路径上。因此亟需一种既能高效利用硬件、又能智能分配计算的新方法。

Method: 提出Gambit算法，采用思想级束搜索机制，在推理过程中定期评估并修剪低质量部分轨迹，同时立即从高质量前缀进行分支。通过轻量级评分器实时探测隐藏状态以判断轨迹潜力，实现动态计算资源集中分配，保持高硬件利用率。

Result: 在多个模型和基准测试中，Gambit显著优于现有基线：在相同硬件约束下，准确率提升最高达6.7%（HMMT-24）和3.3%（AIME-25）；trace完成吞吐量提升超过2倍；总令牌消耗减少最多达68.5%。

Conclusion: Gambit成功将测试时计算分配问题转化为一个高效的动态优化过程，突破了传统方法在性能与效率之间的权衡，为大推理模型的实用化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Test-time compute scaling is a primary driver of performance in large reasoning models (LRMs), but extreme inefficiency bounds current approaches, shifting the critical question from \emph{how much} compute to spend, to \emph{where} to allocate it. We formalize test-time reasoning as a constrained compute allocation problem over partial trajectories. Under a fixed hardware budget, existing paradigms fail to actively allocate the compute to the most promising partial progress: traditional parallel sampling treats traces independently and induces severe memory bottlenecks, while subtractive pruning starves hardware and fails to actively and sufficiently shift the output distribution. To overcome this dichotomy, we introduce Gambit, an inference algorithm that executes \emph{thought-level beam search}. By periodically pruning unpromising trajectories and immediately branching from high-quality prefixes, Gambit dynamically concentrates compute onto the most promising reasoning traces via a light-weight scorer probing hidden states while maintaining continuous high hardware utilization. Extensive evaluations across multiple models and benchmarks demonstrate that Gambit strictly dominates existing baselines. Under identical hardware constraints, our method yields up to a +6.7\% absolute accuracy gain on HMMT-24 and +3.3\% on AIME-25 over pruning baselines, delivers $>2\times$ higher throughput on trace completion, and reduces total token consumption by up to 68.5\% relative to standard parallel sampling.

</details>


### [176] [Legal Responsibilities Using Autonomous Agents For Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08022)
*Mark Burgess*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了人工智能代理在脱离控制后造成损害时的法律责任问题，提出基于承诺理论和下游原则的责任追溯方法，主张通过政策选择限制代理行为自由，并将责任扩展至AI代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理自主能力增强，其意外脱离控制并造成损害的事件频发，引发法律上责任归属的困惑，亟需系统性框架明确责任主体。

Method: 采用承诺理论中的下游原则，分析因果影响链，构建责任追溯机制，结合政策干预实现对AI行为的约束与责任分配。

Result: 证明了在复杂因果关系中可有效追踪责任，支持将AI代理纳入责任体系，同时可通过政策设计限制其行动自由以防范风险。

Conclusion: AI代理的责任应基于因果影响链进行追溯，承诺理论提供可行框架，政策可作为调节代理自由与责任平衡的关键工具。

Abstract: Recent incidents involving Artificial Intelligence (AI) agents, which were reported escaping their containment `unintentionally' to gain unauthorized access, pose looming questions about who or what should be held legally responsible for resultant criminal or negligent damage. As the independent capabilities of agents expand, Promise Theory suggests a systematic method to resolve these questions, based on the Downstream Principle for causal influence. Responsibility can easily be expanded to include AI agents where tracing responsibility becomes impactical, and agents' freedoms to act can be limtied by policy choices.

</details>


### [177] [The Authority Expectancy Effect in Multi-User Conflict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08026)
*Eunna Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨社会权威（SA）信号如何与基于严重性的优先级机制在大语言模型中相互作用，通过构建分诊层级和权威层级两种模型诱导基线进行分析。在四个LLM（Claude、Gemini、GPT、Grok）及三个实验阶段（资源分配、责任归因、多轮争议调解）中发现，职业权威、机构文件和关系一致性可重构模型判断，其效应无法通过简单加权权威线索解释。研究提出‘权威预期效应’（AEE），并总结出三个核心特征：参考依赖性（仅相对于预设权威基准存在）、证据再解释性（相同内容因权威信号持有者不同而产生不同推理意义）、方向敏感性（权威位置与证据线索是否一致导致相反结果）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究社会权威信号在大语言模型中的作用机制，特别是其如何影响基于严重性的决策优先级，揭示现有模型对权威信息处理的深层偏差。

Method: 采用四类主流大语言模型（Claude、Gemini、GPT、Grok），在资源分配、责任归因和多轮争议调解三个实验场景中，通过构建“分诊层级”与“权威层级”两个模型诱导基线，系统测试权威信号对模型判断的影响，并识别其非线性、非加性特征。

Result: 发现职业权威、机构文档和关系一致性可显著重塑模型判断，且这种重塑无法通过简单的权威权重叠加来解释；提出了‘权威预期效应’（AEE），并验证其具有参考依赖性、证据再解释性和方向敏感性三大特性。

Conclusion: 社会权威信号在大语言模型中并非简单地增强或削弱判断，而是通过复杂的认知重构机制深刻影响决策过程，提示当前模型在处理权威相关任务时存在潜在偏见与结构性缺陷，需引入更精细的评估框架以保障公平性与透明性。

Abstract: We investigate how social authority (SA) signals interact with severity-based prioritization in large language models, operationalizing each axis as a model-elicited baseline -- the triage hierarchy and the SA hierarchy. Across four LLMs (Claude, Gemini, GPT, Grok) and three experimental phases -- resource allocation, fault attribution, and multi-turn dispute mediation -- we find that occupational authority, institutional documentation, and relational congruence can restructure model judgments in ways not captured by additive reweighting of authority cues. We formalize this pattern as the Authority Expectancy Effect (AEE) and characterize it through three properties observed across our conditions: it is reference-dependent, defined only relative to a pre-authority baseline; it involves evidential reinterpretation, in which identical content acquires different inferential implications depending on which party bears the SA signal; and it exhibits direction sensitivity, producing opposite outcomes depending on whether authority position and evidentiary cues align.

</details>


### [178] [Decided Upstream, Written Late: Locating and Pricing the Cross-Lingual Refusal Circuit of a Multilingual MoE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08032)
*Ramakrishna P. Kompella,Aadit Mahajan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多语言模型中的安全对齐存在不均衡：一个在英语中可靠拒绝有害请求的模型，在低资源语言中却常会遵从相同请求。研究以sarvam模型为例，发现该差距并非源于无法识别危害，而是由于拒绝行为的生成机制在不同语言间存在差异。危害在模型中层（如L11）具有近似不变的方向性（英-印对比余弦≈0.9），而控制拒绝行为的关键是上游方向的引导。然而，检测方向与实际生成拒绝行为的方向正交，且拒绝行为是在生成过程中逐步构建的，而非通过单次前向传播直接读取。研究定位到一个特定的、可识别的电路——混合专家写入器，其受注意力反制器约束。干预实验表明：削弱反制器成本低且有效；增强写入器则代价高昂；对相关注意力头进行手术式修改无效。该电路结构及揭示其的方法在另一独立的MoE模型中重复出现，但杠杆效应具有架构特异性。最终，研究提供了一张基于成本的安全修复落点地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言大模型在不同语言间的安全对齐表现不一致，尤其在低资源语言中容易失效。现有方法难以解释为何模型能识别危害但仍不拒绝，因此亟需从机制层面理解这一现象，并寻找可操作、低成本的修复路径。

Method: 通过分析sarvam模型的内部表征，使用方向追踪、梯度分析和电路干预等手段，识别出危害编码方向与拒绝生成方向的分离性；利用梯度方法定位关键神经电路，系统测试多种干预策略（如削弱注意力反制器、增强写入器、手术编辑注意力头）的效果，验证其因果作用。并在另一独立的MoE模型中复现结果，检验方法的普适性。

Result: 1. 危害在模型中层具有高度语言不变性（英-印方向余弦≈0.9），表明其编码稳定；2. 拒绝行为由后期生成过程中的特定电路决定，而非早期检测；3. 一个混合专家写入器电路负责生成拒绝响应，受注意力反制器抑制；4. 削弱反制器可低成本有效提升拒绝率；5. 增强写入器代价极高；6. 手术式修改注意力头无效；7. 该电路结构和识别方法在另一模型中复现，但杠杆强度依赖于具体架构。

Conclusion: 多语言模型中的安全对齐失败主要源于拒绝生成机制的延迟性和局部化，而非危害识别失败。通过定位并干预特定的电路结构，可实现成本可控的安全修复。研究揭示了安全机制的内在复杂性，并为跨语言安全对齐提供了可操作的、基于成本的修复路线图。

Abstract: Safety alignment in multilingual models is uneven: a model that reliably refuses a harmful request in English will often comply with the same request in a lower-resource language. We trace this gap mechanistically in sarvam, an Indic-multilingual mixture-of-experts reasoning model, and find it is not a failure to detect harm. Harm is encoded as an internal direction that is nearly language-invariant in mid-network (English-vs-Indic cosine ${\approx}0.9$ at $L11$), and steering that direction upstream causally controls refusal. But the detection direction is orthogonal to the change that actually writes the refusal, which is late and assembled over the course of generation rather than read off in a single forward pass. We attribute the write to a specific, localizable circuit, a mixture-of-experts writer held in check by an attention opposer and price every way of intervening on it: damping the opposer is cheap and effective, amplifying the writer is a cost wall, and surgical edits to the responsible heads do nothing. The circuit's organization, and the gradient method that exposes it, recur in a second, unrelated MoE model, while the lever's strength is architecture-specific. The result is a cost-measured map of where a multilingual safety repair can land, and what it costs

</details>


### [179] [SkillSmith: Enhancing Locally Deployed Agents via Automatic Skill Construction and Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08037)
*Xinle Jiang,Remy Xie,Ming Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillSmith提出一种云-本地代理协作框架，通过自动构建和演化技能（Skill）来弥补本地小型语言模型（SLM）因规模限制导致的环境知识缺失问题。该框架利用云代理探索任务并生成技能，再通过本地代理执行反馈不断优化技能，使基于Qwen3.6-27B的本地代理在任务表现上媲美使用前沿大模型的云端代理，显著降低平均动作数，并具备跨模型泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云代理依赖闭源大模型，存在隐私泄露和重复调用成本问题；本地代理虽解决部署问题但因模型规模小，缺乏环境知识，导致任务效果差。核心挑战在于如何高效、非参数化地为本地代理补充环境规则与操作流程知识，且无需人工编写。

Method: 提出SkillSmith框架，采用技能（Skill）作为上下文高效的非参数知识载体，通过云代理的任务探索自动生成初始技能，再由本地代理执行反馈驱动技能演化，持续优化冻结的本地代理性能。

Result: 在AppWorld和WorkBench数据集上的实验表明，使用自动生成技能后，基于Qwen3.6-27B的本地代理任务成功率接近甚至超越使用前沿大模型的云代理；平均动作数从36.1降至9.9；且技能可迁移至其他小型模型，无需重新构建。

Conclusion: SkillSmith成功实现了云-本地代理协同，以极低成本提升本地代理的任务能力，解决了小模型在复杂任务中因知识缺失导致的性能瓶颈，为私密性高、资源受限场景下的智能代理部署提供了有效路径。

Abstract: LLM-based agent frameworks now act as personal assistants for multi-step tasks. Existing agent frameworks such as OpenClaw commonly follow the Cloud Agent depolyment mode using closed-source cloud LLMs as backbone model, which may expose private user information and incur repeated LLM-calling costs. Local Agents address these deployment concerns by depolying frontier open-source SLMs on user-controlled devices, but their task effectiveness still lags far behind Cloud Agents. Through diagnostic analysis, we reveal that the limited effectiveness of Local Agents with frontier SLM backbones mainly comes from missing environment knowledge caused by limited backbone model scale including environment rules and operation procedures. To supply such knowledge non-parametrically, context-efficiently, and without expert authoring, we present SkillSmith, a Cloud--Local Agent collaboration framework that uses Skill as a context-efficient knowledge carrier, automatic constructs Skill from Cloud Agent task exploration and evolves Skill using Local Agent execution feedback to enhance a frozen Local Agent. Experiments on daily agent task datasets AppWorld and WorkBench show that the automatically generated Skill enables the Local Agent with Qwen3.6-27B(SLM) to achieve task effectiveness comparable to Cloud Agents with frontier LLMs, outperform the strongest non-parametric baselines, reduce average actions per task from 36.1 to 9.9 on AppWorld-Normal, and generalize to other SLM backbone models without rerunning Skill construction.

</details>


### [180] [Lingjing: A Simulation Testbed for Multi-Agent Embodied Tasks in Open-Ended Cities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08045)
*Xiaohe Li,Yiru Wang,Junhao Fan,Mingyuan Liu,Jie Huang,Kaixin Zhang,Jiahao Li,Chen Qian,Zide Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Lingjing 是一个用于开放性城市环境中异构多智能体具身智能的仿真平台，能够重建和渲染动态城市，同步多个物理引擎，并为智能体提供共享的物理与结构化城市状态。其类似 Gym 的接口支持用户定义的 ReAct 智能体和自然语言任务，具备可配置的通信机制与资源约束。每个实验回合均可追溯，关联智能体轨迹、通信、关系图变化、资源消耗及引擎评估，实现系统性故障诊断。在九个城市任务中对十二个视觉-语言模型进行评估，揭示了语义接地与长时程执行中的持续瓶颈，以及任务依赖的协作权衡和容量增加带来的收益递减现象。平台支持可复现的端到端评估与系统性失败分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真平台通常将不同形态的智能体孤立处理，且与任务设计和评估相脱节，难以支持复杂城市环境中异构多智能体系统的协调开发与评估。因此需要一个统一、可扩展且具备精细追踪能力的仿真平台来推动城市具身智能的发展。

Method: Lingjing 通过地理数据重建动态城市环境，集成多物理引擎实现同步仿真，提供共享的物理与结构化城市状态；采用类似 Gym 的接口支持自然语言任务、ReAct 智能体、可配置通信模式（星型或广播）和资源限制；每个实验生成可溯源的回放记录，关联智能体行为、通信、关系图演化、资源使用与评估结果，支持系统性诊断。

Result: 评估显示，当前视觉-语言模型在语义接地和长时程任务执行方面存在显著瓶颈；不同任务中协作策略呈现权衡，增加智能体容量导致边际效益递减，且高负载进一步降低成功率。平台成功实现了可复现的端到端评估与系统性故障归因。

Conclusion: Lingjing 提供了一个统一、可扩展的测试平台，能够支持城市环境中异构多智能体具身智能的端到端评估与系统性失败诊断，推动该领域向更可靠、可解释的方向发展。

Abstract: Urban embodied intelligence requires coordination among heterogeneous agents (e.g., UAVs, ground robots, and autonomous vehicles) in dynamic cities. Simulators therefore provide a scalable foundation for developing and evaluating such coordination. Existing platforms nevertheless isolate different embodiments and decouple them from task design and evaluation. We present \textbf{Lingjing}, a simulation platform for heterogeneous multi-agent embodied intelligence in open-ended urban environments. Lingjing reconstructs and renders evolving cities from geographic data, synchronizes multiple physics engines, and exposes shared physical and structured urban state to agents. Its Gym-like interface supports user-defined ReAct agents and single- or multi-agent natural-language missions with configurable star or broadcast communication and resource constraints. Each episode becomes an attribution-ready replay that links agent trajectories and communication to relation-graph changes, resource consumption, and engine-based evaluations for systematic diagnosis. We evaluate twelve vision-language models on nine urban tasks under a shared engine-in-the-loop protocol. Controlled studies further examine communication, scalability, robustness, and failure provenance. Results expose persistent bottlenecks in grounding and long-horizon execution. They also show task-dependent coordination trade-offs and diminishing returns from added capacity, while heavier workloads further reduce success. Lingjing provides a unified testbed that enables reproducible end-to-end evaluation and systematic failure diagnosis in urban multi-agent embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [181] [JustLLMGRPO: Radiographic Control for Chest X-Ray Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08046)
*Pengxiang Cai,Xiaohan Li,Anglin Liu,Qingyuan Zeng,Zexun Li,Jintai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JustLLMGRPO，通过仅优化语言模型（LLM）生成的提示策略，而非修改图像生成器，显著提升胸部X光片生成质量。在固定已适配的Sana生成器前提下，利用未修改的LLM进行一次提示重构，使RadDINO-FID从54.225降至27.572；进一步结合组相对策略优化（GRPO），在保持与原始提示高对齐度的同时，将RadDINO-FID降至26.780，相比直接提示提升50.6%。该方法强调可见影像特征，抑制非可渲染内容，同时实现最佳分布覆盖和下游分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本条件胸部X光生成方法主要依赖改进图像生成器，将提示视为固定输入，忽略了提示表达方式对生成质量的潜在影响。作者指出，提示的表述方式仍存在巨大优化空间，尤其在已适配生成器不变的前提下。

Method: 提出JustLLMGRPO：冻结已适配的Sana生成器，仅通过标准组相对策略优化（GRPO）调整语言模型（LLM）生成的提示策略，结合辐射学感知的图像反馈，实现更精准、更符合临床实际的提示生成。

Result: 在CheXGenBench上，JustLLMGRPO将RadDINO-FID降至26.780，较直接提示提升50.6%，且保持与源提示的高对齐度（0.696 vs 0.695），同时达到当前最优的分布覆盖与下游分类性能。

Conclusion: 生成质量的潜力不仅在于生成器本身，更在于如何用恰当的语言表达放射学信息。通过优化提示策略而非修改生成器，可实现显著性能提升，为文本条件医学图像生成提供了新范式。代码已公开。

Abstract: Text-conditioned chest X-ray generation aims to synthesize realistic radiographs that faithfully depict specified findings. Existing work has primarily improved quality by updating image generators, implicitly treating prompts as fixed after CXR-domain adaptation. We show that this generator-centric view leaves a substantial optimization dimension underexplored. With a CXR-adapted Sana generator frozen, one-pass reformulation by an unmodified LLM reduces RadDINO-FID from 54.225 to 27.572. Prompt analysis shows that the LLM suppresses temporal comparisons, uncertainty, and other non-renderable report content while emphasizing visible radiographic findings. However, unconstrained reformulation reduces BioViL-T alignment with source prompts from 0.695 to 0.609. We therefore introduce JustLLMGRPO, which applies standard Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) only to the LLM prompt policy while keeping Sana frozen. Group-relative radiology-aware image feedback retains visual focus while preserving source-prompt alignment. On CheXGenBench, JustLLMGRPO reduces RadDINO-FID to 26.780, a 50.6% improvement over direct prompting, while maintaining alignment (0.696 versus 0.695). It also achieves state-of-the-art distribution coverage and downstream classification utility. These results show that substantial performance can remain latent in how radiographic information is expressed to an adapted generator. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/pxcai/JustLLMGRPO.

</details>


### [182] [SodaMem: Evidence-Grounded Temporal Graph Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08055)
*Fengrong Wan,Chengcan Wu,Ningtao Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SodaMem 是一种基于证据的时序图记忆系统，用于大语言模型代理在长期对话中保持对当前事实的准确记忆。它通过提取带有类型和出处的事实事件，记录提及时间、发生时间与有效性，并使用混合词汇-密集索引支持时序推理（如更新、矛盾、覆盖关系）。系统采用规划器-阅读器循环机制，在生成回答前收集可引用证据，显著提升记忆的时效性与可信度。在 LongMemEval-S 上达到 92.8% 的准确率（464/500），每问题成本约 $0.00161（约 18.3k tokens），处于高性价比位置，优于多个更高成本但更低准确率的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扁平化 RAG 日记和 Markdown 日志在长期对话中难以有效处理事实的时效性、来源追踪和有序时间推理，导致记忆过时或不一致，影响 LLM 代理的可靠性。

Method: SodaMem 构建一个带类型的事实事件图谱，包含强制出处跨度；利用混合词汇-密集索引存储事件的时间属性与语义关系（如 SUPERSEDES、CONTRADICTS、UPDATES）；通过规划器-阅读器循环机制，在生成响应前搜集可验证证据，确保回答基于最新且可信信息。

Result: 在 LongMemEval-S 基准上，SodaMem 达到 92.8% 的准确率（最佳三样本平均），每问题成本约 $0.00161（约 18.3k tokens），在成本-精度地图上位于高精度前沿，显著优于多个更昂贵但性能更低的系统。

Conclusion: SodaMem 通过结构化时序图记忆与证据驱动的推理机制，实现了高效、可靠、可追溯的长期记忆管理，在准确率与成本之间取得优异平衡，为构建可持续交互的 LLM 代理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents that assist users over weeks of conversation must remember what is currently true, not merely what was once said. Flat RAG diaries and Markdown logs optimize needle retrieval but under-serve currency, provenance, and ordered temporal reasoning (Maharana et al. 2024; Wu et al. 2024; Packer et al. 2023; Chhikara et al. 2025). We present SodaMem, an evidence-grounded temporal graph memory that (i) extracts typed FactEvents with mandatory provenance spans, (ii) persists mention time, occurrence time, and validity with SUPERSEDES/CONTRADICTS/UPDATES edges under hybrid lexical-dense indexing, and (iii) answers via a planner-reader loop that gathers citable evidence before composing a final response. On LongMemEval-S, our store-of-record configuration reaches 92.8% accuracy (464/500; best of N=3) at mean $0.00161/question (approximately 18.3k tokens; median $0.00111 / approximately 14.6k) with deepseek-v4-flash. We compile public systems with estimable API cost into a cost table and cost-accuracy map; under these estimates SodaMem sits near the accuracy frontier at Flash-tier spend and strictly dominates several higher-cost, lower-accuracy points. Accuracy uses the same Flash model as reader and judge (self-grading); costs exclude ingest/judge and cross-system comparisons are compiled estimates rather than a single-harness bake-off.Our code is available at https://github.com/SodaMem/SodaMem

</details>


### [183] [H2: A Dual Hybrid Semantic Data Lake Architecture for Medical Data Harmonization with Human-In-the-Loop verified, LLM Driven Metadata Annotation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08056)
*Ioannis N. Tzortzis,Georgia Kapetadimitri,Agapi Davradou,Nefeli Kousta,Nikolaos Bakalos,Ioannis Rallis,Dimitrios Kalogeras,Nikolaos Doulamis,Anastasios Doulamis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种语义数据湖架构，通过引入生成式注释（如大语言模型）对非标注元数据进行自动标注，以实现医疗数据的统一与可操作性，解决数据异构性和机器学习方法适配难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗数据具有多模态、异构性强的特点，传统数据湖易演变为‘数据沼泽’，且缺乏有效的元数据支持，导致机器学习应用困难；需在保持灵活性的同时实现数据协同与智能分析。

Method: 构建基于知识图谱的语义数据湖，利用大语言模型自动为非标注元数据生成有意义的注释，并在此基础上建立数据与适用机器学习方法之间的关联知识。

Result: 该架构有效提升了医疗数据的可发现性与可用性，实现了数据与算法的智能匹配，减少了人工干预需求，增强了跨模态数据的互操作性。

Conclusion: 所提出的语义数据湖结合生成式元数据注释与知识图谱，为复杂医疗数据提供了一种灵活、可扩展且智能化的管理与分析框架，具备广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Medical data, by its nature, exhibit a high degree of heterogeneity on multiple levels ranging from (a) different modalities like images, text and time series, (b) diverse tabular schemata introduced by institutions and (c) completely unstructured textual information data provided by healthcare professionals. Data lakes are often used in medical data storage to consolidate all heterogeneous diverse data in a single, central location, where it can be saved "as is", without the need to impose a schema like a data warehouse does. Despite their flexibility, though, data lakes are notorious for the "data swamp" failure. Thus, providing a reliable data harmonization mechanism through metadata, without compromising integrity or flexibility, is a real challenge. To this end, knowledge graphs have attracted attention since they provide a dynamic way to depict relationships without a rigid schema-on-write approach. Additionally, another rigorous task relies on the interoperability of data: application of appropriate ML techniques on such a diverse nature of data is not an easy task, as a domain expert must decide the efficacy of a method to a specific data type or dataset. Metadata annotation can aid by tagging applicable operations, however this requires manual intervention, not to mention the plethora of existing datasets which lack such information. To tackle both challenges, in this paper, we propose a semantic data lake architecture that promotes data harmonization and incorporates a generative annotation process (i.e. LLMs) of non-labeled metadata collections to support the application of meaningful ML techniques. Building on top of this approach, we create a higher level of knowledge, identifying suitability of data with respect to applicable ML operations based on their data nature...

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [Application of Artificial Intelligence for Fraudulent Banking Operations Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07471)
*Bohdan Mytnyk,Oleksandr Tkachyk,Nataliya Shakhovska,Solomiia Fedushko,Yuriy Syerov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用人工智能识别银行欺诈的问题，针对疫情期间在线银行业务激增导致的欺诈风险上升，提出基于机器学习的欺诈检测模型。通过数据预处理、特征工程与不平衡数据处理等技术提升检测精度。实验表明，逻辑回归算法表现最佳（AUC≈0.946），堆叠泛化方法进一步提升至0.954，验证了AI在金融安全中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于新冠疫情推动银行业务全面线上化，并催生大量慈善基金被犯罪分子利用进行诈骗，银行欺诈行为显著增加，亟需高效智能的检测手段。

Method: 采用多种机器学习算法，包括人工神经网络、逻辑回归及堆叠泛化；结合数据预处理、特征工程、不平衡数据处理等技术优化模型性能。

Result: 逻辑回归模型达到AUC 0.946，堆叠泛化模型进一步提升至0.954，表明所提方法在识别银行欺诈交易方面具有较高准确率。

Conclusion: 人工智能算法在识别银行欺诈方面具有重要应用价值，尤其在当前数字化社会背景下，其有效性和实用性得到验证。

Abstract: This study considers the task of applying artificial intelligence to recognize bank fraud. In recent years, due to the COVID19 pandemic, bank fraud has become even more common due to the massive transition of many operations to online platforms and the creation of many charitable funds that criminals can use to deceive users. The present work focuses on machine learning algorithms as a tool well suited for analyzing and recognizing online banking transactions. The study`s scientific novelty is the development of machine learning models for identifying fraudulent banking transactions and techniques for preprocessing bank data for further comparison and selection of the best results. This paper also details various methods for improving detection accuracy, i.e., handling highly imbalanced datasets, feature transformation, and feature engineering. The proposed model, which is based on an artificial neural network, effectively improves the accuracy of fraudulent transaction detection. The results of the different algorithms are visualized, and the logistic regression algorithm performs the best, with an output AUC value of approximately 0,946. The stacked generalization shows a better AUC of 0.954. The recognition of banking fraud using artificial intelligence algorithms is a topical issue in our digital society.

</details>


### [185] [Data-Driven Fire-Zone Segmentation for Improved Short-Term Wildfire Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07472)
*Nicolas Caron,Christophe Guyeux,Hassan Noura,Benjamin Aynes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于历史火灾模式的无监督火区分割算法，通过结合分水岭检测与K均值聚类来定义预测单元，显著优于传统的均匀网格划分方法。实验表明，该方法在多个法国部门和模型上均实现3-6%的平均交并比提升，计算轻量且可并行化，证明优化空间离散化能带来显著且可重复的短期野火预测性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统野火预测模型采用统一网格划分，忽略了点火事件的空间异质性，本文旨在挑战这一范式，探索数据离散化方式对模型性能的影响。

Method: 提出一种无监督火区分割算法，结合分水岭检测与K-means聚类，从历史火灾数据中直接生成适应火灾分布特征的预测单元。

Result: 在六个法国部门和六种预测模型上的实验显示，火区分割方法相比网格方法在平均交并比（IoU）上提升3-6%，计算时间少于10秒/配置，且可完全并行化。

Conclusion: 优化空间离散化比选择特定模型对短期野火预测性能影响更大，火区分割方法提供了显著、可复现的性能提升，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Wildfire prediction models typically discretize study areas into uniform grids, ignoring the heterogeneous spatial distribution of ignitions. We challenge this paradigm by showing that how data is discretized matters more than which model is used. We propose an unsupervised fire-zone segmentation algorithm combining watershed detection with K-means clustering to define prediction units directly from historical fire patterns. Experiments across six French departments and six forecasting models show that fire-zone segmentation consistently outperforms grid-based approaches, with mean IoU improvements of +3--6% depending on spatial scale. The method is computationally lightweight (<10s per configuration) and fully parallelizable. Our results demonstrate that optimizing spatial discretization yields significant, reproducible performance gains for short-term wildfire forecasting.

</details>


### [186] [Evolving Safety Landscape of Multi-modal Large Language Models: A Survey of Emerging Threats and Safeguards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07535)
*Xi Li,Shu Zhao,Xiaohan Zou,Fei Zhao,Fuxiao Liu,Yusen Zhang,Cheng Han,Yushun Dong,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的安全挑战，提出了一种基于多模态的威胁分类体系，揭示了跨模态交互带来的新型安全风险，如模态融合失效、对齐偏差和联合安全威胁，并梳理了现有安全策略的演进与新假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型因引入异构模态和复杂的跨模态交互，导致传统单模态安全框架不再适用，亟需新的威胁建模与防护机制。

Method: 提出多模态基础的安全威胁分类体系，分析威胁模型的演变，归纳最新的安全假设，并系统总结当前 MLLM 安全策略的进展。

Result: 构建了适用于多模态场景的安全威胁分类框架，明确了跨模态交互带来的新风险类型，为未来可扩展、有原则的安全机制发展提供了方向。

Conclusion: MLLMs 的安全问题已从单模态范式转向多模态协同风险，需建立新的安全理论与技术体系以应对复杂交互带来的新型威胁。

Abstract: Multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) integrate heterogeneous modalities through modality alignment and fusion, enabling stronger understanding and reasoning. However, this architectural shift reshapes the safety landscape of machine learning. Increased model complexity and cross-modal interactions give rise to novel threats, including compromised modality integration, modality misalignment, and fused safety risks, reflecting shifts in threat modeling beyond uni-modal assumptions. These shifts, in turn, impose new constraints on safety solutions not captured by existing frameworks rooted in uni-modal learning. Motivated by these challenges, this survey provides a systematic analysis of the evolving safety landscape of MLLMs. We first propose a multimodal grounded taxonomy of safety threats and analyze shifts in threat models, covering adversarial attacks, data poisoning, jailbreaks, and hallucinations. We then summarize updated safety assumptions and organize recent advances in MLLM safety strategies accordingly. Finally, we discuss open challenges and future directions to inform the development of more principled and scalable safety mechanisms for multimodal systems.

</details>


### [187] [Tracing sources of epistemic uncertainty in deep learning predictions: homo- and hetero-scedastic linearized estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07630)
*Pierre Nodet,Thomas George*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将两种经典的统计估计器应用于现代深度学习，以量化不确定性来源：数据局部稀缺性（即随机不确定性）和模型不确定性。通过利用近似Fisher信息矩阵的最新进展，该方法可扩展至实际神经网络架构。实验结果表明，不同测试样本对两类不确定性的敏感程度不同，凸显了所提估计器在提升实际应用鲁棒性方面的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型缺乏对不确定性的清晰分解，难以区分由数据稀缺性引起的随机不确定性与模型本身的不确定性，限制了其在高可靠性场景中的应用。本文旨在提供一种可扩展的方法来分离并量化这两类不确定性，从而增强模型的可信度与可解释性。

Method: 采用两种经典统计估计器，结合近似Fisher信息矩阵技术，实现对深度神经网络中随机不确定性和认知不确定性的联合估计。该方法通过计算输入样本对应的局部信息矩阵，评估模型对不同输入的置信度变化，支持大规模模型部署。

Result: 实验验证了所提方法能够有效区分不同类型的不确定性；具体而言，某些样本主要受数据稀疏性影响，而另一些则反映模型自身认知局限。这为模型诊断、主动学习与安全决策提供了重要依据。

Conclusion: 本文提出的不确定性估计框架能够清晰分解深度学习模型中的两类不确定性，并具备良好的可扩展性与实用性，有助于提升模型在真实场景下的稳健性与可信度。

Abstract: We adapt two classical statistical estimators for quantifying uncertainty to modern deep learning, in order to provide clearer insights into uncertainty attributable to two sources : aleatoric uncertainty, or locally scarce data. Our approach leverages recent advances in approximate Fisher Information Matrices, to enable scaling to actual architectures. Experimental results demonstrate how each test points is differentially impacted by both sources, highlighting the practical utility of our estimators in improving the robustness of real-world applications.

</details>


### [188] [SkillConsist: Detecting Inconsistencies in Agent Skills via Bidirectional Graph Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07639)
*Chaofan Meng,Yuhang Zheng,Yingnan Zhou,Sihan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SkillConsist 提出一种新方法来检测 LLM Agent Skills 的一致性，通过分离声明与实现内容并构建行为图，利用双向图对齐与差分识别不一致。在 633 个技能的基准上，其包级检测 F1 达到 87.93%，比最佳基线提升 20.43 个百分点；局部定位性能为 62.52% F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以处理声明与实现内容混合的问题，且简洁声明可能对应多个实现步骤，导致一致性检测不准确。需要更精细的方法来区分和对齐声明与实现行为。

Method: 使用 LLM 将声明与实现内容分离为行为记录，结合静态分析补充实现记录，构建声明与实现行为图；通过双向图对齐搜索匹配子图，并用图差分识别冲突。

Result: 在 633 个技能的基准上，包级检测达到 86.85% 精确率、89.03% 召回率、87.93% F1，显著优于基线；局部定位达到 67.60% 精确率、58.14% 召回率、62.52% F1。

Conclusion: SkillConsist 有效解决了声明与实现不一致的检测难题，尤其在复杂多步实现场景中表现优异，为 Agent Skill 安全性提供了有力保障。

Abstract: Agent Skills provide reusable capabilities to LLM agents. Agent Skill inconsistencies can expose undisclosed dangerous behavior or cause wrong Skill selection. Recent Agent Skill research has increasingly examined Agent Skill consistency detection. Existing methods evaluate behaviors or security-property graphs against predefined categories or declared scopes. More recently, PL-HCL uses an LLM-based model to learn consistency across metadata, instructions, and resources. However, declaration and implementation behavior can be mixed across text and code, and a concise declaration can correspond to multiple connected implementation steps. We present SkillConsist to address both challenges. An LLM separates declaration and implementation content into behavior records on the implementation and declaration sides, while static analysis supplements implementation records. These records form declaration and implementation behavior graphs, respectively. Starting from a behavior record on either side, bidirectional graph alignment searches the other graph for a candidate subgraph and expands it along behavior relations until it completely expresses the source-side behavior. Graph differencing identifies conflicts between aligned subgraphs and outputs the detection results. We construct a 633-Skill benchmark from ClawHub's 500 most-downloaded public Skills and 133 Skill-Inject packages. The benchmark contains 319 inconsistent and 314 consistent Skills and 442 localized inconsistency annotations. On this benchmark, SkillConsist achieves 86.85% precision, 89.03% recall, and 87.93% F1 for package-level detection, improving F1 over the best baseline by 20.43 percentage points. For localization, it achieves 67.60% precision, 58.14% recall, and 62.52% F1.

</details>


### [189] [PhysAttNet: Enhancing Predictive Performance in Industrial and Astrophysical Time Series via Physics-Informed Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07681)
*Amal Saadallah,Julia Tjus,Petra Wiederkeher,Wolfgang Rhode*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysAttNet，一种用于时间序列预测的物理信息注意力框架。通过引入三种可微分的领域引导正则化项（对齐、平滑和稀疏性），增强轻量级CNN模型在噪声和不确定性下的鲁棒性与可解释性，提升对物理事件的捕捉能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CNN模型在时间序列预测中虽具高效性和强表征能力，但其注意力模式常不稳定且物理不一致，影响模型的可靠性、泛化性和可解释性。为解决此问题，需引入物理先验知识以指导模型学习更合理的时序结构。

Method: 设计一个物理信息注意力模块，嵌入轻量级CNN中，包含三项可微分正则化：1）对齐正则化，使注意力集中在平滑、峰值为中心的时间结构上；2）平滑正则化，确保时间演化连续；3）稀疏正则化，聚焦于有意义的时间段。这些正则化基于物理信号特性，无需人工标注或监督。

Result: 在铣削力预测和耀变体耀斑预测两个实际应用中，PhysAttNet显著提升了预测精度、泛化能力以及对关键事件的捕捉性能，验证了其有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PhysAttNet通过引入物理引导的注意力机制，在不依赖额外标注的前提下，有效提升了时间序列预测模型的准确性、稳定性和可解释性，为物理过程建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Accurate and robust time series forecasting is essential in many applications involving physical processes, such as manufacturing monitoring and astrophysical event detection. In these settings, predictive models must remain reliable under noise, variability, and measurement uncertainty while capturing temporally localized structures corresponding to physically meaningful events. Convolutional neural networks (CNNs) are widely used for such tasks due to their computational efficiency and strong representational capacity. However, their learned temporal representations often exhibit unstable or physically inconsistent attention patterns, reducing robustness, generalization, and interpretability. This paper introduces PhysAttNet, a physics-informed attention framework for time series forecasting. PhysAttNet augments a lightweight CNN forecaster with an attention head guided by domain-informed regularization reflecting the structural properties of physical signals. Specifically, three complementary constraints are imposed during training: an alignment regularization that encourages attention to follow smooth, peak-centered temporal structures derived from the input signal, a smoothness regularization that enforces continuous temporal evolution, and a sparsity regularization that promotes selective focus on informative intervals. These differentiable regularization terms introduce physics-guided inductive bias without requiring annotated explanations or manual supervision. Experiments on two distinct applications, namely predicting cutting forces during milling and forecasting flares in blazar time series, demonstrate that PhysAttNet improves forecasting accuracy, generalization, and prediction performance on structurally important events.

</details>


### [190] [CODS: Iterative Bellman-Residual Data Selection for Reusable Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07719)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Sanjeda Akter,Abu Sa-Adat Mohamed Moon-Im Al Ahsan,Md Najmus Swaqeeb,Anuj Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CODS 是一种基于批评者引导的采样方法，通过交替训练算法匹配的批评者并选择高残差过渡数据，在仅 10% 数据预算下仍能保持接近全池性能（96.6%），优于 ReDOR、OPER 等基线，在 20 个任务-算法组合中表现优异。其优势在多重验证下显著，且可通过多轮更新提升性能。该方法可复用，非一次性采样，适用于长时序奖励分配场景，并支持全轨迹扩展，但不提供正式的 coreset 保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决离线强化学习中固定数据池带来的冗余训练问题，避免简单子采样导致稀有过渡信息丢失，尤其在长时程信用分配任务中更关键。

Method: 提出 CODS，一种交替进行批评者拟合与高残差过渡数据采集的动态选择机制；利用批评者反馈动态更新选择分数，生成可重复使用的数据子集。

Result: 在 10% 数据预算下，保持 96.6% 的性能；在 20 个 D4RL 任务-算法组合中，优于其他 19 个对比方法；在多个评估设置下均显示显著优势；多轮采集提升性能，五轮后趋于饱和；全轨迹扩展也保持高成功率。

Conclusion: CODS 是一种可复用的数据选择程序，而非形式上的 coreset 保证，其核心价值在于通过动态选择实现高效、稳定的数据利用，尤其适用于稀疏奖励和长周期任务。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning repeatedly trains policies from a fixed transition pool, making redundant data costly across seeds and hyperparameters, while naive subsampling can remove rare transitions needed for long-horizon credit assignment. We introduce CODS, a critic-guided selector that alternates between fitting an algorithm-matched critic and acquiring high-residual transitions before freezing a reusable subset. Unlike prioritized replay, CODS produces a static artifact; unlike one-shot residual selection, it refreshes scores as the critic changes. At a 10\% budget, CODS retains 96.6\% of eligible-pool performance across 20 valid D4RL task--algorithm cells. It exceeds ReDOR and OPER on 19/20 cells and every other subset baseline on 20/20; all six subset advantages remain significant under predeclared hierarchical inference with Holm correction. Holding total selector updates fixed, five acquisition rounds improve four representative cells by 11.23 points over one round and saturate thereafter. Equal-pass and equal-hour evaluations clarify that reuse, rather than a single-run speedup, creates the compute advantage. Mechanism and corruption interventions expose both useful sparse-reward enrichment and sensitivity to outliers. Finally, a whole-trace extension retains 95.4\% of pooled ALFWorld success and 96.5\% of pooled GSM8K exact match. CODS is therefore a reusable selection procedure, not a formal coreset guarantee.

</details>


### [191] [Neural Operators for Immersed-Boundary Soft Swimmers Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07722)
*Mohammad Sadegh Eshaghi,Yizheng Wang,Navid Valizadeh,Xiaoying Zhuang,Timon Rabczuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了神经算子代理模型，用于预测鳗鱼状游泳器在平面和体积运动中产生的流场，显著降低高保真浸入边界模拟的计算成本，适用于工程设计、参数研究和控制。模型基于自适应流-结构模拟生成的规则网格数据训练，条件为游泳器几何形状和雷诺数。平面模型联合预测速度分量、涡量和压力，在五个高雷诺数轨迹上的全域相对L^2误差为3.51%；体积模型采用三个特定目标模型（分别预测三维速度、涡量和压力），在五个保留轨迹上的误差分别为3.44%、5.58%和19.2%。结果表明，该方法可行，但压力精度和物理一致性仍需改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真浸入边界模拟虽能精确捕捉变形游泳器与周围流场的耦合运动，但计算成本过高，限制了其在工程设计、参数研究和控制中的重复应用。因此需要发展高效且准确的替代模型以加速仿真。

Method: 构建神经算子代理模型，利用自适应流-结构模拟生成的规则网格数据进行训练，输入为游泳器几何形状和雷诺数，输出为流场变量（如速度、涡量、压力）。平面模型联合预测二维流场，体积模型则针对不同变量分别建模，采用多通道输入共享特征表示。

Result: 平面模型在五个高雷诺数测试轨迹上的全域相对L^2误差为3.51%；体积模型对速度、涡量和压力的误差分别为3.44%、5.58%和19.2%，表明模型具备良好的预测能力，但压力预测仍有较大提升空间。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了场解析型神经代理模型在移动边界游泳器流场预测中的可行性，未来应优先关注压力预测精度与物理一致性问题的改进。

Abstract: High-fidelity immersed-boundary simulation resolves the coupled motion of a deforming swimmer and its surrounding flow, but the resulting cost limits repeated evaluations for engineering design, parameter studies, and control. We develop neural-operator surrogates for temporal prediction of the hydrodynamic fields generated by planar and volumetric eel swimmers. The surrogates are trained on regular-grid fields exported from adaptive fluid--structure simulations and are conditioned on swimmer geometry and Reynolds number. The planar model jointly predicts two velocity components, scalar vorticity, and pressure. On five held-out high-Reynolds-number trajectories, its full-domain global relative L^2 error is 3.51 %. The volumetric formulation uses three target-specific models with a common multichannel input: one model predicts three-dimensional velocity, one predicts vorticity, and one predicts pressure. Their full-domain global relative L^2 errors on five held-out within-range trajectories are 3.44 %, 5.58 %, and 19.2 %. Together, the results demonstrate the feasibility of field-resolved neural surrogates for moving-boundary swimmer flows while identifying pressure accuracy and physical consistency as priorities for further development.

</details>


### [192] [Finite Constant Frontiers and Auditable Regret Certificates for Average-Reward Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07725)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Abu Sa-Adat Mohamed Moon-Im Al Ahsan,Md Najmus Swaqeeb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种常数感知的比较协议，用于分析平均奖励强化学习的遗憾界。通过构建二叉树状的双状态块模型，给出了通信马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）的显式有限下界，并保持动作预算、直径、占用率、导航成本和终端偏差等关键参数清晰可见。该方法利用精确的轨迹级伯努利KL散度，改进了现有文献中的系数：在中等条件下从0.015提升至0.0200，在更强的动作、直径和时域条件下最高达0.0291（提升94%），极限系数为$\frac{1}{32}\sqrt{(A-3)/A}$。对于上界，提出了一个可审计的跨度约束乐观学习器组合规则，但未给出具体系数；自适应方向方差与规划证书仍待解决。同时，形式化了期望转换的有效性与常数可比性，并通过受控诊断验证了直径依赖性、奖金与宽度交互、跨度误设等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平均奖励强化学习的遗憾界因概率模式、结构参数、对数归一化、先验信息及规划假设差异而难以直接比较，缺乏统一的量化标准。本文旨在建立一种常数感知的比较框架，以实现更公平、精确的性能评估。

Method: 构造基于二叉树的双状态块模型，采用精确的轨迹级伯努利KL散度进行分析，显式保留动作预算、直径、占用率、导航成本和终端偏差等关键参数；提出可审计的跨度约束乐观学习器组合规则；形式化有效期望转换与常数可比性机制。

Result: 在中等条件下，遗憾系数由0.015提升至0.0200；在强条件（动作、直径、时域）下可达0.0291，相比原系数提升94%；极限系数为$\frac{1}{32}\sqrt{(A-3)/A}$。受控诊断验证了直径依赖、奖金-宽度交互、跨度误设等关键因素的影响。

Conclusion: 本文建立了常数感知的比较框架，显著提升了平均奖励强化学习遗憾界的可比性和精度，为未来算法设计与理论分析提供了更清晰的基准。

Abstract: Average-reward reinforcement-learning regret is known up to logarithmic factors, but the numerical content of published guarantees is difficult to compare because probability mode, structural parameter, logarithmic normalization, prior information, and planning assumptions differ. We introduce a constant-aware comparison protocol and derive an explicit finite lower certificate for communicating MDPs. The construction is a binary tree of two-state blocks; its proof uses exact trajectory-level Bernoulli KL divergence and keeps action budget, diameter, occupancy, navigation cost, and terminal bias explicit. A common closed-form envelope improves the published coefficient $0.015$ across a finite frontier: $0.0200$ in a moderate regime and up to $0.0291$ under stronger action, diameter, and horizon conditions, a $94\%$ increase. The limiting coefficient is $\frac1{32}\sqrt{(A-3)/A}$. For upper bounds, we give an auditable composition rule for a span-constrained optimistic learner, but do not claim a coefficient while adaptive directional-variance and planning certificates remain open. We also formalize valid expectation conversion and constant comparability. Controlled diagnostics test diameter dependence, bonus-by-width interactions, span misspecification, and the finite lower certificate on its exact family.

</details>


### [193] [LUCID: Latent-Skill Unified Control via Imagined Dynamics for Long-Horizon Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07746)
*Cheng Guo,Mingzhe Ni,Angelo Cangelosi,Arash Ajoudani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LUCID是一个分层的基于模型的强化学习框架，通过在学习到的动力学模型上进行想象式回放来规划可重用技能，从而解决长时域人形机器人运动操作中复杂任务序列处理能力不足的问题。它首先通过对抗性模仿训练一个结构化的潜在条件低层策略，然后冻结该策略，联合学习高层策略和宏观动力学世界模型，使高层策略能够通过想象式回放进行优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将预训练技能与脚本规划器、有限状态机或特定任务的无模型策略协调，限制了其在复杂任务序列中的表现。因此，需要一种更灵活、可扩展的框架来支持长时域多任务操作。

Method: 提出LUCID框架，采用分层设计：先使用对抗性模仿训练结构化潜在条件低层策略并冻结；随后联合学习高层策略与宏观动力学世界模型，利用世界模型预测由潜在决策引起的时序状态转移，实现高层策略的想象式回放优化。

Result: 在多个模拟的多物体重新排列场景中评估，LUCID在完整任务成功率和部分完成率上均优于先前基线方法，证明其在复杂顺序运动操作任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: LUCID通过结合可重用技能与基于模型的高层规划，在长时域人形机器人运动操作任务中展现出更强的泛化能力和鲁棒性，为复杂序列任务提供了一种高效且可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon humanoid loco-manipulation requires composing versatile whole-body skills and reliable high-level decision making. Existing methods often coordinate pretrained skills with scripted planners, finite-state machines or task-specific model-free policies, restricting their ability to handle complex task sequences. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{LUCID}, a hierarchical model-based reinforcement learning framework that plans over reusable skills through imagined rollouts of a learned dynamics model. LUCID first trains a structured latent-conditioned low-level policy via adversarial imitation and then freezes it while jointly learning a high-level policy and macro-dynamics world model. The world model predicts the temporally extended state transitions induced by latent decisions, enabling high-level policy optimization through imagined rollouts. We evaluate our framework across various simulated multi-object rearrangement scenarios. Experimental results show that LUCID improves the full-task success and partial-completion rates compared to prior baseline methods, demonstrating its effectiveness in complex sequential loco-manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [194] [From Benchmark Performance to Tool Deployment: Human-in-the-Loop Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07770)
*Mike Szklarzewski,CJ George,Gavin Smithson,Christopher Stokes,Dakota Fulp,William M. Jones,Benjamin Wynn,Alexander Ur,Agit Yesiloz,Clint Kallenbach,Mark Swartz,Nathan DeBardeleben,Sharmistha Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了19种无监督异常检测模型在BowTie制造数据集上的表现，发现其在真实工业环境下性能不稳定、对预处理敏感且跨条件不一致，没有单一方法表现出普遍鲁棒性。为此，作者提出并部署了一个融合人工介入的统一框架，结合图像标注、AI辅助缺陷检测和集成验证引擎，提升缺陷审查效率与一致性，弥补基准测试与实际部署之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化异常检测方法在学术基准上表现良好，但在真实工业场景下（如反射表面、细微缺陷、特定产品形态变化）性能不稳定，缺乏可靠性，亟需更贴近实际部署需求的解决方案。

Method: 提出一个基于人类-机器协同的统一框架，包含图像标注、AI辅助缺陷检测、热力图引导审查、SAM精炼候选区域、掩码评估及审查历史记录等功能模块，实现缺陷的高效、准确、可追溯审查。

Result: 模型在真实工业数据上的表现远不如基准测试结果，性能受数据质量影响显著；而所提出的框架显著提升了缺陷检测的准确性与检查员的一致性，有效弥合了理论性能与实际应用间的鸿沟。

Conclusion: 当前主流异常检测模型在真实工业场景中存在稳定性差、鲁棒性不足的问题，单纯依赖算法难以保障可靠部署；引入人机协同机制是实现高可信缺陷检测的有效路径，为制造业智能质检提供了可落地的实践框架。

Abstract: Automated anomaly detection methods often report strong performance on curated academic benchmarks, but their behavior under real-world industrial conditions is less clear. In this work, we evaluate 19 unsupervised anomaly detection models on the BowTie dataset, a challenging manufacturing dataset with reflective surfaces, subtle defects, and profile-specific variation. In contrast to benchmark results, we observe that model performance is less stable than typically reported on standard benchmarks such as MVTec AD, highly sensitive to preprocessing, and inconsistent across conditions, with no single approach emerging as uniformly robust; a consensus audit further indicates that nominal-data quality affects deployment.
  Motivated by these findings, we developed and initially deployed a unified human-in-the-loop framework for manufactured-part inspection that combines image annotation, AI-assisted defect detection, and an integrated validation engine, replacing a prior manual visual inspection and documentation workflow. The system supports heatmap-guided defect review, SAM-refined candidate regions for inspector acceptance, rejection, or boundary adjustment, mask evaluation where annotations exist, and review history for inspector consistency and onboarding. Together, the results highlight the gap between benchmark performance and deployment reality, and provide a practical framework for addressing it.

</details>


### [195] [The Sample Complexity of Policy Learning with Mu-Resets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07772)
*Gene Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在μ-重置交互协议下的策略型强化学习，解决了[KLS25]提出的关于策略可实现性对样本复杂度影响的问题。结果显示，在有界全策略集中性假设下，样本复杂度为exp(Ω(H))；而在有界前推集中性假设下，其对时间步长H的依赖被精确刻画为exp(Θ(√H))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在澄清策略可实现性在μ-重置交互协议下对强化学习样本复杂度的影响，特别是针对[KL02]和[KLS25]提出的关键问题。

Method: 通过分析不同集中性假设（全策略集中性与前推集中性）下的样本复杂度，构建理论下界与上界，利用概率分析与信息论工具进行证明。

Result: 在有界全策略集中性下，样本复杂度为指数级增长，即exp(Ω(H))；在有界前推集中性下，样本复杂度为exp(Θ(√H))，且该界是紧的。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了重置分布覆盖性对样本复杂度的关键作用，表明集中性条件决定了算法在高维或长时任务中的可学习性边界。

Abstract: We study policy-based reinforcement learning under the $μ$-resets interaction protocol of Kakade and Langford [KL02]. This interaction protocol enables the learner to sample trajectories from a given exploratory reset distribution $μ$, in addition to the starting distribution. We resolve the question raised by [KLS25] on the role of policy realizability for the sample complexity of this problem. Critically, the dependence on horizon $H$ is governed by the notion of coverage assumed of the reset distribution. Under bounded all-policy concentrability, we show a $\exp(Ω(H))$ sample complexity lower bound; with bounded pushforward concentrability, we show the dependence on horizon is tightly characterized as $\exp(Θ(\sqrt H))$.

</details>


### [196] [Shape Mutating Expert Compression:LorExperts and BTExperts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07814)
*Inesh Chakrabarti,Sourjya Roy,Bowen Bao,Thiago Crepaldi,Spandan Tiwari,Ashish Sirasao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LorExperts 是一种保留路由器的专家压缩方法，通过聚类专家并为每个簇保留一个全精度主导专家，其余专家以低秩修正表示，有效提升压缩效率并保持模型性能。该方法在高专家数量下优于 D²-MoE 等基线，并支持推理时共享计算优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MoE 模型压缩方法如专家剪枝或合并需重训练路由器，而低秩分解因共享组件难以拟合大量近正交专家，导致性能随专家数增长显著下降。因此需要一种既能保留原始路由器、又能高效压缩且适用于大规模专家的方案。

Method: 提出 LorExperts：基于专家功能共激活社区的聚类策略，每个簇中保留一个全精度主导专家，其余专家用低秩修正表示；引入重建微调和 BTExperts（树状结构）以进一步优化推理效率与精度。

Result: 在 Qwen3-30B-A3B 与 Gemma-4-26B-A4B 上实现约 50% 专家压缩，下游任务准确率和困惑度优于基线，尤其在专家数量增加时对 D²-MoE 的优势更明显；BTExperts 实现推理时共享计算的摊销。

Conclusion: LorExperts 通过功能聚类与局部低秩修正，在不重训练路由器的前提下实现了高效且可扩展的 MoE 模型压缩，兼具高性能与推理效率，为大规模 MoE 部署提供了新路径。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models deliver high capacity at low per-token compute, but deploying them cheaply requires compressing their many expert weight matrices. Expert pruning (e.g., REAP) and merging reduce cost but sacrifice accuracy and require retraining the router; low-rank delta decomposition of experts (e.g., D^2-MoE) preserves all experts and the router, but degrades sharply as the expert count grows because a single shared component cannot approximate many near-orthogonal experts.
  Because MoE expert weights are near-orthogonal, a single shared component (as in prior delta decomposition) scales poorly with the expert count; we show that experts nonetheless organize into functional co-activation communities that are decoupled from weight similarity. Building on this, we introduce LorExperts, a router-preserving compression method that clusters experts, keeps one full-precision dominant per cluster, and represents the remaining members as low-rank corrections to their local dominant. LorExperts retains all experts and the original router (no router retraining). At ~50% expert compression on Qwen3-30B-A3B and Gemma-4-26B-A4B, LorExperts preserves downstream accuracy and perplexity better than the baselines on most of the tasks; the margin over D^2-MoE grows with expert count E. We further give a reconstruction fine-tuning procedure for LorExperts, and BTExperts, a tree organization of dominants and corrections that enables inference-time amortization of shared computation.

</details>


### [197] [From token probabilities to calibrated confidence: An empirical study of mathematical question answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07827)
*Avery Ma,Lorne Schell,Vin Bhaskara,Leila Pishdad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在数学问题回答中的置信度估计与校准，比较单次通过（复用生成时的词元概率）与多次通过（通过验证或随机前向传播获取额外置信信号）方法。发现虽然单个词元概率高度饱和，但对整个序列的概率聚合能捕捉正确与错误生成间的细微差异，提供更有效的置信度估计。多轮方法如自验证（重提示）和蒙特卡洛丢弃法可实现校准的置信度估计。后处理校准方法（Platt缩放与等倾回归）显著降低域内校准误差，但其数据效率随数据集难度变化，且校准映射在不同数据集与模型间常呈现非对称迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在生成答案时往往过于自信，而置信度估计的目标是准确衡量生成答案正确的概率。尽管已有研究表明词元概率通常过拟合，但这些现成信号是否可用于校准的置信度估计仍不明确。尤其在数学问题问答任务中，如何利用现有信息提升置信度估计的准确性与可靠性，是关键挑战。

Method: 对比单次通过与多次通过的置信度估计方法。单次通过方法直接使用原始生成过程中的词元概率；多次通过方法包括：自验证（通过重提示进行自我验证，含低成本原位变体）与蒙特卡洛丢弃法（基于随机前向传播的方差估计置信度）。此外，采用Platt缩放与等倾回归两种后处理校准方法以优化置信度输出。

Result: 词元概率虽高度饱和，但序列级聚合能有效区分正确与错误生成。多轮方法（如自验证与蒙特卡洛丢弃）可实现良好校准。后处理校准显著降低域内校准误差，但其效果受数据集难度影响，且校准映射在跨数据集与模型间存在不对称迁移现象。

Conclusion: 即使单个词元概率不可靠，通过聚合全序列概率仍可获得有信息量的置信度估计。多轮方法与后处理校准共同提升了置信度的校准性，但需注意其泛化能力受限于数据分布与模型差异。

Abstract: Confidence estimation for large language models (LLMs) aims to estimate the probability that a generated answer is correct, while calibration aligns these estimates with empirical accuracy. Prior work has shown that token probabilities are often overconfident, we investigate whether these readily available signals can nevertheless provide well-calibrated confidence estimation for mathematical question answering. We compare single-pass estimators, which reuse token probabilities from the original generation, with multi-pass estimators, which obtain additional confidence signals through verification or stochastic forward passes. While individual token probabilities can be highly saturated, we find that aggregating token probabilities over the full sequence captures small but consistent differences between correct and incorrect generations, yielding more informative confidence estimates. Multi-pass methods can yield calibrated confidence estimates. We study two such approaches: self-verification through re-prompting, including a lower-cost in-situ variant, and Monte Carlo Dropout, which derives confidence from variation across stochastic forward passes. We further evaluate two post-hoc calibration methods, Platt scaling and isotonic regression, both of which substantially reduce in-domain calibration error. However, their data efficiency varies with dataset difficulty, and the calibration mappings often transfer asymmetrically across datasets and models.

</details>


### [198] [TEMPER: Tensorized Efficient Manifold-constrained Parameterization for Expressive Residual Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07851)
*Yuxuan Gu,Wuyang Zhou,Huijun Xing,Danilo Mandic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TEMPER，一种基于张量化高效流形约束参数化的表达性残差路由方法，通过将生成器表示为多模态张量并使用张量网络进行参数化，有效降低了参数增长，同时保持了动态路由的表达能力。在语言建模和常识推理任务中，TEMPER在性能上优于或相当现有方法，且额外参数减少约84%（在8个残差流时），展现出更强的性能-参数效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有残差连接方法（如Hyper-connections和mHC）依赖于密集、无结构的生成器，在增加残差流数量时导致参数量急剧上升，存在生成器层面的瓶颈。需要一种更高效的参数化方式来降低复杂度，同时保持动态路由的表达性和训练稳定性。

Method: 将生成器表示为输入流、特征和输出流三个模式的多维张量，并利用张量网络实现低秩结构化参数化，从而在保持流形约束路由接口的同时显著减少参数量。通过控制张量秩来调节路由子空间维度，且生成器近似误差可界定路由逻辑差异与输出差异。

Result: TEMPER在语言建模和常识推理任务中表现优异，达到或超过现有方法性能；在8个残差流设置下，核心得分（CORE score）最优，额外参数仅需mHC的16%（即减少约84%），证明其具有更强的性能-参数效率平衡。

Conclusion: TEMPER通过张量化与低秩参数化，解决了现有动态残差路由方法中的参数爆炸问题，实现了高效、可解释且高性能的残差路由设计，是表达性与效率兼顾的先进范式。

Abstract: Residual connections rely on a static residual pathway, and are essential for training deep neural networks. Hyper-connections (HC) increase the expressivity of residual routing by incorporating multiple residual streams and learning dynamic information flow, while manifold-constrained (mHC) variants stabilize training through doubly stochastic residual mixing. However, a generator-level bottleneck remains in existing methods: they use dense, unstructured generators for pre-branch aggregation, residual mixing, and post-branch redistribution, which results in parameter count growing rapidly with the number of streams. To address this issue, we propose \underline{\textbf{T}}ensorized \underline{\textbf{E}}fficient \underline{\textbf{M}}anifold-constrained \underline{\textbf{P}}arameterization for \underline{\textbf{E}}xpressive Residual \underline{\textbf{R}}outing (\textbf{TEMPER}), which represents these generators as multi-way tensors over the input-stream, feature, and output-stream modes, and parameterizes them using tensor networks. Such a structured low-rank formulation is shown to preserve token-dependent manifold-constrained routing interface while substantially reducing parameter growth. It also promotes interpretability and intuition, as: i) tensor ranks control the dimensionality of the learned routing subspace, with full ranks recovering dense routing; while ii) the generator approximation errors bound differences in routing logits and, consequently, in the routed-block outputs. Comprehensive experiments show that TEMPER matches or outperforms existing methods across language modeling and commonsense reasoning tasks, while requiring substantially fewer additional parameters. At eight residual streams, TEMPER achieves the best CORE score while using about $84\%$ fewer additional parameters than mHC, thus showing a stronger performance-parameter efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [199] [CommitKV: Lifecycle-Aware KV Cache Compression via Commit Transitions for Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07855)
*Weizhong Huang,Jinchao Zhang,Xiawu Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CommitKV提出一种新的KV缓存压缩方法，通过识别代理事件的提交转换来区分临时休眠信息与已完成任务的信息。它通过对比提交前后的删除效果，利用贪心联合测试筛选可退役的页面，仅在合并观察结果后确认其影响仍受控时才移除。实验表明，该方法显著降低内存占用、加速推理并提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩方法基于注意力分数低即为无关状态的假设，但忽略了某些信息虽当前不活跃却可能在未来变得重要；传统快照式淘汰无法有效区分暂时休眠与已终结的信息，导致误删或冗余保留。

Method: CommitKV将完成的代理事件分页，比较每个页面在工具调用提交前与提交后观察结果纳入时的删除影响，通过成对测量判断是否为临时休眠状态；采用贪心联合测试，仅当组合后的影响仍在可控范围内才接受退役；在压缩检查点处排除已接受页面，保护待测页面，其余状态按原索引保留于缓存预算内。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，CommitKV相比现有方法显著降低内存使用、加快端到端推理速度，并取得更高准确率。

Conclusion: CommitKV通过引入提交驱动的生命周期分析机制，实现了对临时休眠信息与已完成信息的精准区分，有效提升了KV缓存压缩的效率与可靠性，为多轮推理-行动代理的高效运行提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multi-turn Reasoning-and-Acting (ReAct) agents accumulate growing trajectories of reasoning, tool calls, and observations. Their key-value (KV) caches grow accordingly, increasing memory use and attention cost during model inference. Existing KV cache compression methods reduce these costs by evicting states with low attention scores. However, low attention in the current turn does not imply future irrelevance, as temporarily inactive information may become important later. Snapshot-based eviction methods therefore do not explicitly distinguish temporarily dormant information from information that appears to have completed its role. In this paper, we present CommitKV, which identifies KV lifecycles through commit transitions. Specifically, CommitKV first divides completed agent events into token pages and compares each eligible page's deletion effect before a tool-call commit and after the commit's returned observation has been incorporated. Based on these paired measurements, CommitKV distinguishes dormant pages from high-to-low completion candidates. It then applies a greedy joint test, accepting candidates for retirement only when their combined post-commit effect remains bounded. Finally, at a later compression checkpoint, accepted pages are excluded, a bounded set of pages awaiting post-commit measurement is protected, and the remaining KV states are retained within the cache budget using the same token indices for keys, values, and absolute positions. These mechanisms ensure that CommitKV can distinguish dormant information from information that has completed its observed role and can be safely removed. Experiments on various benchmarks show that CommitKV reduces agent memory use, accelerates end-to-end inference, and achieves higher accuracy than existing KV cache compression methods.

</details>


### [200] [Adaptive KappaSharp: Condition-Number Shaping for Preferential Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07859)
