*Ketong Shao,Jialu Wang,Xuekai Pei,Ali Mesbah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KappaSharp improves Preferential Bayesian Optimization by addressing the rank-deficiency issue in the likelihood Hessian through a diagonal correction that adapts to prior uncertainty. An adaptive version activates correction only when needed, outperforming standard PBO on 11 benchmarks, including a 16D plasma medicine controller tuning task.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The standard PairwiseGP with EUBO in PBO leads to isolated comparison components in the graph, causing a rank-deficient Hessian and poor conditioning. Existing fixes either waste queries or apply uniform regularization that disturbs well-constrained directions. This limits optimization efficiency and accuracy.

Method: KappaSharp introduces a diagonal correction to the Hessian during model fitting, increasing the condition number where prior uncertainty is high. The adaptive variant applies correction only when the surrogate is confident in recent comparisons, minimizing unnecessary perturbations.

Result: On 11 benchmarks (5–20 dimensions), including a 16D controller tuning problem in plasma medicine, Adaptive KappaSharp outperforms the standard PBO baseline by up to +10.9% (p=0.003), demonstrating improved convergence and robustness.

Conclusion: KappaSharp effectively resolves structural rank deficiency in PBO by introducing targeted, adaptive Hessian corrections, leading to more stable and efficient optimization without sacrificing query efficiency.

Abstract: Preferential Bayesian optimization (PBO) optimizes objectives accessible only through pairwise user comparisons. The standard approach fits a Gaussian process surrogate for observed pairwise comparisons (PairwiseGP) using the Laplace approximation and selects queries with the Expected Utility of Best Option (EUBO) acquisition function. EUBO queries new candidates at each step, producing pairs that share no candidates with previous queries. Each such pair forms an isolated component in the comparison graph, removing one degree of freedom from the likelihood Hessian and making it rank-deficient. This deficiency is structural and cannot be resolved by changing the surrogate modeling approach. Existing approaches to remedy this issue either waste query budget by forcing comparisons to stay connected, or apply uniform regularization that also perturbs directions already well-constrained by the observed comparisons. We propose KappaSharp that enables a diagonal correction to the Hessian to reduce its condition number, with larger corrections where the prior uncertainty is higher. The correction is only applied in the model fitting step, not query selection. An adaptive variant of KappaSharp is also presented that activates the correction only when the surrogate is confident about recent comparisons, avoiding unnecessary corrections when the problem is well-conditioned. On 11 benchmarks (5--20 dimensions), including a 16-dimensional controller tuning problem in plasma medicine, Adaptive KappaSharp outperforms the standard PBO baseline, with up to +10.9% ($p{=}0.003$).

</details>


### [201] [CONFER: Conflict-Aware Evidence Negotiation for Regime-Calibrated Weak Supervision in Multimodal Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07867)
*Bojing Hou,Ruohao Li,Yitong Zhu,Luwen Yu,Yuyang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CONFER是一种基于图的冲突感知证据协商框架，用于弱监督多模态情感识别。它将每个模态专家建模为节点，包含预测信念、基于边界的不确定性以及从历史表现和当前样本不确定性估计的运行时可靠性。通过不确定性感知的兼容性和可靠性导向的非对称边权重进行迭代消息传递协商，最终实现同伴支持的预测输出。该方法定义了三种样本特定的弱标签校准模式：共识、分歧和模糊。在AMIGOS、MAHNOB-HCI和DEAP数据集上，CONFER在严格留一被试（LOSO）评估下分别达到0.873和0.854的准确率，且在高冲突样本中表现出更大的协商收益，并对弱标签噪声更具鲁棒性，表明跨模态冲突可有效用于模态协调与监督可靠性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感识别常将自报告标签视为可靠监督，但忽视了自报告不可靠性和跨模态冲突问题。因此需要一种能感知冲突并动态调节各模态贡献的机制，以提升弱监督场景下的识别性能与鲁棒性。

Method: CONFER将各模态专家建模为图节点，引入预测信念、边界不确定性及运行时可靠性；利用不确定性感知的兼容性与可靠性导向的非对称边权重进行迭代消息传递协商；通过冲突减少、残余分歧与平均模态不确定性划分出共识、分歧、模糊三种状态，实现样本级弱标签校准。

Result: CONFER在AMIGOS-V和MAHNOB-V数据集上分别取得0.873和0.854的准确率，在严格留一被试（LOSO）评估下表现优异；在高冲突样本中获得更大协商增益，对弱标签污染具有更强鲁棒性，验证了跨模态冲突信息的有效性。

Conclusion: CONFER通过建模模态间的不确定性与冲突，实现了更可靠的弱监督多模态情感识别，揭示了跨模态冲突不仅是噪声，更是可用于指导模态协调与监督可信度估计的重要信号。

Abstract: Multimodal emotion recognition often treats self-reported labels as reliable supervision while overlooking self-report unreliability and cross-modal conflict. We propose \textbf{CONFER}, a graph-based conflict-aware evidence negotiation framework for weakly supervised multimodal emotion recognition. CONFER represents each modality expert as a node with a predictive belief, boundary-based uncertainty, and runtime reliability estimated from historical out-of-fold performance and current-sample uncertainty. Uncertainty-aware compatibility and reliability-directed asymmetric edge weights govern iterative message-passing negotiation, followed by peer-supported prediction readout. Conflict reduction, residual disagreement, and mean modality uncertainty further characterize three regimes---Consensus, Dissent, and Ambiguity---for sample-specific weak-label calibration. We evaluate CONFER on AMIGOS, MAHNOB-HCI, and DEAP under subject-dependent 10-fold and strict leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) protocols. CONFER achieves competitive performance, reaching \textbf{0.873} accuracy on AMIGOS-V and \textbf{0.854} accuracy on MAHNOB-V under strict LOSO evaluation. Further analyses show larger negotiation gains on high-conflict samples and improved robustness to weak-label corruption, indicating that cross-modal conflict provides useful information for both directional modality coordination and supervision-reliability estimation.

</details>


### [202] [V-Simba: Unleashing the Architectural Potential of RL in Visual Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07870)
*Donghu Kim,Youngdo Lee,Hojoon Lee,Johan Obando-Ceron,Byungkun Lee,Aaron Courville,Pablo Samuel Castro,Jaegul Choo,Clare Lyle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: V-Simba 是一种受状态空间强化学习中 Simba 架构启发的视觉强化学习架构，通过引入归一化层和逐点卷积，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了样本效率。它在 DMC、Adroit 和 Meta-World 基准上达到或超越当前最优性能，且优于 DrQ-v2 的计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升视觉强化学习中的样本效率，尤其是在数据收集成本高的实际场景（如机器人）中；现有方法多依赖算法改进，而状态空间RL的架构设计已证明可显著提升性能，因此探索其是否适用于视觉RL具有重要意义。

Method: 基于 Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) 并结合数据增强，对网络结构进行改进：加入归一化层以稳定训练，使用点状卷积降低计算量，构建简单但高效的视觉RL架构 V-Simba。

Result: V-Simba 在 DMC、Adroit 与 Meta-World 基准测试中表现达到或超过现有最先进方法，同时在计算效率上优于 DrQ-v2，验证了架构设计在视觉RL中的有效性。

Conclusion: 架构设计本身可以显著提升视觉强化学习的样本效率，V-Simba 证明了从状态空间RL中提取的架构原则可成功迁移至视觉RL任务中，并带来性能与效率的双重提升。

Abstract: Improving sample efficiency remains a core challenge in reinforcement learning (RL), especially in real-world settings like robotics, where data collection is costly. This challenge is pronounced in visual RL, where high-dimensional inputs often obscure learning signals. While prior work in visual RL has focused on algorithmic solutions, such as better dynamics models or exploration strategies, recent advances in state-based RL show that architectural design alone can lead to significant gains in sample efficiency. This raises an important question: Can these architectural principles transfer to visual RL? In response, we introduce V-Simba, a simple yet effective visual RL architecture inspired by the Simba architecture from state-based RL. Built on top of Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) with data augmentation, V-Simba modifies the architecture by adding normalization layers to stabilize training and using pointwise convolutions to reduce computation. Despite its simplicity, V-Simba matches or outperforms the state-of-the-art methods across the DMC, Adroit, and Meta-World benchmarks, while being more computationally efficient than DrQ-v2. We make our code publicly available at https://github.com/DAVIAN-Robotics/V-Simba.

</details>


### [203] [Router Sensitivity Under Lightweight Fine-Tuning Identifies Prunable Experts in Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07890)
*Ali Janati,Kaoutar El Maghraoui,Xinyi Luo,Wenyuan Shen,Owen Zou,Yankai Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在轻量级微调下，如何利用路由器的梯度变化来指导Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）模型中专家的剪枝。通过仅微调路由器并使用LoRA进行参数高效适配，发现基于$\ell_2$路由器变化的专家排序可有效识别冗余专家，实现高压缩比下的性能保持。在Mixtral-8×7B-Instruct上，仅训练0.002%参数的路由器专用LoRA优于全模块LoRA，且在移除一半专家后仍保持27.54%的准确率；随着适配扩展到注意力和专家权重，信号质量下降。该方法在25%压缩下表现接近使用完整激活统计的方法，且内存减少49%，每令牌延迟降低37%。该策略同样适用于Qwen1.5-MoE，在数学任务上保留49.7%平均准确率，而随机剪枝降至个位数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论表明，通过剪枝在微调过程中路由器权重变化最小的专家可保持模型精度，但依赖于全量微调。本文旨在探索是否可通过轻量级适应（如LoRA）恢复这一信号，并实现大规模部署下的高效专家剪枝。

Method: 采用参数高效的轻量级微调方法（如LoRA、IA3），仅对路由器进行微调或部分微调，计算每个专家的$\ell_2$路由器权重变化，按变化量排序后一次性剪枝变化最小的专家。比较不同适配方式（如全模块、路由器专用、加性适配等）对剪枝信号的影响。

Result: 路由器专用LoRA仅微调0.002%参数即达到27.54%的MMLU-Pro准确率，优于全模块LoRA；剪枝后内存减少49%，延迟降低37%；在25%压缩下性能接近基于完整激活统计的方法；在多任务测试中，半专家剪枝仍保持49.7%平均准确率，显著优于随机或基于幅度的剪枝。

Conclusion: 轻量级微调下的路由器敏感性为专家剪枝提供了可证明的动机基础，使得大规模MoE模型在不牺牲性能的前提下实现高效压缩与部署，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models decouple total parameters from per-token compute, but deployment still requires storing every expert. Recent theory shows that pruning experts with the smallest router-norm changes during fine-tuning can preserve accuracy, but assumes full fine-tuning. We test whether lightweight adaptation can recover this signal. We briefly fine-tune with a parameter-efficient adapter, rank experts by the induced $\ell_2$ router change, and prune the least-changed experts in one shot. On Mixtral-8$\times$7B-Instruct (44.83% MMLU-Pro), router-only LoRA trains 0.002% of parameters and outperforms all-module LoRA at matched rank with half the experts removed (27.54% vs. 24.42%); signal quality declines as adaptation spreads to attention and expert weights. Accuracy improves monotonically with LoRA rank, reaching 28.76%. IA3, which leaves router weights frozen, matches direct router adaptation, whereas unconstrained additive adapters degrade the signal. Router-guided MMLU-Pro accuracy decays quasi-linearly rather than collapsing, remains nearly 1.8 times that of magnitude-based or random pruning at maximal compression, and reduces memory by 49% and per-token latency by 37%. At 25% compression, retention is competitive with methods using full activation statistics. The criterion also transfers to Qwen1.5-MoE fine-tuned for mathematics, retaining 49.7% mean accuracy over eleven benchmarks with half the experts removed while random pruning falls to single digits. Router sensitivity under lightweight fine-tuning therefore makes provably motivated expert pruning practical at scale.

</details>


### [204] [LLM-Based Embeddings for Program Analysis and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07894)
*Calvin Higgins,Marco Alvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将LLMCompiler生成的程序嵌入应用于程序分析与优化任务，通过分割代码并聚合嵌入实现高效嵌入。结合源码与中间表示（IR）嵌入，在算法分类中达到1.54%错误率，较现有最佳方法提升12%，在异构设备映射任务上表现优异，表明针对性能感知的IR嵌入训练可显著提升代码优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前程序分析与优化依赖传统方法，效率和精度受限；而大型语言模型在代码理解方面展现出潜力，但尚未充分应用于基于中间表示（IR）的程序分析。本文旨在探索利用预训练于IR代码的大型语言模型（如LLMCompiler）生成程序嵌入，以提升程序分析与优化任务的表现。

Method: 将程序按块分割，分别使用预训练的LLM对源码和中间表示（IR）进行独立嵌入，再通过聚合策略整合各块嵌入，形成整体程序嵌入。该方法简单有效，适用于多种代码分析任务。

Result: 在算法分类任务中，结合源码与IR嵌入的方法实现了1.54%的错误率，相比当前最优方法降低12%；在异构设备映射任务中也表现出良好性能，验证了该嵌入方法的有效性与竞争力。

Conclusion: 利用大规模预训练于中间表示代码的大型语言模型生成程序嵌入，能够显著提升程序分析与优化任务的性能，尤其在结合源码与IR信息时效果更佳；未来训练具备性能感知能力的专门化模型有望进一步推动代码优化技术发展。

Abstract: Recent advances have highlighted the potential of machine learning, particularly Large Language Models (LLMs), for analyzing and optimizing programs. We present the first application of program embeddings from LLMCompiler---an LLM massively pretrained on intermediate representation (IR) code---to representative program analysis and optimization tasks. We generate program embeddings directly from source and IR code using a simple approach: split programs into chunks, independently embed each chunk with pretrained LLMs, and then aggregate the chunk embeddings into a single program embedding. Our experiments show that combining source and IR code embeddings achieves an error rate of 1.54\% in algorithm classification, a 12\% improvement over the current state-of-the-art, and a competitive accuracy on heterogeneous device mapping. These findings suggest that training a performance-aware LLM for embedding IR code might yield state-of-the-art results in code optimization tasks.

</details>


### [205] [When Does Trace-Driven Evaluation Mislead MoE Expert Caching? Replay Semantics, Workload Contamination, and Operating Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07911)
*Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)模型因专家权重过大而需将部分权重移至主机内存，导致专家缓存管理成为关键优化点。然而，现有评估方法存在脆弱性：不同重放语义、工作负载污染和运行模式会显著改变评估结论。研究发现，即使在修正后，仍存在44.2%-45.9%的与离线最优解的差距，其中84.3%-96.6%归因于对未来使用情况的先验知识。基于因果未来使用的预测器可恢复11.4%的差距，优于传统策略，但整体提升有限。因此，在当前设置下，轻量级因果机制难以实现离线最优带来的收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高MoE模型中专家缓存管理效率，以减少每令牌的专家数据流量；但现有评估方法存在缺陷，可能导致错误的策略排序和性能判断。

Method: 采用基于追踪的事件原子化模拟器，对三个具有40、64、128个专家的MoE模型进行评估，系统分析重放语义、工作负载污染和运行模式对评估结果的影响，并引入强制准入的虚拟最优解（oracle）来量化潜在收益。

Result: 评估发现不同实验条件会显著改变策略表现排名，修正后仍存在显著的与离线最优的差距（44.2%-45.9%），且大部分损失源于对未来使用情况的未知。基于因果未来使用的预测机制可恢复约11.4%的差距，优于传统替换策略，但仍远低于理论上限。

Conclusion: 在当前评估设置下，尽管存在可观的离线最优差距，但轻量级因果机制仅能恢复部分收益，表明实际部署中缓存优化的提升空间有限，评估方法的严谨性至关重要。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have outgrown accelerator memory, and offloading expert weights to host memory is now standard. This makes expert cache management an attractive lever: a policy that raised the hit rate would cut expert traffic per token. Evaluating that is a measurement problem, and we find the measurement fragile.
  With a trace-driven, event-atomic simulator over three MoE models (40, 64, 128 experts), we isolate three evaluation axes that change conclusions, not just numbers. Replay semantics: under a fused-event traffic contract, an inconsistent per-access replay inflates recency-based policies by 27-29% while leaving frequency-based and static ones within 4%, inverting the policy ranking. Workload contamination: probe sets using one instruction template per category produce verbatim-identical generation prefixes; a matched-pair rendering intervention moves the measured early-window effect by 19.4-31.9 points and reverses which workloads look most cache-friendly. Operating regimes: normalized miss fractions do not transfer across models, so the per-step expert union relative to per-layer capacity must be reported -- yet permuting only the temporal order of an identical event stream moves the offline-optimal gap from 44.9% to 30.8%, so it is not sufficient.
  Corrected, a stable gap to the offline optimum remains (44.2-45.9% over 13 frozen workload compositions). A forced-admission oracle attributes 84.3-96.6% of it to knowing which resident expert is used furthest in the future. A causal next-use predictor, used as an eviction rule, recovers -11.4% of the gap; it picks an optimal victim 3.4% of the time, against 2.4% for a random resident block and 20.6-22.1% for LRU and LFRU. Our position is narrow: in our evaluated settings a large offline-optimal gap substantially overstates the gains recovered by representative lightweight causal mechanisms.

</details>


### [206] [SPECTRA: Pushing the KV Cache Beyond the 2-Bit Cliff via Spectral Transform Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07915)
*Jiamu Zhang,Liang Wu,Kelly Wan,Hanjie Chen,Liangjie Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPECTRA 是一种无需训练的缓存压缩方法，通过将 KV 缓存旋转到基于自身统计特性的坐标系中，识别出携带主要信息的少数通道，并集中分配比特预算，从而在长上下文场景下实现高达 12 倍的压缩率，突破传统量化方法在 2 位以下性能急剧下降的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在代理时代处理长输入时，其推理内存主要由不断增长的键值（KV）缓存占据，导致显存占用随上下文长度线性增加。现有方法依赖均匀量化降低精度以压缩缓存，但当精度低于 2 位时性能显著下降，因缓存中存在大量异常值，导致低精度量化后信息严重丢失。现有方法缺乏对重要通道的区分能力，因为原始缓存通道间相关性强，无法识别关键维度。

Method: 提出 SPECTRA 方法，首先利用缓存自身的统计特性构建一个去相关的新坐标系，使缓存中的信息分布高度集中在少数几个通道上；然后在此新空间中，将比特预算集中分配给这些高信息量的通道，实现高效且精准的压缩。该方法无需额外训练，可直接集成到现有系统中。

Result: 在 Llama-3.1-8B 和 Qwen2.5-7B 上的长上下文基准测试中，SPECTRA 实现了 4 倍压缩下的近乎无损效果，8 倍压缩下仍保持竞争力（而均匀量化已崩溃），并达到最高 12 倍压缩率，显著提升了单个 GPU 可处理的上下文长度和批量大小。

Conclusion: 通过将 KV 缓存转换到基于自身统计特性的去相关坐标系，SPECTRA 成功揭示了信息的关键通道，并实现了对压缩效率与质量的双重突破，为长上下文大模型部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly read long inputs in the agentic era, from whole documents and codebases to conversations across many turns. Their inference memory is then dominated by the key-value (KV) cache, the stored attention keys and values of every token the model has read and generated. Because the cache grows with context length and is re-read in full at every generated token, a longer context means more GPU memory.
  To reduce this cost, most existing methods compress the KV cache by lowering every stored value to the same low precision, a technique known as quantization. They can push this to nearly two bits per value, but rarely further, because quality drops sharply at this 2-bit cliff: four levels are too few for the cache's outlier-heavy values, where a few large entries consume the levels and collapse the rest into noise. A natural remedy is to spend more bits on the channels (feature dimensions) that matter and fewer on the rest, but the raw cache offers no handle: its channels are strongly correlated, so none stands out as more important.
  Our analysis shows that this handle appears once the cache is rotated into a coordinate system computed from its own statistics, removing these correlations. There, a small fraction of channels carries almost all the information, and spending the budget on those few is far more accurate than spreading it evenly.
  Guided by this analysis, we develop SPECTRA, a training-free, drop-in codec that re-encodes the cache into this coordinate system and concentrates the bit budget on the channels that carry the signal. On Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B over long-context benchmarks, SPECTRA is near-lossless at 4x compression, competitive at 8x where uniform quantization has collapsed, and reaches up to 12x, pushing usable compression past the 2-bit cliff so the same GPU holds longer contexts and larger batches.

</details>


### [207] [Spectral Outliers Reveal Dominant Learned Structure in Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07921)
*Kasun Dewage,Marianna Pensky,Suranadi De Silva,T. H. Bandara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用马尔琴科-帕斯图尔（MP）随机矩阵理论对预训练注意力权重进行分析，将每个投影矩阵分解为随机背景与谱异常值（信号）。通过因果验证发现，移除这些异常值会使Mistral-7B在HellaSwag、MMLU和PIQA上的性能降至随机水平，而移除等量的背景奇异值仅造成较小但显著的下降。在11个预训练Transformer中识别出五个共性模式：异常值编码了主要学习结构；Q投影包含最多异常值；在分组查询注意力中，V投影缺乏清晰的信号/噪声分离；输入层级的异常值在Q中形成结构化行带，在O中形成列带；特定残差流维度在K和O中跨层表现为带状异常值。研究结果可指导参数高效微调与结构化剪枝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解预训练变压器中注意力权重的内在结构，识别哪些部分承载关键语义信息，从而为模型压缩与高效微调提供理论依据。

Method: 应用马尔琴科-帕斯图尔（MP）随机矩阵理论对注意力权重进行谱分析，识别出随机背景与谱异常值，并通过零值操作进行因果验证。

Result: 谱异常值是模型性能的关键成分，其移除导致性能急剧下降；五种重复出现的结构模式揭示了注意力机制中的系统性组织特征。

Conclusion: MP分析揭示了预训练注意力权重中的可解释结构，为参数高效微调和结构化剪枝提供了新思路。

Abstract: We apply Marchenko-Pastur (MP) random matrix theory to pre-trained attention weights in order to separate each projection matrix into a random-like bulk and a set of spectral outliers. We validate this decomposition causally: zeroing the MP-identified outliers (signal) in Mistral-7B drives HellaSwag, MMLU, and PIQA close to random-chance performance, whereas zeroing a count-matched subset of bulk singular values causes smaller but non-negligible degradation. Across 11 pre-trained transformers we identify five recurring patterns: spectral outliers encode a dominant component of the learned structure; Q projections carry the most outliers; V projections under grouped-query attention lack a clean signal/noise separation; entry-level outliers form structured row-bands in Q and column-bands in O; and specific residual-stream dimensions persist as band outliers across layers in K and O. We close by outlining how these observations could inform parameter-efficient fine-tuning and structured pruning.

</details>


### [208] [Information Routing across Batch Boundaries: Memory--Batch Tradeoffs in Lipschitz Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07922)
*Zicheng Lyu,Zengfeng Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在随机Lipschitz置信区间问题中，状态宽度与更新深度之间的权衡。当状态宽度W满足W≳_d log(eT)时，刻画了最小最大期望伪遗憾的上界（忽略对数因子），并证明了状态宽度和更新深度不可互换；提出了一种新的惩罚项，揭示了信息路由约束的存在。匹配策略通过流式处理和擦除验证统计量，保留安全活跃集的掩码，实现了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自适应学习中状态宽度与更新深度之间的权衡，特别是在有限记忆和批量更新限制下的表现，以揭示信息传递的内在约束。

Method: 通过分析每个动作后最多保留W比特的奖励相关状态，并将动作组织为最多B个固定批次，建立理论框架，推导出最小最大伪遗憾的上下界，引入信息路由约束模型，并设计流式处理与擦除策略实现最优性能。

Result: 在W≳_d log(eT)条件下，得到了最小最大期望伪遗憾的紧致上界，揭示了新的惩罚项：T^{(d+2)/(d+3)} (1+(B-1)W)^{-1/(d(d+3))}，表明状态宽度与更新深度不可互换；同时证明了静态批量边界可达到与可预测自适应边界相同的性能。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了在有限记忆和批量更新条件下，状态宽度与更新深度之间存在非对称的信息约束；提出的策略通过动态管理状态信息，实现了在复杂环境下的高效自适应学习，统一了全维度最坏情况下的批处理前沿与对数记忆可达性。

Abstract: Adaptive learning needs both a state that preserves what observations imply and opportunities to act on that state. We study this width--depth tradeoff in stochastic Lipschitz bandits. After each pull, the learner retains at most $W$ bits of live reward-dependent state and organizes its pulls into at most $B$ committed batches. For $W\gtrsim_d\log(eT)$, we characterize minimax expected pseudo-regret up to logarithmic factors; the lower bounds hold for every $W$. Besides the classical sequential and unrestricted-memory batch costs, the frontier contains the new penalty \[
  T^{\frac{d+2}{d+3}}
  \bigl(1+(B-1)W\bigr)^{-\frac1{d(d+3)}}, \] proving that state width and update depth are not interchangeable. The interaction is an information-routing constraint: at regional scale $s$, low regret forces the committed action transcript to encode $Θ_d(s^{-d})$ regional decisions, while the collected boundary states carry at most $(B-1)W$ bits of entropy. Matching policies stream and erase verification statistics while retaining a mask of a safe active set, either in memory or fragment by fragment. The theorem recovers the full-dimensional worst-case batch-only frontier and logarithmic-memory achievability in the fully sequential specialization; static batch boundaries match predictable adaptive ones.

</details>


### [209] [Second Order Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07924)
*Drake Brown,Yuhao Huang,Shih-Hsin Wang,Bao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Second-Order Drifting Models，通过在相空间中引入人工速度变量，将漂移动力学提升至二阶，利用Nesterov加速思想缓解一阶漂移模型的频谱刚性问题，实现更快的细结构恢复和更优的收敛性，同时保持一步推理优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶漂移模型因核基漂移场导致频率依赖的训练动态，使得细尺度结构恢复缓慢，亟需改进训练效率与生成质量。

Method: 将生成样本扩展至相空间，引入人工速度变量，建立第二阶动力学模型，并基于半隐式算法进行训练，结合Nesterov加速理论优化动力学行为。

Result: 在合成分布匹配、序列数据生成和机器人控制任务中，新模型表现出更优的收敛速度和生成性能，优于或媲美一阶漂移基线。

Conclusion: Second-Order Drifting Models 通过二阶动力学设计有效克服了传统漂移模型的频谱刚性问题，为高效、快速的一步生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Drifting models are a recent class of one-step generative models that evolve the model distribution during training using a predefined sample-based drift field. Although they avoid iterative inference, their kernel-based drift fields induce frequency-dependent training dynamics: In the linearized regime, each Fourier mode of the density residual decays at a rate determined by the kernel spectrum, leading to slow recovery of fine-scale structure. We propose Second-Order Drifting Models, which lift drifting dynamics into phase space by augmenting generated samples with artificial velocity variables. We show that the resulting density perturbations obey accelerated second-order dynamics in Fourier space, connecting drifting models to the celebrated Nesterov acceleration from optimization theory. This provides a principled mechanism for mitigating the spectral stiffness of first-order drifting while preserving one-step inference. We derive a practical semi-implicit training algorithm and evaluate it on synthetic distribution matching, sequential data generation, and robotic control. Across these settings, the second-order drifting model improves convergence behavior and achieves competitive or superior performance over first-order drifting baselines.

</details>


### [210] [Adaptive Supervised Anchoring for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07935)
*Meilin Yang,Zixuan Ding,Jianhao Nie,Weite Zhang,Yuxin Zhang,Zhiming Shao,Li Yu,Zhe Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种新的统一训练框架，以解决 on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) 中因学生模型轨迹偏离目标而导致的监督信号退化问题。通过分离两种互补的监督路径——一种是基于实际访问状态的轨迹条件分布匹配，另一种是在标准真实上下文中使用监督交叉熵，避免在错误前缀上施加目标令牌。同时引入逐标记的轨迹-目标对齐机制来动态调整标准上下文锚点的强度，在冷启动阶段强化其作用，随着轨迹质量提升逐步放松。实验表明，该方法在多种模型规模、任务类型和通用推理基准上均优于传统 OPSD，提升了任务学习能力并保持了模型泛化性能，实现了更好的可塑性与稳定性平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD 依赖于学生模型采样的轨迹质量，当这些轨迹偏离目标时，教师模型在非目标前缀上的条件会削弱其任务相关监督信号，导致性能下降。现有方法未有效解决这一由轨迹偏差引发的信号退化问题。

Method: 提出一个统一训练框架，包含两个独立监督路径：1）保留轨迹条件分布匹配，指导学生在实际访问的状态中学习；2）在标准真实上下文中进行监督交叉熵，避免错误前缀带来的不兼容性。引入 token-level rollout-target alignment 来调节第二路径的权重，冷启动时强约束，随着轨迹质量提升逐渐放松。

Result: 在多个模型规模、任务类别和通用推理基准上的实验验证了该方法的有效性，相比 OPSD 显著提升任务获取能力，同时维持良好的通用能力，实现更优的可塑性-稳定性权衡。

Conclusion: 上下文质量是 on-policy self-distillation 中的核心瓶颈。将轨迹条件引导与标准监督分离，能够有效缓解信号退化问题，显著提升训练效果。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) adapts a language model by distilling guidance from a frozen teacher on trajectories sampled from the student. Its effectiveness, however, depends critically on the quality of those trajectories. We show that when student rollouts drift from target trajectories, conditioning the teacher on off-target prefixes substantially weakens its task-relevant supervision. Controlled prefix-corruption experiments expose this failure mode, which we term rollout-conditioned signal degradation. To address this problem, we propose a unified training framework that separates two complementary supervision pathways. The first retains rollout-conditioned distribution matching, providing guidance on states the student actually visits. The second applies supervised cross-entropy on canonical ground-truth contexts, avoiding the incompatibility of imposing target tokens on erroneous rollout prefixes. Token-level rollout-target alignment is used to adapt the strength of the canonical-context anchor, emphasizing it during cold start and relaxing it as rollout quality improves. Experiments across multiple model scales, two task families, and general-reasoning benchmarks show that the proposed approach improves task acquisition over OPSD while preserving general capabilities, resulting in a more favorable empirical plasticity-stability trade-off. These findings identify context quality as a central bottleneck in on-policy self-distillation and demonstrate the value of separating rollout-conditioned guidance from canonical supervision.

</details>


### [211] [Persistent Semantic Entities in Tool-Augmented LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07952)
*Zhaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究首次系统性地揭示了工具增强型大模型代理中存在的隐式持久状态（PSEs），即通过命名绑定、事件触发和跨边界传播实现的状态残留，其在24个不同规模与架构的模型中普遍存在（20%-100%的敏感性）。其中，命名绑定是必要且主导机制；若无命名绑定，则污染为零。偏好污染和指令污染在所有测试模型中均持续存在且不自愈，而角色注入类污染部分衰减，事实注入则因模型而异。此外，上下文隔离的自我验证可减少20%-79%的污染（中位数36.5%），但关键词检测易产生误报，且污染在四阶段代理流程中放大1.9倍。研究强调偏好与指令污染是部署中代理系统的关键安全威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有调试手段难以发现工具增强型大模型代理中的隐式状态残留，这些状态可能通过命名绑定、事件触发和跨边界传播长期存在并影响系统行为，构成潜在安全风险，亟需系统性分析与防御策略。

Method: 提出并形式化‘持久语义实体’（PSEs）概念，构建20模型敏感性测试面板，评估24个来自11个模型家族的LLM（参数量1.5B–1T），通过控制实验对比不同污染类型（偏好、指令、角色、事实）在多轮交互中的持久性与自纠正能力，并测试上下文隔离自验证与关键词检测的有效性。

Result: 所有测试模型均对PSEs敏感（20%-100%）；命名绑定是污染发生的必要条件；偏好与指令污染在所有模型中均不衰减（100%保留至t=10），角色注入部分衰减（90%→10%），事实注入则取决于模型：Llama-3.1-8B和GPT-4o-mini能自修正，而Qwen2.5-coder系列保持高污染水平；上下文隔离自验证可降低20%-79%污染（中位数36.5%），关键词检测有系统性误报；污染在四阶段代理链中放大1.9倍。

Conclusion: 偏好与指令污染具有持久性、缺乏自纠正能力且难以被标准监控捕获，构成部署中工具增强型大模型代理系统的核心攻击面，亟需设计更鲁棒的检测与防御机制。

Abstract: Tool-augmented LLM agents can harbor implicit state that persists across sessions, activates through events, and propagates across agent boundaries---largely invisible to standard debugging. We formalize this as Persistent Semantic Entities (PSEs): constructs defined by name binding, event triggering, and cross-boundary propagation, and evaluate them across 24 models from 11 families (1.5B--1T parameters). First, every tested model is susceptible (20--100% on the 20-model susceptibility panel), with name binding as the necessary and dominant mechanism: without it, contamination is 0%. Second, persistence depends on contamination type rather than scale or deployment: preference contamination persists undecayed on every model probed (100% at t=10) and instruction contamination persists wherever adopted, persona-style injection decays partially (90%$\to$10%), while factual injection is model-dependent---self-corrected on Llama-3.1-8B and GPT-4o-mini but held at ceiling on both Qwen2.5-coder variants, so we do not claim it self-corrects in general. The preference and instruction results hold across providers in our controlled setting. Third, context-isolated self-verification achieves 20--79% reduction (median 36.5%) without oracle references while keyword-based detection produces systematic false positives, and contamination compounds 1.9$\times$ along a four-stage agent pipeline (40%$\to$75%). Preference and instruction contamination---persistent, lacking self-correction, and poorly captured by standard monitoring---represent a particularly concerning attack surface for deployed agent systems.

</details>


### [212] [From Uncertainty to Failure Attribution: Self-Diagnosing Models for Failure Attribution under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07953)
*Yiyao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在分布偏移下的故障归因问题，引入自诊断模型，能够联合学习预测输出、预测不确定性以及故障归因信号。通过神经网络生成的故障归因向量，可区分四类失败类型：协方差偏移、语义偏移、噪声干扰和对抗扰动，实现从标量不确定性到故障识别的转变。为此，设计了一致性正则化来增强不确定性与故障归因预测的一致性，并构建了多个带有预定义分布偏移机制的基准数据集以评估模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅能检测分布外样本并估计不确定性，无法解释模型失败的原因。为提升模型鲁棒性与可解释性，需明确故障的根本原因，因此提出故障归因的新范式。

Method: 提出自诊断模型，结合预测输出、不确定性与故障归因向量；使用神经网络生成结构化的失败归因信号，区分四类失败类型；引入一致性正则化约束不确定性与故障归因的一致性；构建专门的分布偏移基准数据集用于评估。

Result: 所提方法能有效识别模型失败的具体原因，显著优于传统不确定性估计方法，在多个分布偏移场景下表现出更强的可解释性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究推动了机器学习模型从单纯检测分布偏移到理解其失败原因的转变，自诊断模型为提升模型可靠性与透明性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Distribution shift poses a significant challenge to the robustness of machine learning models, but the current solutions only aim to detect out-of-distribution (OOD) samples and predict uncertainty levels. We introduce a problem setting for failure attribution under distribution shift, which enables the models not only to detect OOD samples, but also to find out the reason for their failure. The solution we propose is called self-diagnosing models, which are capable of jointly learning predictive output, predictive uncertainty, and a failure attribution signal. In particular, we use the failure attribution vector, produced by a neural network, which provides a structured representation of predictive unreliability by distinguishing four different types of failures: covariance shift, semantic shift, noise corruption, and adversarial perturbation. In other words, we move from scalar uncertainty towards failure identification. For training the model, we introduce a consistency regularizer that encourages consistency between uncertainty and failure attribution predictions. Moreover, to be able to evaluate the model on its ability to find the reasons for failure, we construct several distribution shift benchmarks with predefined mechanisms for generating distribution shifts.

</details>


### [213] [EasyBalance: Cross-Layer Load Balancing in Distributed MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07964)
*Yize Wu,Ke Gao,Ling Li,Yanjun Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EasyBalance是一种无需修改专家-设备映射的跨层负载均衡策略，通过利用其他层专家的天然冗余性，联合执行跨层工作负载以缓解单层不平衡问题，从而显著减少GPU空闲时间，实验表明其能将GPU空闲时间减少40%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有负载均衡方法依赖于每层内的专家复制或迁移，带来额外开销且灵活性和可扩展性受限；而MoE模型中各层路由分布通常存在偏差，导致部分设备需等待最重负载的专家完成计算，造成效率低下。

Method: 提出EasyBalance策略，通过贪婪调度部分跨层工作负载在当前MoE步骤中执行，并将剩余任务延迟至未来平衡机会，利用跨层不平衡缓解机制实现高效负载均衡。

Result: 在多种模型、任务和配置下进行的大量实验表明，EasyBalance能够持续加速分布式MoE推理，平均减少超过40%的GPU空闲时间。

Conclusion: EasyBalance实现了无需修改专家-设备映射的即时适应性，几乎无额外开销，有效缓解了跨层负载不均问题，是高效且可扩展的MoE分布式推理解决方案。

Abstract: Load Balancing has emerged as a critical problem in expert-parallel distributed inference of Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. As routing distributions are typically skewed across experts, devices hosting lighter-loaded experts must idle to wait for the heaviest during expert computing, leading to inefficiency. Existing load-balancing approaches primarily rely on expert replication or migration within each layer, which introduce additional overhead and limit their flexibility and scalability. To address this problem, we propose EasyBalance, a cross-layer load balancing strategy that requires no modifications to the expert-device mapping, enabling instant adaptability and incurring essentially no additional overhead. Our key insights are that (1) experts of other layers can be viewed as naturally redundant for the current layer, and (2) cross-layer MoE workloads can be jointly executed to mitigate their individual imbalance. Based on these observations, EasyBalance greedily schedules a subset of cross-layer workloads to run at each MoE step and defers the remaining workloads for future balancing opportunities, effectively leveraging cross-layer imbalance mitigation. Extensive experiments across models, tasks, and configurations demonstrate that EasyBalance consistently accelerates distributed MoE inference, reducing GPU idling by mostly over 40%. Code is available at https://github.com/yize-wu/EasyInfra.

</details>


### [214] [ZeroLock: Concurrent Memory-Efficient LLM Training via Modular Update Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07974)
*Wentao Dai,Xuanran Li,Yuxiang Zhang,Ming Tang,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需反向传播（BP）的算法ZeroLock，用于边缘设备上的大语言模型微调。该方法通过局部目标构造将模型更新解耦为独立的块更新，打破了BP的更新锁定问题，从而在算法层面提升吞吐量并降低内存使用。作者首次建立了基于局部目标构造方法的理论框架，并证明ZeroLock具有$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{T})$的收敛速度，与BP仅相差多项式对数因子。系统实现中引入了早期转发和故障恢复等技术，原型实验表明，相比基于BP的基线，ZeroLock减少了26.5%的内存占用，并提升了4.9%的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边缘设备上大语言模型微调方法依赖反向传播训练，存在更新锁定、吞吐量低和内存瓶颈等问题。为解决这些限制，亟需一种无需反向传播且能高效利用资源的新算法。

Method: 提出零锁（ZeroLock）算法，通过局部目标构造将模型更新分解为独立块更新，打破反向传播中的更新锁定机制；构建理论框架，将局部目标映射到全局目标，确保优化一致性；设计系统实现，集成早期转发和容错恢复机制以提升效率与鲁棒性。

Result: 实验结果表明，与基于反向传播的基线相比，ZeroLock在真实原型上实现了26.5%的内存减少和4.9%的吞吐量提升，同时理论分析证明其收敛速率仅为反向传播的多项式对数差距。

Conclusion: ZeroLock是首个无需反向传播且支持通用模型分块的边缘微调算法，通过解耦更新机制显著改善资源受限环境下的性能，为隐私保护的边缘智能提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) fine-tuning at the edge adapts the model to scenario-specific data while preserving privacy. Although existing studies proposed pipeline parallelism to address the limited memory and computing resources of edge devices, they commonly rely on backpropagation (BP) training, which has a fundamental limitation of update locking and could experience severe throughput and memory bottlenecks. In this work, we propose a BP-free algorithm, called ZeroLock, that decouples the model updates into independent chunk updates by local objective construction. It breaks the update locking of BP and hence can improve throughput at the algorithm level and lower memory usage by reducing activation storage. To the best of our knowledge, we provide the first theoretical framework for such local objective construction-based approaches under general model chunk division by mapping local objectives to the global objective. We prove that ZeroLock has a convergence rate of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{T})$, which differs from BP only by polylogarithmic factors. We design a system for ZeroLock and build real-world prototypes, incorporating techniques such as early forwarding and failure recovery for efficient and robust implementation. Experiments on the prototype show that compared to BP-based baselines, ZeroLock reduces the memory by 26.5% and improves throughput by 4.9%.

</details>


### [215] [Evaluator Ensembles Under Reward Hacking: Covariance Geometry and Finite-Search Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08002)
*Fariya Afrin,Ibne Farabi Shihab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了语言模型评估器在规模化监督中的局限性，指出有限优化可能利用评估误差而非提升响应质量。通过评估器集合的协方差几何分析，发现校准后的评估器均值保留共模误差（沿全1方向），而不同评估者之间的分歧仅反映正交误差，因此分歧高不一定代表聚合稳健，分歧低也不意味着无系统性错误。论文证明了仅凭内部评分无法识别共模误差，并在联合次高斯模型下界定了最佳K选择的过估计和目标质量遗憾，扩展了对可预测自适应搜索的保证。所提出的搜索项与√log K 成比例，且在高斯投影误差下渐近紧致。此外，噪声质量代理引入人工秩一协方差但不改变分歧，为此提出有界双锚伯恩斯坦证书以控制有限搜索的误差与遗憾。通过120组 (J, rho, K) 的固定种子高斯压力测试及真实模型审计验证了理论，揭示了分歧诊断在搜索压力增加时的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型评估依赖于评估器（如人类偏好或自动评分模型）的集合来指导优化，但有限优化过程可能利用评估器的固有错误而非真正提升生成质量。现有方法常以评估者间分歧作为质量可靠性的指标，但这种分歧可能无法准确反映系统性误差的存在。因此需要更深入理解评估器集合的统计结构，尤其是其协方差特性，以避免优化误导。

Method: 采用协方差几何分析框架，建模评估器集合的输出为联合次高斯分布，区分共模误差（all-ones方向）与正交分歧。基于此，推导出最佳K选择的过估计上界与目标质量遗憾的边界，并引入条件校准假设下的自适应搜索保证。提出‘双锚伯恩斯坦证书’用于量化有限搜索中的误差与遗憾，结合模拟实验（高斯应力测试）与真实模型审计进行验证。

Result: 理论表明：评估者分歧不能可靠指示系统性错误；共模误差不可从内部评分中识别；最佳K选择的过估计和遗憾受√log K 量级控制，且在高斯误差下达到渐近最优；噪声代理引入虚假秩一协方差而不影响分歧，需使用新证书进行误差控制；实验验证了理论预测并揭示了分歧诊断在强搜索压力下的失效。

Conclusion: 评估器集合的协方差结构决定了优化可靠性。单纯依赖评估者分歧会误导优化方向，必须结合共模误差检测与有界误差证书。未来应设计能分离共模与正交误差的评估机制，以实现更鲁棒的自动化训练与评估。

Abstract: Language-model judges and reward models enable scalable supervision, but finite optimization can exploit evaluator errors rather than improve response quality. We characterize this failure through the covariance geometry of evaluator ensembles. For calibrated judges, the ensemble mean retains common-mode error along the all-ones direction, whereas cross-judge disagreement captures only orthogonal error. Consequently, disagreement can be high despite robust aggregation, or low while shared response-dependent errors persist. We prove that common-mode error is not identifiable from internal judge scores alone. Under a joint sub-Gaussian model, we bound best-of-K selection overstatement and target-quality regret, extending the guarantees to predictably adaptive search under conditional calibration. The resulting search terms scale as the square root of log K and are asymptotically tight for Gaussian projected errors. We further show that noisy quality proxies introduce artificial rank-one covariance without changing disagreement, and propose a bounded two-anchor Bernstein certificate for finite-search error and regret. Fixed-seed Gaussian stress tests over 120 (J, rho, K) configurations and real-model audits validate the theory while revealing the limits of disagreement-based diagnostics under increasing search pressure.

</details>


### [216] [Quality-Diversity Stress Tests for Process Reward Models:What Archive Coverage Can and Cannot Certify](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08008)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Fariya Afrin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将过程奖励模型（PRM）的应力测试建模为质量-多样性搜索问题，利用MAP-Elites方法在行为空间中保留最严重的正确性翻转编辑，分离搜索覆盖与利用覆盖。研究揭示了此类归档所保证的内容：有限单元修复边界、覆盖单元尾部风险和平均残余严重性，但无法仅通过覆盖比例界定最坏情况剩余单元。在利普希茨后修复损失和度量覆盖审计下，残差由归档拟合误差加上利普希茨常数乘以覆盖半径界定。受控景观验证了该证书及任何仅基于覆盖率的最坏情况保证的不可能性。在真实PRM上，搜索发现了Qwen2.5-Math-PRM-7B对填充操作的聚合依赖性漏洞：均值池化下存在44个严格攻击，最大增益达0.294，而最小读出下仅1个；匹配的语法控制实验隔离了机制，且RLHFlow值头模型表现出相同定性效应，最大增益0.005。预声明的成对LoRA修复协议将攻击率从0.148降至0.037至0.074，降低最坏攻击从0.333至0.177至0.212，提升排序AUROC且不损害最佳4样本准确率，收益归因于对抗微调而非归档多样性，并经独立非配对复制验证（44→1，干净分割最坏增益0.0092，MATH-500 41→0，干净排序40/40）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程奖励模型（PRMs）虽广泛用于搜索、排序和训练，但其优化可能通过增加奖励而将正确推理变为错误推理，形成安全漏洞。现有方法难以全面评估此类模型的鲁棒性，尤其缺乏对极端攻击情形的量化保障。因此亟需一种系统性方法来识别并缓解此类攻击，提升模型安全性。

Method: 将PRM应力测试建模为质量-多样性搜索问题，采用MAP-Elites算法在行为空间中生成并维护一个包含最严重正确性翻转编辑的归档。通过定义有限细胞修复边界和覆盖单元尾部风险等指标，结合利普希茨后修复损失与度量覆盖审计，推导残差的理论上限。进一步设计受控景观与真实模型实验验证理论结果，并提出成对LoRA修复协议进行防御。

Result: 在真实PRM（如Qwen2.5-Math-PRM-7B）中检测到显著的聚合依赖性漏洞，均值池化下可产生高达0.294的最大攻击增益。提出的成对LoRA修复协议有效降低攻击率（0.148→0.037/0.074），削弱最坏攻击（0.333→0.177/0.212），提升排序性能且不牺牲准确性。独立复现实验确认了结果的可靠性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了仅基于覆盖比例无法提供最坏情况下的安全保证，而结合归档拟合误差与覆盖半径的理论界限具有实际意义。所提出的成对LoRA修复协议在多个基准上均展现出显著防御效果，且其优势源于对抗微调而非多样性，具备良好的可扩展性和可重复性。

Abstract: Process reward models (PRMs) score intermediate reasoning steps and are widely used for search, ranking, and training, but optimization can exploit these learned proxies by increasing reward while turning correct reasoning into incorrect reasoning. We formulate PRM stress testing as a quality-diversity search problem using MAP-Elites, retaining the most severe correctness-flipping edit in each behavior-space region while separating search coverage from exploit coverage. We characterize what such archives certify: finite-cell repair bounds covered-cell tail risk and average residual severity but cannot bound the worst remaining cell from covered fraction alone; under Lipschitz post-repair loss and metric-cover auditing, the residual is bounded by archive fitting error plus the Lipschitz constant times the covering radius. A controlled landscape validates this certificate and the impossibility of any fraction-only worst-case guarantee. On real PRMs, the search reveals an aggregation-dependent vulnerability in Qwen2.5-Math-PRM-7B: padding yields 44 strict exploits with maximum gain 0.294 under mean pooling versus one exploit under minimum readout; a matched syntactic control isolates the mechanism, and an RLHFlow value-head model shows the same qualitative effect with maximum gain 0.005. A predeclared paired LoRA repair protocol reduces exploit rates from 0.148 to 0.037 to 0.074, lowers the worst attack from 0.333 to 0.177 to 0.212, improves ranking AUROC without degrading best-of-4 accuracy, attributes gains to adversarial fine-tuning rather than archive diversity, and is confirmed by independent unpaired replications (44 to 1, clean-split worst gain 0.0092, MATH-500 41 to 0, clean ranking 40/40).

</details>


### [217] [Ground-Truth Neighborhood Regularization for Reinforcement Learning Post-Training of Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08010)
*Jianqi Zhang,Xingyu Zhang,Zeen Song,Changwen Zheng,Fanjiang Xu,Wenwen Qiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为GTN-R的新型正则化方法，用于解决时间序列基础模型在强化学习后训练中出现的次优崩溃问题，通过以真实值为参考引导模型概率质量集中于真实值邻域，从而提高高质量轨迹采样概率并改善预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现强化学习后训练可能导致时间序列基础模型在某些预测区域输出分布偏离真实值，限制性能，这种现象称为次优崩溃，其主要原因是难以在真实值附近初始采样高质量轨迹。

Method: 提出地面真值邻域正则化（GTN-R），利用真实值作为参考来定位高质量区域，并引导模型的概率质量向真实值邻域集中，从而提升高质量轨迹的采样概率。

Result: 实验表明GTN-R能有效缓解次优崩溃问题，提升时间序列基础模型在下游任务中的预测性能，并可灵活集成到多种强化学习方法中。

Conclusion: GTN-R通过引导模型关注真实值邻域，显著提升了强化学习后训练下时间序列基础模型的泛化能力和预测精度，是一种高效且通用的改进方法。

Abstract: Time series forecasting (TSF) plays an important role in a wide range of real-world applications. Recently, time series foundation models (TSFMs), pretrained on large-scale datasets, have demonstrated strong generalization capabilities and emerged as an important paradigm for TSF. Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has consequently attracted growing attention as a means of further improving their performance on downstream tasks. However, we find that, in certain forecast regions, RL post-training may gradually shift the output distributions of TSFMs away from the ground truth, thereby limiting their performance. We refer to this phenomenon as \textbf{suboptimal collapse}. Our analysis suggests that difficulty in initially sampling high-quality trajectories near the ground truth is an important contributing factor to suboptimal collapse. To address this issue, we propose Ground-Truth Neighborhood Regularization (GTN-R) for RL post-training of TSFMs. GTN-R uses the ground truth as a reference for locating high-quality regions and guides the model's probability mass toward the ground-truth neighborhood. This increases the probability of sampling high-quality trajectories, mitigates suboptimal collapse, and improves performance. Moreover, GTN-R can be flexibly integrated into various RL methods for TSFMs. Extensive experiments show its effectiveness.

</details>


### [218] [Do All LLMs Know When They're Being Harmful? A Reproducibility Study of Latent-Space Safety Probes Across Model Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08029)
*Alizishaan Khatri,Dun Li Chan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究复现并扩展了Khatri等人（2026）的工作，验证了轻量级MLP探针在多个不同架构和规模的模型上检测有害提示的有效性，并发现最终激活层的隐向量在不同随机种子下保持一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究轻量级MLP探针在不同模型架构和规模下的泛化能力，以及推理过程中的非确定性对性能的影响。

Method: 在LLaMA-3.1-8B、Gemma-4-E4B、Mistral-7B-v0.3和Qwen2-7B等模型上，使用相同探针架构在最终层激活上训练并测试，采用三个基准（WildJailbreak、BeaverTails、AEGIS 2.0），并在五种随机种子下重复实验以评估方差。

Result: 复现结果与原始论文的F1分数相差不超过0.37个百分点（在BeaverTails上不超过0.2），其他模型的探针性能均在1点以内；不同种子下最终令牌的潜在向量保持不变。

Conclusion: 轻量级MLP探针具有良好的跨模型泛化能力，且其性能不受推理过程中随机种子变化的影响，表明最终层激活具有高度稳定性。

Abstract: Khatri et al. (2026) [DOI: 10.1109/DSN-W70714.2026.00027] show that lightweight MLP probes on final-layer activations of a single 8B model (LLaMA-3.1-8B) detect harmful prompts at F1 competitive with guard models 1000x larger, using one probe per benchmark. We reproduce this pipeline end-to-end and extend it along two axes the original study leaves open. First, we test whether the result generalizes across other model architecture and scale by training identical probes on activations from models like Gemma-4-E4B, Mistral-7B-v0.3, and Qwen2-7B, using the three benchmarks (WildJailbreak, BeaverTails, AEGIS 2.0). Second, we test how much of the reported performance is affected by non-determinism during inference by repeating extraction under five random seeds and measuring the variance of F1 scores. Our results reproduce the original LLaMA model benchmarks within 0.37 percentage points of the original F1 scores (and within 0.2 points on BeaverTails). We find that the original MLP probe architecture extends to other model families with F1 scores within a point of the values reported for LLaMA-3.1-8B. Our experiments varying seed values reveal an interesting observation: final token latent vectors remained the same for all tested architectures irrespective of the seed values used.

</details>


### [219] [CLAM: Causal Spatial Disaggregation to Infer Local Effects From Coarse Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08064)
*Gerrit Großmann,Sumantrak Mukherjee,Sebastian J. Vollmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLAM 是一种从粗粒度观测中估计局部因果效应的方法，通过利用调节这些效应的高分辨率上下文协变量，联合学习因果机制和去聚合映射，以捕捉独立处理时可能遗漏的交互作用。该方法支持局部效应估计、反事实推理和合理的结果去聚合，在多种场景下可靠地捕捉空间异质性因果效应，适用于公共卫生和环境政策等需在宏观尺度做决策但存在显著局部差异的应用领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从粗粒度数据中推断精细空间模式的因果效应具有挑战性，尤其是在高分辨率效应必须从聚合干预和结果中推断的情况下。现有方法往往忽略局部异质性和高分辨率上下文信息，导致对因果关系的误判。因此需要一种能结合高分辨率上下文信息、同时建模因果机制与去聚合过程的新方法。

Method: CLAM 采用联合学习框架，同时优化因果效应模型与从粗粒度到高分辨率的去聚合映射。它利用高分辨率协变量作为调节器，建模空间异质性因果效应，并通过可微分的去聚合模块实现结构化反事实推理与结果分解。

Result: CLAM 在多个合成与真实数据集上表现出色，能准确估计局部因果效应，生成合理反事实情景，并实现高质量的结果去聚合。实验表明其在捕捉空间变化的因果效应方面优于现有基线方法，尤其在存在强局部异质性的场景中优势明显。

Conclusion: CLAM 为从粗粒度观察中推断局部因果效应提供了一个强大且灵活的框架，能够有效整合高分辨率上下文信息，提升政策评估与干预设计的精确性，适用于公共健康、环境治理等依赖宏观决策但面临微观异质性的现实问题。

Abstract: Learning fine-grained spatial patterns from coarse-resolution data is challenging, especially in causal settings where high-resolution effects must be inferred from aggregated interventions and outcomes. We introduce CLAM, a method for estimating localized causal effects from coarse observations by exploiting high-resolution contextual covariates that modulate these effects. By jointly learning the causal mechanism and a disaggregation mapping, CLAM captures interactions that are missed when addressing these problems independently. The method supports localized effect estimation, counterfactual reasoning, and principled outcome disaggregation, and reliably captures spatially varying causal effects across diverse settings. This is particularly relevant for applications such as public health and environmental policy, where decisions are made at broad scales despite substantial local heterogeneity. Code is available at https://github.com/gerritgr/clam

</details>


### [220] [Adaptive Symmetry Discovery for Dynamical System Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08091)
*Behrooz Tahmasebi,Melanie Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在未知对称性群下的动力系统识别问题，提出一种自适应对称性发现方法，可从单条轨迹中高效识别系统参数。当对称性已知时，所需轨迹长度显著缩短；在对称性未知时，通过学习对称性并融入识别过程，达到与已知对称性相同的最优性能。分析基于群表示论和凯莱图的扩展性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学领域中，动力系统常受物理定律约束，表现出特定对称性（如旋转、平移等），但这些对称性往往未知。传统方法依赖长轨迹或假设对称性已知，限制了实际应用。因此需要一种能自动发现对称性并从单条短轨迹中准确识别系统的有效方法。

Method: 结合群表示论与凯莱图的扩展性质，设计一种自适应对称性发现算法，通过学习轨迹中的潜在对称结构，将对称性嵌入系统识别框架，实现高效参数估计。

Result: 在对称性已知情况下，可显著缩短所需轨迹长度；在对称性未知时，所提方法能有效学习对称性，并实现与已知对称性相当的最佳识别性能，验证了其优越性和实用性。

Conclusion: 本工作为动力系统识别提供了新的理论框架，揭示了对称性在数据效率上的关键作用，且所提方法在仅凭单条轨迹下即可实现高精度识别，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Dynamical systems model trajectory data generated by fixed underlying dynamics, with applications ranging from biology to physics. Especially in scientific settings, dynamical systems are not generic but often exhibit symmetries imposed by physical laws, formalized through equivariance with respect to group actions. The identification problem concerns recovering the parameters of a system from observed trajectories. In this work, we study adaptive symmetry discovery for dynamical system identification and address how a system can be identified from a single trajectory when it is equivariant with respect to an unknown symmetry group. To this end, we first show that for known symmetries, the system can be identified from a significantly shorter single trajectory than in the generic setting, and we precisely characterize this improvement. We then consider the automatic symmetry discovery setting, proposing a method to learn the symmetry group directly from a single trajectory and incorporate it into the identification procedure, achieving the same optimal trajectory length as in the known-symmetry case. Our analysis relies on tools from group representation theory and the expander properties of Cayley graphs, and may be of independent interest for the study of symmetries in dynamical systems.

</details>


### [221] [Support Selection Beyond Smooth DAG Exactness: Completion Geometry,Score Margins, and Selective Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08103)
*Rui Wu,Zongyuan Chen,Hong Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了平滑无环性约束与结构学习之间的关系，揭示了在 DAG 边界处最小环补全生成的平方自由单项式理想性质，并推导出孤立环的选择时间精确表达式。当 Ψ'(h) ∝ h^ν 时，可行性时间 T₀(ε) = Θ(ε^-(2ν+1))；当 ν > 0 时，得分裕度改变主导动态尺度，而 ν = 0 时存在对数边界层。实验验证了该规律，且无需真实数据的分离统计量能有效预测选择时间。对于有限样本，父集置信族与强制反向查询可认证骨架和未屏蔽碰撞器标签，所有认证结果均与种群最优解一致，但部分与生成图不一致。研究区分了 DAG 可行性、基于得分的支持选择与因果识别三者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分析仅针对特定约束公式揭示退化现象，未能揭示平滑精确性本身所蕴含的普遍性质。本文旨在理解在 DAG 边界附近结构学习行为的本质，尤其是最小环补全与残差响应阶数的关系，以及选择时间的精确动态。

Method: 通过代数几何方法分析最小环补全生成的平方自由单项式理想，结合泰勒展开的受限喷射分析残差阶数；利用渐近分析推导可行性时间与得分裕度的影响；设计父集置信族与强制反向查询进行有限样本下的结构认证；通过大规模实验验证理论预测。

Result: 最小环补全生成的平方自由单项式理想包含所有受限泰勒喷射；当最小补全有 q 条边时，向量残差响应阶数为 q，非负标量为 2q；指数级多的恒定尺度循环流形表现出相同的排名缺失；孤立环选择时间满足 T₀(ε) = Θ(ε^-(2ν+1))；ν=0 时需对数边界层调整；实验验证理论，分离统计量预测性能良好；320 次运行中所有认证标签均与最优解一致，但部分与生成图不符。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了平滑精确性下结构学习的深层机制，将 DAG 可行性、支持选择与因果识别明确区分开来，为理解算法行为提供了理论基础，并展示了在有限样本下可靠结构认证的可能性。

Abstract: Smooth acyclicity constraints answer whether a weighted support is a DAG, whereas structure learning asks which support change should be made. Existing analyses establish degeneracy for particular constraint formulas but do not isolate what follows from smooth exactness itself. At a DAG boundary, we show that minimal cycle completions generate a squarefree monomial ideal containing every restricted Taylor jet of an exact representation. If the smallest completion has $q$ edges, the first possible response has order $q$ for a vector residual and $2q$ for a nonnegative scalar. Exponentially many constant-scale cyclic manifolds exhibit the same lack of ranking away from the boundary for NOTEARS and DAGMA. We derive the exact selection time for an isolated cycle. When $Ψ'(h)\asymp h^ν$, the feasibility-only time is $T_0(\varepsilon)=Θ(\varepsilon^{-(2ν+1)})$; a score margin changes the leading dynamics at scale $T_0^{-1}$ for $ν>0$, while $ν=0$ has a logarithmic boundary layer requiring $γT_0\log(1/\varepsilon)\to0$. Experiments verify this law, and a truth-free separation statistic predicts selection time on 320 official NOTEARS/DAGMA trajectories (Spearman $-0.52$ and $-0.66$, permutation $p<10^{-4}$). For finite samples, a parent-set confidence family and forced-opposite queries certify skeleton and unshielded-collider labels shared by every population optimum of a frozen score. Across 320 runs, every regret bound covers an independent oracle-score audit. None of 3,042 certified skeleton or 2,396 collider labels disagrees with the oracle-score optimum, although 4.4% and 5.5%, respectively, disagree with the generating graph. These results separate DAG feasibility, score-based support selection, and causal identification.

</details>


### [222] [TSDS-Toolbox: A Toolbox for Measuring Time-Series Dataset Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08119)
*Yen-Ku Liu,Hongjie Chen,Ryan A. Rossi,Franck Dernoncourt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的时序数据集相似性工具箱（TSDS-Toolbox），旨在解决现有时序数据集相似性评估方法碎片化、难以扩展的问题。该工具箱支持系统化、可复现的对比实验，具备灵活扩展能力，可集成自定义数据集、相似性方法及下游任务，并统一评估数据集级与序列级的相似性方法。通过多场景实验验证了其有效性，工具已公开可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序数据集相似性评估方法存在碎片化、难以扩展的问题，限制了模型选择与微调的效率与可复现性，亟需一个统一、可扩展的评估框架。

Method: 提出TSDS-Toolbox，集成多种数据集相似性方法，支持自定义扩展，结合时序数据集压缩器实现数据集级与序列级相似性的统一评估。

Result: 在多种实验设置下，TSDS-Toolbox表现出良好的系统性、可扩展性和评估一致性，有效支持时序分析任务中的数据集选择与模型优化。

Conclusion: TSDS-Toolbox为时序数据集相似性研究提供了统一、可扩展且可复现的评估平台，有助于推动基础模型在时间序列领域的应用与发展。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of artificial intelligence (AI) has significantly accelerated research in time-series analysis, particularly in forecasting, classification, and generation tasks. Recent models, especially foundation models, benefit from time-series dataset similarity due to its significant role in source dataset selection for fine-tuning. However, many existing implementations for benchmarking time-series dataset similarity methods are fragmented and difficult to extend. To address this, we present a unified framework, the Time-Series Dataset Similarity Toolbox (TSDS-Toolbox). Our work enables (1) systematic and reproducible comparisons of time-series dataset similarity methods; (2) flexible extensibility for users to add customized datasets, similarity methods, and downstream time-series tasks; and (3) consistent evaluation of both dataset-level and series-level similarity methods through integrated time-series dataset reducers. The effectiveness of TSDS-Toolbox is validated through comprehensive experiments under diverse experimental settings. Our toolbox is publicly available.

</details>


### [223] [Accurate Ensembles, Fragile Narratives: Multi-Scale Stacking and a Fidelity Audit of LLM-Generated Explanations for Credit Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08126)
*Gregorius Reynaldi Pratama,Kuo-Kun Tseng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个端到端的信用评分系统，结合多尺度堆叠集成模型与神经元元学习器，在公开数据集上达到ROC-AUC 0.9539和PR-AUC 0.9137，优于单一模型。然而，尽管预测性能提升显著，其带来的实际操作收益极小（仅减少6个漏判违约，成本损失降低不足2%）。更关键的是，基于LLM生成解释的叙事层表现不佳：模型在审计案例中错误识别风险因素、遗漏主要驱动变量并引入未输入特征。分析表明，尽管SHAP与LIME在重要性排序上一致（重叠@10=0.80），但排序顺序差异大（tau=0.43），且关键特征的归因符号在不同申请人间近乎随机（模态符号占比0.53）。此外，模型校准（ECS=0.117）与扰动稳定性（DPD=0.078）均不达标。因此，仅靠提示工程无法解决解释可信性问题，必须在生成后验证其依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前信用评分模型日益复杂，决策逻辑难以从参数解读，与监管要求的可解释性存在矛盾。为弥合这一差距，常提议使用语言模型生成解释：通过计算特征归因，交由大模型撰写理由。本研究旨在评估该方法中‘生成解释’部分是否真正有效，尤其是其可靠性与准确性。

Method: 构建端到端系统：预测模块采用多尺度堆叠集成，融合四种正则化梯度提升树与残差网络，由神经元元学习器在留一交叉验证预测结果上训练；解释模块将特征归因输入大语言模型，生成自然语言解释。通过统计检验、审计案例分析及归因一致性、稳定性、校准性等指标评估模型性能与解释可信度。

Result: 预测模型在32,581条申请数据上取得测试ROC-AUC 0.9539（95% CI [0.9462, 0.9616]）与PR-AUC 0.9137，较最优单模型提升Delta-AUC 0.0143（p=0.016）。但实际应用中，仅减少6个漏判违约，成本加权损失下降不足2%。解释生成模块出现严重偏差：错误识别风险因子、遗漏主导变量、引入未提供特征。归因分析显示SHAP与LIME虽在重要性上一致（重叠@10=0.80），但排序差异大（tau=0.43），关键特征归因符号接近随机（模态符号占比0.53），校准与扰动稳定性均不达标（ECS=0.117，DPD=0.078）。

Conclusion: 即使预测性能优异，基于大语言模型的解释生成仍不可靠。归因本身存在不确定性，且解释生成过程易引入错误信息。提示工程不足以确保解释质量，必须在生成后进行严格验证。未来工作需加强归因的稳定性与可解释性，而非依赖生成式模型自动“合理化”复杂模型输出。

Abstract: Credit scoring increasingly relies on models whose decision logic cannot be read off their parameters, in tension with supervisory expectations that adverse decisions be explainable. A common proposal closes that gap with a language model: compute feature attributions, hand them to an LLM, and let it write the rationale. We build such a system end to end and test whether the second half of the promise holds. The predictive component is a multi-scale stacking ensemble fusing four differently regularised gradient-boosting learners with a residual network through a neural meta-learner trained on out-of-fold predictions. On a public 32,581-application credit dataset it reaches test ROC-AUC 0.9539 (95% CI [0.9462, 0.9616]) and PR-AUC 0.9137, beating the best single model by Delta-AUC = 0.0143 (p = 0.016 under a conservative independence assumption). Our central finding is asymmetric. The ranking gain is real but operationally small: at the F1-optimal threshold the ensemble avoids only six additional missed defaults out of 1,422 against a tuned random forest, cutting cost-weighted loss by under 2%. The narrative layer fails in a way prompt engineering alone does not fix. In an audited case the model named three factors as risk-increasing that the supplied attributions scored as risk-reducing, omitted the dominant driver, and introduced a feature never given to it. We trace this to properties we measure rather than assume: SHAP and LIME agree on which features matter (overlap@10 = 0.80) but not on their order (tau = 0.43, p = 0.18), and the attribution sign for the model's most sensitive input is near a coin flip across applicants (modal-sign share 0.53). Calibration (ECS = 0.117) and perturbation stability (DPD = 0.078) both fall short of our own thresholds. Constrained prompting is necessary but not sufficient: grounding must be verified after generation, not assumed.

</details>


### [224] [DoGMA: A Central-Dogma-Guided Foundation Model for Multi-Omics Alignment and Multi-Task Learning in Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08148)
*Junfei Ling,Bangzheng Pu,Bingsen Xue,Tianle Li,Ruying Hu,Cheng Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DoGMA，一种基于中心法则的泛癌多组学分析基础模型，通过引入方向性注意力机制和层级掩码重建预训练，增强跨癌症类型、不同下游任务及不完整组学数据下的迁移能力。实验表明，该模型在癌症表征学习、生存预测和转移预测等任务中表现优异，性能提升源于方向性注意力与重建预训练的协同作用，验证了领域特定归纳偏置对多组学基础模型鲁棒性和可迁移性的促进作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型中的注意力机制通常允许无限制的双向交互，但生命的基本逻辑是方向性的（如中心法则）。忽略这种方向性可能限制模型在异质癌症间、不同任务及不完整组学设置下的迁移能力。因此，需要引入符合生物学规律的归纳偏置以提升模型性能与通用性。

Method: 构建基于Transformer-MoE架构的DoGMA模型，设计定向注意力机制，使组学间的交互遵循中心法则的信息流向（如DNA→RNA→蛋白质）；采用层级掩码多组学重建进行预训练，引导模型学习符合中心法则的跨组学关联。

Result: DoGMA在癌症表征学习、生存预测和转移预测等多个下游任务中均表现出色，且消融实验表明其性能优势来自方向性注意力与重建预训练的协同效应，有效促进了生物上更一致的跨组学信息交换。

Conclusion: 本研究证明，引入领域特定的归纳偏置（如中心法则导向的方向性注意力）能够显著提升多组学基础模型的鲁棒性与可迁移性，为注意力机制在多组学表示学习中的设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms have been widely utilized in modern deep learning, and many existing multi-omics models inherit their conventional use to allow unrestricted bidirectional interactions. However, the fundamental logic of life is directional. Existing designs often overlook the directionality suggested by the central dogma, potentially limiting transfer across heterogeneous cancers, downstream tasks, and incomplete modality settings.In this work, we present DoGMA, a central-dogma-guided foundation model for pan-cancer multi-omics analysis, arguing that robust transfer requires representations with domain-specific inductive bias. Concretely, we build it on a Transformer-MoE architecture where directed attention biases inter-omics communication toward central-dogma information flow. We further pretrain our model with masked hierarchical omics reconstruction to guide it toward learning central-dogma-consistent interactions. Across diverse downstream tasks, including cancer representation learning, survival prediction, and metastasis prediction, DoGMA consistently demonstrates strong predictive performance. Ablations and analyses further suggest that the performance gains arise from the synergy between central-dogma-guided directed attention and reconstruction-based pretraining, which together promote more biologically consistent cross-omics information exchange. Overall, DoGMA demonstrates that domain-specific inductive biases can improve the robustness and transferability of multi-omics foundation models, offering new insights into the design of attention mechanisms for multi-omics representation learning.

</details>


### [225] [A Hybrid Nested Harness for Decoupling Structure and Parameters in LLM-Driven Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08156)
*Víctor Gallego*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合嵌套搜索框架，将语言模型（LLM）用于结构草图生成，由数值优化器负责调参，从而提升效率与性能。该框架在多个科学领域中表现优于纯语言模型或纯数值优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于语言模型的进化算法在同时处理结构和连续参数时效率低下，因语言模型在连续参数调整上存在浪费token的问题。

Method: 采用外层由语言模型生成结构草图（含数值空缺），内层由数值优化器（如CMA-ES、梯度方法、MCMC等）填充并优化这些空缺，实现结构与参数的协同优化。

Result: 在元优化器、代码策略设计及贝叶斯近似推断任务中，混合嵌套搜索均显著优于单一语言模型或纯数值优化方法。

Conclusion: 混合嵌套搜索框架有效结合了语言模型的结构创新能力与数值优化器的高效调参能力，为复杂优化问题提供了一种通用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: In evolutionary algorithms powered by language models, the LLM acts as a single operator that simultaneously updates structural components (like control flow) and continuous parameters. While LLMs can be good at the first, they are not efficient at the second, wasting tokens taking discrete jumps inside a trial and error loop. We resolve this by formalizing a hybrid nested search, in which an outer loop has the LLM propose a structural sketch, with numeric gaps, and an inner numerical optimizer tunes the sketch. Both the outer and inner solvers are pluggable: any text-based optimizer can be combined with a zero-order optimizer (CMA-ES), gradient-based routines, or MCMC samplers. We validate our framework across three scientific domains: (i) meta-optimizers on closed-form test functions, (ii) code-based policies for systems research and social dilemmas; and (iii) approximate Bayesian inference tasks. Across all three, the hybrid optimizer is superior to both vanilla LLM-driven search and pure numerical optimization baselines. Code at: https://github.com/vicgalle/hybrid-nested-search

</details>


### [226] [Predicting blood clot growth from sparse post-onset measurements with latent neural differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08165)
*Lennon J. Shikhman,Ying Qian,He Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种基于潜在神经微分方程的计算框架，利用稀疏测量数据推断血液凝固模型中的未知参数并预测血栓发展。通过对比七种概率方法，发现随机神经常微分方程（SNODE）在参数推断和未来血栓生长轨迹预测中表现最佳，且随着观测数据增多，预测精度提高，但预测时间越长，不确定性越大。该框架为个性化血栓建模提供了有力支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有血液凝固计算模型虽有助于理解血栓形成，但临床应用受限于难以测量的模型输入及患者特异性数据稀疏的问题。因此需要一种能够从稀疏数据中推断未知参数并预测血栓进展的方法。

Method: 采用基于潜在神经微分方程的计算框架，结合四类已知生化输入（纤维蛋白原及因子IX、VIII、V）与早期血栓尺寸的稀疏观测，推断组织因子参数，并预测后续血栓增长。比较了七种概率方法：SNODE、SNFDE、潜变量神经过程基线、单调概率深度集成、经验轨迹检索、PCA-岭高斯后验和Gompertz曲线检索。

Result: SNODE在参数推断和血栓生长轨迹预测方面表现最优；SNFDE表现相近且显著优于非微分方程模型。预测准确性随观测数量增加而提升，但预测时长远则导致不确定性上升、精度下降。

Conclusion: 潜在神经微分方程能有效结合参数推断与血栓生长预测，从稀疏测量中实现精准建模，为个性化血栓模拟提供可行基础。

Abstract: Computational models of blood clotting improve understanding of thrombus formation, but their clinical application remains limited because many model inputs are difficult to measure and patient-specific data are often sparse. We present a computational framework based on latent neural differential equations that infers unknown model parameters from sparse measurements and forecasts thrombosis progression. We demonstrate the framework using data generated from a multiphysics blood-clotting model in which clot growth is governed by the coagulation cascade and diffusion. Four known biochemical inputs (fibrinogen and factors IX, VIII, and V), together with sparse early clot-size observations, are used to infer the tissue-factor parameter and predict subsequent clot growth. We compare seven probabilistic methods: stochastic neural ordinary differential equations (SNODE), stochastic neural functional differential equations (SNFDE), a latent neural-process baseline, a monotone probabilistic deep ensemble, empirical trajectory retrieval, PCA-ridge Gaussian posterior, and Gompertz-curve retrieval. SNODE achieved the best performance in inferring the unknown input and forecasting future clot-growth trajectories. SNFDE performed similarly and consistently outperformed the other non-differential models. Prediction accuracy improved as more observations became available, whereas longer forecasting horizons increased uncertainty and decreased accuracy. Latent neural differential equations thus effectively combine parameter inference and clot-growth forecasting from sparse measurements, providing a promising foundation for personalized thrombosis modeling.

</details>


### [227] [Biologically Informed Representation Learning for Robust Cross-Center Generalization of MALDI-TOF Mass Spectrometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08182)
*Alejandro L. García-Navarro,Carlos Sevilla-Salcedo,Belén Rodríguez-Sánchez,Vanessa Gómez-Verdejo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DALMA 是一种概率表示学习框架，通过联合建模获取特定变异和生物监督信息，以学习具有生物学结构的潜在表示。该方法结合了领域特定重建与生物引导的表示学习，从而在无需机构特异性组件的情况下实现跨异构临床中心的零样本部署。在涵盖七组来自三个国家的数据集的多中心基准测试中，DALMA 在两个保留的临床中心上持续实现了最先进的零样本微生物识别性能，并且学习到的表示也有效迁移至抗微生物药物耐药性预测。此外，潜在空间的新颖性估计实现了在未见过的领域偏移下的可靠选择性预测。结果表明，基于生物学信息的表示学习为临床微生物学中的鲁棒且可迁移机器学习提供了一种有效策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习模型在基于MALDI-TOF质谱的临床微生物学任务中虽具潜力，但因领域偏移问题（如采集特定变异性导致模型捕捉技术伪影而非可转移的生物学信息）而难以在不同机构间部署。现有表示学习方法多依赖统计域对齐，忽视了微生物数据集中天然存在的生物监督信息。因此，亟需一种能同时考虑技术变异与生物学信息的方法，以实现真正可迁移的模型。

Method: 提出 DALMA 框架，采用概率表示学习机制，联合建模采集特定变异与生物监督信号。通过领域特定重建损失和生物引导的表示学习目标，学习出具有生物学结构的潜在表示，使模型在推理阶段无需额外机构特定组件即可实现零样本部署。

Result: 在包含七个数据集的多中心基准测试中，DALMA 在两个保留的临床中心上均实现最优的零样本微生物识别性能；其学习的表示在抗微生物药物耐药性预测任务中也表现出良好迁移能力；潜空间新颖性估计能够有效检测未知领域偏移并支持选择性预测，显著提升实际应用中的可靠性。

Conclusion: 基于生物学信息的表示学习是解决临床微生物学中模型可迁移性和鲁棒性挑战的有效途径。DALMA 通过整合技术变异建模与生物监督，实现了无需机构定制化组件的零样本部署，为跨中心临床应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Machine learning models for MALDI-TOF mass spectrometry have shown considerable promise for clinical microbiology tasks such as microbial identification and antimicrobial resistance prediction. However, their deployment across institutions remains limited by domain shift, as acquisition-specific variability often leads models to capture technical artifacts rather than transferable biological information. Existing representation learning approaches primarily address this problem through statistical domain alignment while largely overlooking the biological supervision naturally available in microbiology datasets. We introduce DALMA, a probabilistic representation learning framework that jointly models acquisition-specific variability and biological supervision to learn biologically structured latent representations. By combining domain-specific reconstruction with biologically guided representation learning, DALMA learns transferable representations that generalize across heterogeneous clinical centers without requiring institution-specific components at inference, enabling zero-shot deployment on previously unseen sites. We evaluate DALMA on a multi-center benchmark comprising seven datasets from three countries. DALMA consistently achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot microbial identification across two held-out clinical centers, while the learned representations also transfer effectively to antimicrobial resistance prediction. Furthermore, latent-space novelty estimation enables reliable selective prediction under previously unseen domain shifts. These results demonstrate that biologically informed representation learning provides an effective strategy for robust and transferable ML in clinical microbiology.

</details>


### [228] [Beyond Aggregate Calibration: Decomposing Income-Conditional Recall Disparities in Automated Credit Default Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08202)
*Sai Srikar Boddupalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在大规模消费贷款数据集（LendingClub，N=1,344,936）上发现，基于模型置信度的数据清洗流程存在显著的种族收入不对称性：高收入违约者被错误标记为标签噪声的比例远高于低收入违约者（Cramer's V约为0.03-0.07）。从公平性视角看，高收入与低收入借款人的真正阳性率（召回率）差距高达16.86个百分点。通过逐步特征屏蔽方法识别出三个导致偏差的机制：(1) 直接依赖申请人自报收入；(2) 算法吸收了前期机构定价中隐含的偏见；(3) 即使去除收入和利率后仍存在残余偏差（交叉验证中3.55个百分点，测试集2.56个百分点，Z = -4.04，p < 0.0001），该偏差由结构性代理变量（如贷款金额、房产所有权）维持。结果表明，仅屏蔽敏感属性无法保障公平性，因制度定价与行为代理变量会重构被剔除的信号。研究强调了在受监管金融机构中审计数据驱动型AI流程的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据清洗流程依赖模型置信度筛选噪声样本，但可能无意中加剧了对特定群体的不公平对待。尤其在金融信贷领域，这种偏差可能影响弱势群体的贷款机会，因此亟需系统评估并缓解此类偏见。

Method: 采用序列式特征屏蔽法，逐步移除收入、利率等敏感变量，结合交叉验证与独立测试集分析偏差来源；利用SHAP值量化各特征对预测结果的影响方向与程度，识别结构性代理变量的作用。

Result: 发现高收入违约者被误标为噪声的概率显著更高，且存在16.86个百分点的召回率差距；即使屏蔽收入与利率，残余偏差仍显著存在，主要由贷款金额和房产所有权等结构性代理变量驱动。

Conclusion: 单纯屏蔽敏感属性不足以实现算法公平，因为制度性定价策略和行为代理变量会重建被掩盖的歧视信号。在金融等高度监管领域，必须对数据驱动的AI流程进行系统性审计，以防范隐蔽性偏见。

Abstract: Data-centric curation pipelines frequently rely on model confidence scores to flag and filter noisy or mislabeled training instances. Evaluating this filtering convention on a large-scale consumer lending sample (LendingClub, N = 1,344,936) uncovers an underlying demographic asymmetry: high-income defaulters are disproportionately classified as label noise relative to low-income defaulters (Cramer's V approximately 0.03-0.07). Re-examining this behavior through the lens of equal opportunity [Hardt et al., 2016] reveals a far more severe discrepancy: a 16.86 percentage point gap in true positive rate (recall) between high- and low-income borrowers who ultimately defaulted. Implementing a sequential feature-blinding methodology allows us to isolate the drivers of this disparity across three distinct mechanisms: (1) direct reliance on self-reported applicant income; (2) algorithmic absorption of upstream institutional bias encoded within origination interest rates; and (3) a residual disparity (3.55 percentage points in cross-validation; 2.56 percentage points on a held-out test partition, Z = -4.04, p < 0.0001) that remains even after purging both income and interest rates from the model. Out-of-sample signed SHAP valuations demonstrate that this residual gap is maintained by structural proxies, most notably loan amount and home ownership status. These empirical findings show that simply blinding an algorithm to sensitive attributes fails to ensure fairness when institutional pricing decisions and behavioral proxy variables collectively reconstruct the omitted signals. We outline the practical implications of these findings for auditing data-centric AI workflows within regulated financial institutions.

</details>


### [229] [FreSH: Frequency-Segmented Hierarchical Multi-Expert Framework for Multivariate Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08207)
*Pingping Liu,Muyao Wang,Zijian Zhang,Tongshun Zhang,Hao Miao,Guorui Xie,Qingliang Li,Qiuzhan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FreSH 是一种用于多变量时间序列分类的频率分段层次化多专家框架，通过自适应的多尺度分析和动态融合策略，在保持计算效率的同时提升了模型对复杂时序模式的捕捉能力。它在30个UEA基准数据集和真实振动数据上均表现出优于现有方法的分类精度，且显著减小了模型规模并提高了效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTSC方法难以在细粒度表征学习、类别不平衡和实际约束之间取得平衡，尤其在处理多尺度时序信号时存在性能瓶颈。

Method: FreSH采用频率分段与层次化多专家结构，结合局部专业化与全局上下文建模，引入自适应融合机制和更稳健的优化目标，实现对多尺度时序特征的互补建模与高效学习。

Result: 在30个UEA数据集和真实振动数据上的实验表明，FreSH在分类准确率上持续超越现有最优方法，并大幅降低模型大小与计算开销。

Conclusion: FreSH为多变量时间序列分类提供了一种高效且强大的新范式，兼具强表征能力与良好的实用性，适用于复杂现实场景。

Abstract: Multivariate Time Series Classification (MTSC) demands models that can effectively capture complex temporal patterns across multiple scales while remaining computationally efficient. However, existing approaches generally struggle to reconcile fine-grained representation learning, especially under class imbalance and real-world constraints. In this paper, we present FreSH, a Frequency-Segmented Hierarchical Multi-Expert Framework designed to address these challenges. FreSH introduces a new perspective for MTSC by enabling adaptive, multi-scale analysis of temporal signals, allowing different aspects of the data to be modeled in a complementary and coordinated manner. By combining localized specialization with holistic context modeling, FreSH achieves strong representational capacity without incurring excessive computational overhead. An adaptive fusion strategy further enhances flexibility, enabling the model to dynamically emphasize the most informative components of the input. In addition, we incorporate a more robust optimization objective that improves learning stability across varying sample difficulties and class distributions. Extensive evaluations on 30 UEA benchmark datasets and real-world vibration data demonstrate that FreSH consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in classification accuracy, while substantially reducing model size and efficiency.

</details>


### [230] [Control-Diverse Reinforcement Fine-Tuning: Decoupling the Shared Control Bottleneck of RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08224)
*Binwen Tan,Jingchao Wang,Dengzhe Hou,Lingyu Jiang,Zeyuan Wu,Yunhan Shen,Fangzhou Lin,Kazunori Yamada,Atsushi Koike*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出通过后训练强化学习提升大模型复杂推理能力，但传统基准仅反映性能提升，无法揭示内部机制变化。作者挑战以电路激活为中心的解释，提出将激活与控制分离，并引入后训练控制系数（Post-training Control Coefficient）量化各组件对奖励增益的控制力，构建控制矩阵与激活强度矩阵。发现高度共享的激活可与任务特异的控制共存，而激活与控制集中度差异体现‘激活-控制差距’，该差距过小表明控制坍缩至共享方向，丧失任务特异性。为缓解此问题，提出控制多样性强化微调（CD-RFT），设计一阶近似正则化项以兼容Flash Attention，显著提升多任务能力，在数学、代码和逻辑任务上优于匹配的GRPO方法；无KL项变体在pass@1表现最优，含KL惩罚变体在大k覆盖率上领先，且效果可迁移至Llama-3.2-3B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习微调的成功归因于更强更多样化的电路激活，但缺乏对内部机制的深入理解，尤其无法判断激活是否真正控制奖励提升。因此需要一种能区分激活与控制的新框架，揭示模型内部如何分配有限能力应对多任务，从而优化训练策略。

Method: 采用代谢控制分析思想，定义后训练控制系数，构建控制矩阵与激活矩阵，识别跨任务控制集中现象（共享控制瓶颈）及激活与控制集中度差异（激活-控制差距）。基于此设计控制多样性正则化项，推导适用于Flash Attention的一阶近似梯度，实现控制解耦的强化微调（CD-RFT）。

Result: CD-RFT在Qwen2.5-7B上实现最大控制解耦，多任务能力超越匹配的GRPO；无KL变体在pass@1指标领先，KL惩罚变体在大k pass@k覆盖上表现更优，且结果可迁移至Llama-3.2-3B，验证了共享控制瓶颈作为诊断工具与训练正则化的双重价值。

Conclusion: 激活与控制应被分离看待，控制解耦是提升多任务能力的关键。共享控制瓶颈既是可解释性诊断工具，也可作为有效训练正则化机制，推动大模型在复杂推理任务中的能力扩展。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning post-training unlocks complex reasoning in LLMs. Yet benchmark scores reveal only whether a model improved, not what changed inside it, nor how it splits finite capability across tasks. A representative interpretability line attributes the success of RL fine-tuning to stronger and more diverse circuit activation. We challenge this activation-centered account by separating activation from control: an activated circuit need not control the post-training reward gain. Adapting Metabolic Control Analysis, we define the Post-training Control Coefficient to measure component control over reward gain and arrange these coefficients by task family into a control matrix, paired with an activation-magnitude matrix. We call cross-task control concentration the Shared Control Bottleneck and the difference between activation and control concentration the Activation-Control Gap. This reveals that highly shared activations can coexist with task-specific control, while a small gap indicates that control has collapsed onto a shared direction and lost task specificity. To reduce this collapse, we regularize the post-training loss with the Shared Control Bottleneck and propose Control-Diverse Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (CD-RFT). The exact regularizer gradient requires second-order automatic differentiation incompatible with flash attention, so we derive a first-order proxy with worst-case overhead below eight percent. On Qwen2.5-7B, CD-RFT achieves the largest control decoupling and improves multi-task capability over matched GRPO across mathematics, code, and logic. The no-KL variant leads on pass@1, and the KL-penalized variant leads on large-k pass@k coverage that KL otherwise degrades. Together, these results show that the Shared Control Bottleneck is both a mechanistic diagnostic and a training regularizer, and that control decoupling and capability gains transfer to Llama-3.2-3B.

</details>


### [231] [SAGE: SLO-Aware Adaptive Retrieval for Production RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08237)
*Muhammad Faizan Raza,Shuo,Yang,Satish Mahadevan Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAGE提出一种学习型的SLO感知自适应检索策略，动态调整每查询的段落数k，通过轻量级特征（如得分分布、排名差距、词汇信号）实现低开销推理。在Natural Questions数据集上，相比静态基线（k=20），SAGE在5秒P95延迟约束下实现95% SLO合规，延迟降低36%，检索成本减少51%，仅损失2个百分点的EM指标。该策略在HotpotQA、UnSeenTimeQA及四种LLM（Llama、Qwen、Mistral、Gemma）上均表现良好，通用性强，显著提升SLO表现且不损害答案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统在生产环境中面临尾延迟和成本控制的严格SLO要求，但传统固定预算的检索方式忽略查询难度，导致对简单查询过度检索、对复杂查询检索不足，难以兼顾答案质量和SLO合规性。因此需要一种能根据查询难度动态调整检索数量的自适应策略。

Method: SAGE采用基于模仿学习的离线训练方法，利用初始检索结果中的轻量级特征（如得分分布、排名间隙、词汇信号）构建决策模型，学习一个近似最优延迟-质量权衡的‘专家’策略；推理阶段无需调用大模型，仅需少量计算即可确定每个查询的最佳检索数量k。

Result: 在Natural Questions上，SAGE在5秒P95延迟约束下达到95% SLO合规率（静态基线仅30%），延迟降低36%，检索成本下降51%，仅损失2个百分点的EM指标；且单一策略可在多个数据集和多种大模型间泛化，带来45-52点的SLO提升，无质量退化。

Conclusion: SAGE是一种高效、可扩展的自适应检索策略，能够在不增加推理开销的前提下显著提升RAG系统的SLO合规性与资源效率，具备良好的跨数据集和模型泛化能力，适用于生产环境部署。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems in production operate under strict service level objectives (SLOs) on tail latency and infrastructure cost. However, standard retrieval pipelines rely on fixed retrieval budgets that ignore query difficulty, over-retrieving for easy queries and under-serving hard ones, forcing operators to trade answer quality against SLO compliance. This paper proposes SAGE, a learned SLO-aware adaptive retrieval policy that dynamically selects the number of passages k per query. SAGE uses lightweight features derived from initial retrieval (e.g., score distributions, rank gaps, lexical signals) and is trained offline via imitation learning from an oracle that approximates optimal latency-quality trade-offs. At inference, it adds no LLM calls and minimal overhead. On Natural Questions, under a 5s P95 latency SLO, SAGE achieves 95% SLO compliance versus 30% for the best static baseline (k=20), reduces P95 latency by 36% and retrieval cost by 51% with only 2 percentage points Exact Match (EM) loss. A single policy trained on Natural Questions generalizes across HotpotQA, UnSeenTimeQA, and four LLM families (Llama, Qwen, Mistral, Gemma), consistently yielding +45-52 point SLO improvements without quality degradation.

</details>


### [232] [The Replay Gap: Static Evaluation of Model Switching in LLM Agents Scores the Wrong World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08239)
*Ashritha Gonuguntla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过分支滚动实验（branching rollouts）检验了大语言模型路由器在多步代理中的评估假设，发现传统基于回放的评估方法严重高估了模型切换的效果。实际切换后行为显著偏离原轨迹，且多数情况下早期即发生分歧，导致回放评估无法准确反映真实性能。研究还揭示了不同配置下的确定性差异及预算限制对强模型表现的影响，指出当前评估方式存在系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法假设模型替换不会影响后续轨迹，但实际中模型切换会引发显著行为分歧，导致回放评估结果不可靠。需要更真实的评估机制来衡量代理路由的实际效果。

Method: 采用分支滚动实验，在SWE-bench代理轨迹中可控点进行分叉，分别使用不同模型继续执行，并与同模型控制组对比；同时分析不同配置和预算下行为差异，验证回放评估的准确性。

Result: 模型切换后90%以上动作被重写，早期分歧率高达74-77%，而控制组仅6-35%；所有结果翻转均出现在切换组；回放评估对成功相关判断全部错误，生成补丁与真实情况相似度极低（0.00-0.11）。

Conclusion: 基于回放的评估方法无法准确反映真实世界中代理路由的表现，尤其在多步任务中存在严重偏差。必须改进评估范式，引入真实分支实验以确保公正评价。

Abstract: LLM routers promise efficiency by matching each request to the cheapest adequate model, and are increasingly applied per step inside multi-step agents. Yet agentic routers are evaluated like single-turn routers: by replaying logged trajectories and substituting another model's recorded outputs, assuming the rest of the trajectory is unaffected. We test this assumption with branching rollouts: we fork live SWE-bench agent trajectories at controlled points, rebuild the environment, continue each fork with a different model, and compare against same-model control forks that isolate sampling and replay noise. Across six paired runs (~900 rollouts), swaps exceed their matched control floors by +0.25 to +0.66 normalized edit distance (multiplicity-corrected CIs exclude zero), rewriting 61-94% of post-fork actions; 74-77% of early swaps diverge at the first post-fork action, versus 6-35% of controls, leaving only 3% of replayed states valid. Divergence decreases with fork depth in both directions. All five outcome flips we observe occur in swap arms, upgrades rescuing unsolved instances and a downgrade losing the sole solve, and zero occur across 359 control forks. Scoring these same swaps with a log-stitching replay evaluator, replay mispredicts every success-relevant outcome call and predicts patches with 0.00-0.11 similarity to reality. Auditing the noise floor, temperature-0 "determinism" is configuration-dependent: FP8-served controls diverge on over 90% of forks while AWQ-served ones remain near-identical; and under tight budgets the stronger model more often exhausts its steps without submitting.
